We further design Instance-aware Expert Fusion (IEF), which adaptively combines semantically complementary experts via on-device routing and performs efficient logit-level fusion using cached expert-specific text features. Extensive experiments on 11 heterogeneous benchmarks show that, under the same privacy constraints, FedSEPT achieves a better trade-off between local adaptation and global generalization than strong baselines.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Identity Anchoring: Closed-Loop Keyframe Placement for Synthetic Paired Supervision in Video Face Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21434)
*Logan Robbins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应身份锚定（AIA）和现实参考纹理恢复（RTR），通过可变的身份锚点集和基于反馈的自动锚点放置策略，结合真实帧的微纹理迁移与频谱验证，解决视频换脸中的身份漂移和过度平滑（美颜滤镜效应）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频换脸方法（如DreamID-V的SyncID-Pipe）仅在第一帧和最后一帧进行身份替换，中间帧仅依赖姿态序列生成，导致长片段、遮挡和大姿态下生成的身份缺乏约束、容易漂移。同时，微纹理缺失导致过度平滑的美颜滤镜效果。目前缺乏对锚点数量和位置影响的消融研究。

Method: (i) 将生成器泛化为支持任意锚点集，利用扩散强制变换器架构，将帧条件化视为将对应token置为零噪声；(ii) 通过闭环反馈循环，对每帧与真实参考身份评分，在最差帧插入图像换脸锚点，直至通过阈值或预算耗尽；(iii) 将循环判定结果作为自动数据过滤。纹理恢复方面：从真实帧非面部区域提取纹理进行匹配再颗粒化，频带分离迁移亚身份微纹理，并使用真实频谱作为第二验收通道。

Result: 提出了可验证的实验方案：漂移-间隙曲线、相同预算下均匀vs自适应放置对比、AIA数据的学生训练、纹理消融和人类美颜滤镜研究。

Conclusion: 身份锚点密度是一个可控的质量调节旋钮，所提出的自适应锚点放置策略和纹理恢复方法有望系统性地解决视频换脸中的身份漂移和纹理退化问题。

Abstract: Video face swapping has no natural paired supervision: no real footage exists of one person's face performing another person's video. The strongest current answer, DreamID-V's SyncID-Pipe, mints pairs by replacing the identity in exactly two frames of a real clip -- the first and the last -- and regenerating the rest from a pose sequence alone. Pose carries no appearance evidence of the swapped-in identity, so over long clips, occlusions, and extreme pose excursions the synthesized identity has a long unanchored span on which to drift; no published ablation examines anchor count or placement. We propose Adaptive Identity Anchoring (AIA): (i) generalize the synthesizer to arbitrary anchor sets, architecturally natural for diffusion-forcing-style transformers where conditioning on a frame is clamping its tokens to zero noise; (ii) place anchors by a closed feedback loop that scores every generated frame against the real reference identity and inserts an image-face-swapped anchor at the worst-scoring frame until the pair passes a threshold or exhausts a budget; (iii) reuse the loop's verdict as an automatic data filter. A second pathology, the beauty-filter look of over-smoothed skin, has the same root cause: micro-texture, like identity, is priced by none of the pipeline's objectives. We therefore pair AIA with Reality-Referenced Texture Restoration: matched re-graining from each real frame's non-face regions, band-split transfer of sub-identity micro-texture from the real footage, and a second, spectral acceptance channel refereed by the footage's own spectrum. Identity-anchor density, we argue, is a controllable quality dial, and we specify falsifiable experiments -- drift-versus-gap curves, uniform-versus-adaptive placement at matched budgets, student training on AIA-minted data, and texture ablations with a human beauty-filter study -- that would validate or refute the proposal.

</details>


### [74] [DAPM: UAV Monocular Depth Estimation from Any Height, Pitch, Roll and FOV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21438)
*Tong Ling,Wenhui Diao,Yingchao Feng,Hanbo Bi,Zhongyan Hou,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPM，首个针对无人机航拍图像、能在连续变化视角下联合估计相机位姿与深度的单目框架，通过理想地面深度(IGD)模块和渐进量化区间(PQB)模块实现SOTA性能，并发布了UAPD数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计方法难以泛化到无人机在高度、俯仰、横滚、视场角等持续变化的动态相机位姿场景，无法应对航拍视角的多样性和深度分布的大尺度范围。

Method: 1) 通过理论分析建立视角与视距之间的几何对应关系；2) 提出理想地面深度(IGD)模块，利用该几何关系实现密集相机位姿监督并增强深度特征；3) 提出渐进量化区间(PQB)模块，通过渐进式监督和层次化量化区间实现鲁棒估计。

Result: 在UAPD数据集上，DAPM在深度估计和相机位姿估计两个指标上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DAPM作为首个面向无人机航拍图像的联合位姿-深度估计框架，有效解决了动态视角下的泛化问题，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation is a fundamental prerequisite for 3D reconstruction and autonomous navigation in Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). In practical deployments, UAVs operate under highly dynamic camera poses characterized by continuous variations in height, pitch, roll, and field of view (FOV). Existing monocular depth estimation methods frequently fail to generalize across such diverse perspectives and the expansive scale of depth distributions inherent in aerial scenes. To address these challenges, we establish a quantitative representation of UAV viewing angles through rigorous theoretical analysis, deriving the geometric correspondence between viewing angles and view distances using the ground plane as a reference for observation. Building upon this, we propose Depth Estimation for Any Perspectives Model (DAPM), representing the first monocular framework specifically designed for UAV aerial imagery to jointly estimate camera pose and depth under continuously varying viewpoints. Specifically, we introduce an Ideal Ground Depth (IGD) module that leverages the derived geometric relationships between UAV perspectives and view distances to implement dense camera-pose supervision and enhance depth features. And we further develop a coarse-to-fine Progressive Quantization Bins (PQB) module. By incorporating progressive supervision and hierarchical quantization bins, the PQB module enables robust estimation in complex UAV aerial imagery. To evaluate the proposed framework, we present the UAV Any Perspectives Depth (UAPD) dataset, featuring comprehensive and continuous distributions of pose parameters. Experimental results on UAPD demonstrate that DAPM achieves state-of-the-art performance across both depth and camera-pose estimation metrics. The source code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/ThisIsLT/DAPM.

</details>


### [75] [GrainGS: Gradient-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21448)
*Jiahao He,Yihua Shao,Zhengkai Zhao,Pan Gao,Fei Ma,Jingcai Guo,Hao Tang,Nicu Sebe,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GrainGS提出了一种结合层次化锚点结构和逐高斯变形场的动态场景重建框架，通过静态预热、停止梯度操作和规范-残差外观分解，在合成和真实多视图基准上实现了高质量重建、实时渲染（435.6 FPS）和紧凑存储（4.67 MB），PSNR达36.98 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3D高斯泼溅方法中，逐基元方法提供灵活局部变形但导致冗余基元增长，锚点方法提升空间规律性但抑制局部变化。GrainGS旨在平衡精细运动建模、结构稳定性和紧凑表示，解决上述权衡问题。

Method: 1) 静态预热阶段，从所有时刻观测中建立时不变的规范表示；2) 联合训练中，使用停止梯度操作阻断变形路径到规范位置的梯度，同时保留通过重建目标的直接精炼；3) 每个高斯独立预测位置、旋转和尺度的时变偏移量，在结构化锚点约束下实现局部运动；4) 规范-残差外观分解建模帧依赖的光度变化，避免将其强制归因于几何变形。

Result: 在合成单目和真实多视图基准上达到PSNR 36.98 dB，渲染速度435.6 FPS，存储仅4.67 MB，实现了高质量重建、实时新视角合成和紧凑存储。

Conclusion: GrainGS通过层次化锚点与逐高斯变形的协同设计，有效解决了动态场景重建中运动建模与结构稳定性之间的平衡问题，验证了规范-残差外观分解对去除几何-光度歧义性的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting requires a balance between fine-grained motion modeling, structural stability, and compact representation. Existing per-primitive methods provide flexible local deformation but often suffer from redundant primitive growth, while anchor-based methods improve spatial regularity at the cost of suppressing locally varying motion. To address these issues, we present GrainGS, a dynamic Gaussian framework that combines a hierarchical anchor scaffold with per-Gaussian deformation. A static warm-up stage first establishes a time-invariant canonical representation from observations across all timestamps. During joint training, a stop-gradient operation blocks the deformation-mediated gradient pathway to the canonical positions while preserving their direct refinement through the reconstruction objective. Each Gaussian then predicts independent temporal offsets for position, rotation, and scale, enabling detailed local motion within a structurally constrained scaffold. A canonical-residual appearance decomposition further models frame-dependent photometric changes without forcing them into geometric deformation. Experiments on synthetic monocular and real-world multiview benchmarks show that GrainGS achieves high reconstruction quality, real-time novel view synthesis, and compact storage. Under the synthetic benchmark setting, it reaches an average peak signal-to-noise ratio of 36.98 decibels, renders at 435.6 frames per second, and requires 4.67 megabytes of storage.

</details>


### [76] [SPDCN: Strip-based Deformable Convolutional Network for Steel Surface Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21456)
*Zhongming Liu,Bingbing Jiang,Guangxin Wan,Xiang Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对钢表面缺陷分割中各向异性缺陷处理难题，提出SPDCN网络，包含模糊增强多尺度上下文模块（FMCM）和自适应方向感知可变形卷积（ADADC），在NEU-Seg上以3.54M参数达到89.60% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法因标准卷积的各向同性感受野和刚性采样网格无法适应不规则缺陷边界，尤其难以处理裂纹、划痕等细长各向异性缺陷。

Method: 1) FMCM：采用分组多分支卷积结合直觉模糊通道注意力机制，自适应捕获多尺度上下文信息；2) ADADC：用解耦的水平与垂直条带卷积替代传统偏移预测器，使可变形采样网格沿细长缺陷主方向各向异性对齐。

Result: 在公开钢表面缺陷数据集上超越SOTA，NEU-Seg上达到89.60% mIoU，参数量仅3.54M。

Conclusion: 提出的SPDCN有效解决了钢表面缺陷分割中的各向异性缺陷问题，在精度和效率上均具有优势。

Abstract: Steel surface defect segmentation is critical for industrial quality inspection, yet existing methods struggle with elongated, anisotropic defects such as cracks and scratches due to the isotropic receptive fields of standard convolutions and rigid sampling grids that cannot adapt to irregular defect boundaries. To address these limitations, we propose Strip-based Predictor for Deformable Convolutional Networks (SPDCN) with two key innovations. The \textbf{Fuzzy-enhanced Multi-scale Context Module (FMCM)} employs group-wise multi-branch convolutions with an intuitionistic fuzzy channel attention mechanism to adaptively capture multi-scale contextual information across varying defect sizes. The \textbf{Adaptive Direction-Aware Deformable Convolution (ADADC)} replaces the conventional offset predictor with decoupled horizontal and vertical strip convolutions, enabling the deformable sampling grid to anisotropically align with the principal orientation of elongated defects. Extensive experiments on public steel surface defect benchmarks demonstrate that SPDCN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving 89.60\% mIoU on NEU-Seg with only 3.54M parameters. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/DWlzm .

</details>


### [77] [CLUIE: Clustering-Aware Recurrent Propagation with Local Structural Compensation for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21467)
*Kui Jiang,Zefan Feng,Laibin Chang,Yan Luo,Junjun Jiang,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对水下图像增强中退化异质性问题，本文提出CRWKV框架，通过聚类感知语义动态重排序（CSDR）将固定递归传播路径改造为内容自适应的token轨迹，并结合暗响应调制局部传播（DMLP）补偿局部结构连续性，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像因波长依赖的光吸收、散射和背向散射导致颜色失真、对比度降低和细节丢失，且退化模式随场景深度和成像条件变化，同一图像不同区域需区域自适应恢复。现有视觉RWKV模型虽提供线性复杂度的长程依赖建模，但其预定义扫描顺序与内容无关，无法适应空间上非均匀的恢复需求。

Method: 提出CRWKV框架：(1) 聚类感知语义动态重排序（CSDR）——根据语义特征相似性对token分组，从簇间上下文关系中推导动态遍历顺序，使WKV状态沿语义相关区域累积；(2) 暗响应调制局部传播（DMLP）——通过深度卷积提取局部结构响应，利用邻域感知的伪暗响应图自适应调制传播强度，补偿动态重排序可能破坏的局部连续性。

Result: 在多个水下图像增强基准上，CRWKV取得了最先进的定量性能和优越的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过内容自适应的递归传播路径替代固定扫描顺序，CRWKV有效解决了水下图像中异质退化区域的差异化恢复问题，验证了语义驱动的token轨迹在视觉RWKV模型中的有效性。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement remains challenging due to wavelength-dependent light absorption, scattering, and backscattering, which jointly cause color distortion, contrast degradation, and detail loss. Since these degradations vary with scene depth and imaging conditions, different regions within the same image often exhibit heterogeneous degradation patterns and thus require region-adaptive restoration. Although visual RWKV models offer an efficient linear-complexity solution for long-range dependency modeling, their predefined scanning orders are content-agnostic and therefore fail to adapt recurrent state propagation to spatially non-uniform restoration demands. To address this limitation, we propose a Clustering-aware RWKV framework, termed CRWKV, which reformulates the fixed recurrent propagation path of conventional RWKV into a content-adaptive token trajectory. Specifically, we introduce Clustering-aware Semantic Dynamic Reordering (CSDR), which groups tokens according to semantic feature similarity and derives a dynamic traversal order from inter-cluster contextual relations. This design enables WKV states to be accumulated along semantically correlated regions rather than fixed spatial or spectral orders. Since dynamic reordering may disrupt the local continuity of original spatial neighborhoods, we further propose Dark-response Modulated Local Propagation (DMLP), which extracts local structural responses via depth-wise convolution and adaptively modulates their propagation strength using a neighborhood-aware pseudo-dark response map. In this way, local structural cues are compensated before recurrent aggregation while preserving content-adaptive long-range modeling. Extensive experiments on multiple underwater image enhancement benchmarks demonstrate that CRWKV achieves state-of-the-art quantitative performance and superior visual quality.

</details>


### [78] [Future Rendering $\neq$ Future Surface: A Benchmark and Dataset for Dynamic Surface Reconstruction Beyond the Observed Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21471)
*Yukun Shi,Minglun Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureSurf基准，用于评估动态场景重建方法在未来时间点上的表面几何预测能力。在8个可控运动数据上的实验显示，现有方法（DG-Mesh、Deformable-3DGS）在可预测未来上存在2.7-6.6×的误差差距，且未来渲染质量与表面几何精度统计上解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态场景重建评估仅在观测时间窗口内进行，无法衡量模型在部署场景（AR叠加、机器人交互、预期规划）中预测未来表面几何的能力，且缺乏标准基准。

Method: 构建8个解析定义的可控运动数据集（含3个证伪控制），提供精确逐帧真值网格。训练时使用序列前75%作为观测，在后25%未来时间上以Chamfer距离评估表面几何，同时报告未来/观测差距作为诊断指标。提供真值侧可恢复性预言机区分可预测与不可预测未来。

Result: DG-Mesh在可恢复未来上存在2.7-4.1×误差差距，Deformable-3DGS为2.0-6.6×；证伪控制行为符合设计（表面不变运动无差距）；未来渲染质量与表面精度统计解耦；未来误差集中在表面运动区域。

Conclusion: FutureSurf揭示了现有动态重建方法在未来几何预测上的显著缺陷，证明当前依赖新视角合成指标无法反映未来几何精度，为未来时序表面重建评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Dynamic-scene reconstruction is almost always evaluated inside the observed time window, yet deployment settings such as AR overlays, robot interaction, and anticipatory planning need the future surface: the geometry at times beyond those captured. No standard benchmark measures this. We introduce FutureSurf, a controlled diagnostic benchmark and dataset for future-time surface reconstruction that trades scene diversity for exact future ground truth and falsification controls. A method trains on the observed first 75% of a sequence; we score its extracted per-frame surface on the held-out future by Chamfer distance, reporting absolute future CD as the primary score and the future/observed gap as a diagnostic. The dataset contains eight analytically defined controlled motions, including three falsification controls, with exact per-frame ground-truth meshes. We also provide a ground-truth-side recoverability oracle. The release includes split files, scoring code, a benchmark card, and Croissant metadata. On the controlled motions, the DG-Mesh backbone leaves a 2.7-4.1$\times$ gap even for futures predictable in principle (four of five recoverable from observed motion by a fixed rule), while the falsification controls behave as designed (the surface-invariant motion shows no gap). Beyond the contributed dataset, the gap persists across six animated DG-Mesh asset scenes and a second backbone, Deformable-3DGS (2.0-6.6$\times$; both share a deformation-MLP temporal model). The benchmark also shows that future rendering quality and future-surface accuracy are statistically decoupled, so the novel-view-synthesis metrics the field reports do not track future geometry. The future error is structured, concentrating where the surface moves. The dataset, evaluation toolkit, and scoring code are available on Hugging Face and GitHub (https://github.com/Ricky-S/futuresurf).

</details>


### [79] [Recurrent Sinusoidal INRs for Efficient High-Fidelity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21485)
*Hyunmin Cho,Jaejun Yoo,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正弦循环的隐式神经表示（INR）迭代机制，通过共享正弦块循环细化潜在表征，实现谐波谱富集。实验表明该方法在图像和3D表示任务上优于前馈基线，参数更少、优化步数更少，并在超分辨率、NeRF和SDF任务中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正弦循环作为隐式神经表示中谐波谱富集的迭代机制，揭示正弦激活如何诱导谐波线谱，以及循环展开如何有效扩展谱支持域。

Method: 提出共享正弦块结构，通过循环迭代方式逐步细化潜在表征；与标准前馈INR、非正弦循环变体及平衡态正弦模型进行对比验证。

Result: 在RGB图像基准上，该方法以更少参数和更少优化步数达到比前馈基线更高的保真度；在超分辨率、NeRF和SDF任务上也表现出良好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 正弦循环机制能有效提升INR的谱表达能力，在多种视觉表示任务中兼具效率与性能优势。

Abstract: We study sinusoidal recurrence as an iterative mechanism for harmonic spectral enrichment in implicit neural representations (INRs). Our analysis reveals that sinusoidal activations induce a harmonic line spectrum, providing a spectral account of how recurrent unrolling enriches the effective spectral support. We realize this principle with a shared sinusoidal block that iteratively refines the latent representation. We empirically validate the resulting spectral behavior against feed-forward INRs, non-sinusoidal recurrent variants, and equilibrium-style sinusoidal models. Complementing this analysis, we evaluate the proposed architecture across image and 3D representation tasks. On RGB image benchmarks, our method achieves higher fidelity than feed-forward baselines with fewer parameters and fewer optimization steps, and it further transfers favorably to super-resolution, NeRF, and SDF tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Texture++: Elevating 3D Asset Texture Resolution with a Region-Aware Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21504)
*Shuaiwei Wang,Shi Li,Jieting Xu,Yuchi Huo,Qi Wang,Wenting Zheng,Rengan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Texture++ 是一个针对3D资产纹理的超分辨率框架，通过在多个渲染视图上执行超分并融合结果，利用自适应视图选择、四叉树纹理区域组织和扩散模型，显著提升低分辨率纹理的细节和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量3D资产因纹理分辨率低被废弃，而现有超分辨率模型忽略纹理图专注于自然图像，需要一种高效且泛化的纹理超分辨率模型来复兴影视和游戏等行业中大量老旧但有价值的资产。

Method: 将UV空间的超分任务重构为在多个渲染视图上执行超分并融合输出。具体包括：(1) 自适应视图选择策略整合UV纹理块中分散的纹理；(2) 基于四叉树的纹理区域组织方法组合不同视角的超分结果并提供掩码；(3) 基于扩散的超分模型增强指定掩码区域的纹理分辨率并与周围区域无缝集成。

Result: 通过全面评估，该方法生成的纹理在细节和连贯性上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Texture++ 能有效提升低分辨率3D资产纹理质量，为老旧资产的再利用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Numerous 3D assets are discarded due to low texture resolution, while current super-resolution models ignore texture maps and focus on natural images. An efficient and generalizable texture super-resolution model can revitalize a large corpus of aging yet valuable assets across industries such as film and video games. We present Texture++, a novel framework for texture super-resolution, which enhances the low-resolution textures of assets to produce high-resolution, high-quality results. Specifically, we reformulate the task of super-resolution in UV space into performing it across multiple rendered views and merging the outputs. Firstly, to achieve more complete and continuous textures in the view space, we propose an adaptive view selection strategy to integrate textures dispersed across UV texture patches. Furthermore, we introduce a quadtree-based texture region organization method for combining super-resolved textures from different viewpoints, providing masks to distinguish regions that require improvement. Finally, we design a diffusion-based super-resolution model that enhances the texture resolution for specified masked regions, seamlessly integrating with surrounding regions. Through comprehensive evaluations, we demonstrate that our approach yields textures with substantially improved detail and coherence over existing methods.

</details>


### [81] [Boosting Robustness for All-Weather Self-Supervised Depth Estimation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21526)
*Mengshi Qi,Xiaoyang Bi,Xianlin Zhang,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多教师蒸馏和鲁棒雷达融合的自监督深度估计方法，通过不确定性感知的多教师蒸馏和POV-BEV雷达融合，解决了恶劣天气下自监督深度估计的感知退化问题，在全天候数据集上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气条件下，传感器感知退化导致自监督深度估计面临两大挑战：一是恶劣条件扭曲像素对应关系并违反自监督损失函数的假设；二是雷达点云在POV视角下分布稀疏，难以实现有效的自监督融合。

Method: 提出不确定性感知多教师蒸馏（UAMTD）方法，利用不同恶劣条件输入生成多样化教师模型，并通过不确定性建模加权知识蒸馏损失；设计POV-BEV雷达融合方法，利用相机像素射线约束建立POV与BEV视角间的关联，以利用更密集的雷达点云。

Result: 在多个全天候数据集上的定量和定性实验表明，该方法具有鲁棒性，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的自训练框架通过多教师蒸馏和鲁棒雷达融合，有效解决了恶劣天气下自监督深度估计的难题，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Self-supervised depth estimation is challenging for safe autonomous driving under various adverse weather conditions due to sensor perception degradation. These challenges arise from two main aspects. Firstly, adverse conditions can distort pixel correspondences and violate the assumptions embedded in the self-supervised loss function, leading to erroneous depth predictions. Secondly, while radar is a widely adopted sensor in adverse weather conditions, the sparse distribution of radar points in the Point of View (POV) poses challenges for self-supervised fusion. To address these issues, we introduce a novel self-training pipeline using unpaired real all-weather data through multi-teacher distillation and robust radar fusion. We propose the Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Teacher Distillation method to generate diverse teacher models with different adverse condition inputs, and then employ uncertainty modeling to weigh the knowledge distillation loss. Additionally, we design the POV-BEV Radar Fusion approach, which leverages camera-pixel ray constraints to establish connections between the camera's Point of View (POV) and the radar's Bird's-Eye View (BEV). This approach enables the utilization of denser radar points, effectively capturing the complementary perspectives of both POV and BEV. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate the robustness of our proposed method on all-weather datasets, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/RobustDepth.

</details>


### [82] [ElasticTTT: Prior-Preserving Test-Time Tuning for Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21529)
*Yueyi Liu,Chi Zhang,Sen Cui,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ElasticTTT框架，解决扩散模型在视频编辑中测试时微调导致的先验坍塌问题，通过目标分布正则化、对比CFG和异步噪声调度三大策略，在保留生成先验的同时实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准测试时微调(TTT)与生成模型的分布匹配特性存在根本性不匹配，导致先验坍塌——模型丢弃文本条件和空间潜变量，生成退化为源视频或区域特征纠缠。

Method: 提出ElasticTTT框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 目标分布正则化防止尖锐记忆极小值；2) 对比CFG引导推理远离源偏差；3) 异步噪声调度保护未编辑区域。

Result: 成功保留基础模型的生成先验，在单次视频编辑任务上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: ElasticTTT通过理论分析和大量实验验证，有效解决了先验坍塌问题，为扩散模型在视频编辑中的TTT应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Test-Time Tuning (TTT) on pretrained diffusion models has emerged as a powerful paradigm for video editing. However, there exists a foundational mismatch between the distribution-mapping nature of generative models and the single-point optimization of standard TTT. In this paper, we demonstrate that this mismatch triggers \textit{Prior Collapse}, a degenerate state where the model discards the text conditions and spatial latents, collapsing generations to the source video, or entangling the features of distinct regions. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{ElasticTTT}, a novel framework that preserves the prior generative distribution and rescues generative elasticity. Specifically, we propose \textit{Target Distribution Regularization} to prevent sharp memorization minima, \textit{Contrastive CFG} to guide inference away from source biases, and \textit{Asynchronous Noise Schedule} to preserve unedited regions. Extensive evaluations, supported by theoretical analysis, demonstrate that ElasticTTT successfully preserves the generative prior of the base model, achieving state-of-the-art performance on one-shot video editing.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Robust Iris Recognition Through Occlusion Identification and Conditional Diffusion-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21545)
*Kamrul Hasan,Mylene C. Q. Farias,Oleg V. Komogortsev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种遮挡感知的虹膜识别框架，包含遮挡类型识别、扩散模型重建和深度学习识别三个模块，通过识别遮挡类型并重建被遮挡区域来提升识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虹膜识别性能常因眼睑、睫毛、反射光等遮挡导致纹理信息丢失而下降。现有方法直接在退化样本上识别或仅依赖可见区域，在大量纹理被破坏时效果不佳。

Method: 框架包含三个顺序模块：(1) 基于残差2D CNN的遮挡类型分类器；(2) 以遮挡图像、二值掩码和预测遮挡类型为条件的去噪扩散概率模型用于区域重建；(3) 基于VGG19-HPMNet（VGG19+水平金字塔映射）进行全局和局部特征提取与识别。

Result: 在CASIA-Iris-Thousand数据集上的受控合成遮挡实验表明，该框架通过识别遮挡类型、重建掩码区域并重新评估恢复的虹膜样本，有效提升了虹膜识别性能。

Conclusion: 遮挡感知的虹膜识别框架通过遮挡类型识别和扩散模型重建相结合，能够有效恢复被遮挡的虹膜纹理，提升识别准确率。

Abstract: Iris recognition is a reliable biometric approach that identifies individuals using the distinctive and stable texture of the iris. However, recognition performance can degrade when discriminative iris texture is partially occluded by eyelids, eyelashes, specular reflections, or other acquisition artifacts. Existing approaches often perform recognition directly on degraded samples or rely only on the remaining visible iris region, which may be inadequate when substantial texture is corrupted. To address this limitation, we propose an occlusion-aware iris recognition framework with three sequential modules: occlusion-type identification, diffusion-based reconstruction, and deep-learning-based recognition. First, a residual 2D CNN-based network determines whether an iris image is non-occluded or belongs to one of the controlled occlusion categories. Second, the occluded image, binary mask, and predicted occlusion type condition a denoising diffusion probabilistic model to reconstruct the corrupted region. Finally, VGG19-HPMNet, a modified VGG19 model with horizontal pyramid mapping, extracts discriminative global and part-wise local iris features for recognition. Experiments on the CASIA-Iris-Thousand dataset under a controlled synthetic-occlusion protocol show that the proposed framework improves iris recognition performance by identifying the occlusion type, reconstructing masked regions, and re-evaluating the restored iris samples.

</details>


### [84] [UnDA: Unpaired Domain Alignment for Cross-Modal Knowledge Transfer in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21546)
*Rafsan Jany,Shadab Tanjeed Ahmad,Ahsan Bulbul,Tahsinul Islam,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnDA框架，通过无配对跨模态知识蒸馏，利用锚定引导、不确定性加权最优传输和原型对比学习，在无需配对数据的情况下实现异构医学图像分割任务中的结构知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态方法优于单模态，但配对的临床数据难以获取；现有跨模态知识蒸馏方法面临模态差距大和源域预测噪声传播的问题。

Method: 提出UnDA框架，包含：(1) 主干无关的Alignment Module，通过注意力池化提取语义结构化的类别标记；(2) 不确定性加权最优传输(UCT-OT)，根据预测置信度动态调整特征级对齐权重以抑制噪声；(3) 每类ProtoNCE目标函数，维护稳定的原型记忆以增强全局判别性。

Result: 在严格的非配对设置下进行代表性分割任务评估，目标模态在准确性和边界精度上均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 证明无需配对数据集即可在异构数据源之间迁移有意义的结构知识。

Abstract: Multimodal based approaches often outperform single modality approaches in downstream tasks as the different modalities provide complementary information, yet acquiring paired clinical data remains a significant challenge in real world scenarios. While cross-modal knowledge distillation addresses this, existing methods often struggle with large modality gaps and the propagation of noise from uncertain source-domain predictions. To overcome these challenges, we propose UnDA, an anchor-guided framework for unpaired cross-modal distillation. Our approach introduces a backbone-agnostic Alignment Module that extracts semantically structured class tokens via an attention based pooling mechanism. To ensure robust knowledge transfer, we propose Uncertainty-Weighted Optimal Transport (UCT-OT), which dynamically weights feature-level alignment based on prediction confidence, effectively suppressing noisy supervision. Furthermore, a per-class ProtoNCE objective maintains stable prototype memories to enforce global discriminability across unpaired batches. Evaluations on representative segmentation tasks under strictly unpaired settings show consistent improvements in accuracy and boundary precision in the target modality, demonstrating that meaningful structural knowledge can be transferred across heterogeneous data sources without paired datasets.

</details>


### [85] [SANA-Video 2.0: Hybrid Linear Attention with Attention Residuals for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21553)
*Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Yitong Li,Shuchen Xue,Haozhe Liu,Jingyu Xin,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Zhangjie Wu,Zian Wang,Daquan Zhou,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-Video 2.0 提出混合线性-软性注意力机制与块注意力残差，在单GPU上实现最高720p视频生成，以线性复杂度匹配全软性注意力视频DiT的质量，5B模型在480p/40步时VBench得分84.30，推理速度13.2秒，相比全软性注意力基线加速3.2倍，结合Sol-Engine优化后5B管线在720p/5秒时仅需13.06秒，比Wan 2.2-A14B快120倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频DiT模型采用全软性注意力机制具有二次计算复杂度，限制了长序列和高分辨率视频生成的可扩展性；而纯线性注意力虽具有O(N)复杂度，但缺乏全秩令牌交互能力，生成质量下降。因此需要一种既能保持线性注意力长序列优势又能恢复软性注意力表达能力的混合方法。

Method: 提出Hybrid Linear-Softmax Attention：以3:1比例组合门控线性注意力（O(N)主导混合）与周期门控软性注意力锚点，恢复纯线性注意力缺失的全秩令牌交互；设计Block Attention Residuals (AttnRes)：将完成的块摘要路由到后续线性层，实现锚点特征复用，提升深层有效秩约12%；从零训练完整混合架构而非线性化预训练模型，通过降分辨率代理研究确定25%软性注意力为最优质量-效率权衡。

Result: 5B模型在480p/40步采样时VBench得分84.30，单H100推理耗时13.2秒；编译后的DiT前向在720p/60秒时比全软性注意力基线快3.2倍，差距随视频时长扩大；结合Sol-Engine优化（核融合、缓存、稀疏注意力）再加速3.58倍，5B管线在720p/5秒时仅需13.06秒，比Wan 2.2-A14B快120倍。

Conclusion: 混合线性-软性注意力设计以显著降低的计算成本恢复了软性注意力级别的表达能力，实现了可扩展的长时高分辨率视频生成，验证了线性复杂度架构在大规模视频生成中的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-Video 2.0, a hybrid video diffusion transformer instantiated at 5B and 14B scales under a unified architecture. Designed to generate high-quality video up to 720p on a single GPU, SANA-Video 2.0 matches full-softmax video DiTs in quality while retaining the favorable long-sequence scaling of linear attention. To avoid quadratic attention throughout, Hybrid Linear-Softmax Attention combines gated linear attention for O(N)-dominated mixing with periodic gated-softmax anchors at a 3:1 ratio, restoring the full-rank token interactions that pure linear attention lacks. To propagate these refreshed representations across depth, Block Attention Residuals (AttnRes) route completed block summaries into later linear layers, enabling anchor-feature reuse and boosting deep-layer effective rank by ~12%. Through from-scratch training, SANA-Video 2.0 learns the complete hybrid directly rather than linearizing pretrained models, with reduced-resolution proxy studies establishing 25% softmax as the optimal quality-efficiency trade-off. With 40-step sampling, SANA-Video 2.0 achieves a VBench score of 84.30 in 13.2s at 480p on a single H100, remaining competitive with far larger softmax video DiTs at a fraction of the latency. Its compiled DiT forward pass is 3.2x faster than a matched full-softmax baseline at 720p/60s, a gap that expands with video duration. Furthermore, full-stack Sol-Engine optimization (kernel fusion, caching, and sparse attention) accelerates this hardware-friendly backbone by a further 3.58x, bringing the 5B pipeline to 13.06s at 720p/5s and making it 120x faster than Wan 2.2-A14B on one H100. Overall, our hybrid design recovers softmax-level expressiveness at substantially reduced cost, unlocking scalable long, high resolution video generation.

</details>


### [86] [Visual Contrastive Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21556)
*Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Yijiang Li,Yuqi Jia,Furong Huang,Tianyi Zhou,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VCSD，一种纯输入条件驱动的同策略自蒸馏方法，通过视觉对比学习（比较原始图像与内容擦除控制下的token分布差异）消除对特权答案和视觉证据的需求，在ViRL39K数据集上显著提升Qwen3-VL和Qwen3.5多模态模型的多基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽无需外部教师，但仍依赖特权答案或视觉证据来制造师生信息不对称。本文探索能否移除这两者，实现完全由输入条件驱动的更简化的OPSD形式。

Method: VCSD方法：在学生生成的每个响应前缀处，EMA教师在同一提示和前缀下生成两种next-token分布——分别以原始图像和内容擦除控制图像为条件；计算两者token级log概率差以突出受实例级视觉内容显著影响的候选词；利用此对比在合理支持域内锐化教师原始图像分布，并将完整分布目标蒸馏给学生。

Result: 在ViRL39K数据集上，VCSD在Qwen3-VL上显著提升：2B参数模型七基准聚合从62.27%→67.04%，4B从71.30%→73.16%，8B从72.51%→76.26%。且无需外部教师、特权答案、视觉证据、推理轨迹或额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 纯输入条件驱动的同策略自蒸馏是可行且有效的，VCSD通过视觉对比学习成功去除了对显式不对称信息的依赖，为多模态模型自蒸馏提供了更简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is promising as it removes the external teacher required by on-policy distillation (OPD), yet it still needs asymmetric information between teacher and student to ensure that the self-teacher provides a stronger learning signal than the student. Existing methods create this asymmetry either through privileged answers or visual evidence. We ask whether both can be removed, yielding a simpler form of OPSD driven purely by input conditioning. For this purpose, we propose Visual Contrastive Self-Distillation, namely VCSD, which converts image-content removal into an on-policy self-distillation signal. At each student-generated response prefix, the EMA teacher produces two next-token distributions under the same prompt and prefix -- one conditioned on the original image and the other on a content-erased control. Their token-wise log-probability difference highlights candidates whose likelihood is specifically increased by the instance-level visual content. We use this contrast to sharpen the teacher's original-image distribution within its plausible support, and distill the resulting full-distribution target into the student. Using ViRL39K dataset, VCSD consistently outperforms matched OPSD across Qwen3-VL and Qwen3.5 models. For example, on Qwen3-VL, it improves the seven-benchmark aggregate from $62.27\% \rightarrow 67.04\%$ at 2B, $71.30\% \rightarrow 73.16\%$ at 4B, and $72.51\% \rightarrow 76.26\%$ at 8B. Furthermore, VCSD requires no external teacher, privileged answers, visual evidence signals, reasoning traces, or additional inference-time cost.

</details>


### [87] [Scene Parameter Saliency via Differentiable Light Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21562)
*Linas Beresna,Eugene Fiume*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“度量显著性图”概念，利用可微渲染器的反向微分，将梯度从渲染图像传播回场景参数，从而识别哪些场景元素对特定度量（如亮度、感知得分）影响最大。与神经网络显著性不同，该方法通过光传输过程而非学习权重进行归因，且显著性排名因度量而异，表明显著性图是度量特定的，而非场景固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微渲染器通常用于参数优化，但作者观察到它们实际上也能产生类似神经网络显著性分析的效果——通过一次反向微分即可获得每个场景参数对某个标量度量的梯度，从而揭示哪些场景元素对该度量影响最大。作者希望将这一被忽视的能力系统化，并证明可微渲染器生成的“导数图像”与原始渲染图像同样具有场景理解价值。

Method: 给定一个在渲染图像上评估的标量度量，通过可微渲染器执行一次反向模式微分，得到每个场景参数对该度量的梯度，即“度量显著性图”。该方法可处理多跳光传输，捕获人工难以直接检查的参数依赖关系。作者在三个不同目标上计算显著性图：心理物理眩光指数、平均场景亮度、神经感知得分。

Result: 同一场景下不同度量的显著性排名存在显著差异：在一个度量中占主导地位的参数在另一个度量中可能可忽略不计。显著性图是度量特定的，而非场景的固有属性。

Conclusion: 可微渲染器生成的导数图像（度量显著性图）与原始渲染图像同样富含场景理解信息。这一发现扩展了可微渲染的应用范围，从单纯的参数优化延伸到场景分析和解释性任务。

Abstract: Gradient-based saliency methods reveal which input features most influence a neural network's output, and are a standard tool for model interpretability. We observe that differentiable renderers, which are conventionally used for parameter optimisation, produce an analogous form of saliency: given any scalar metric evaluated on a rendered image, a single reverse-mode differentiation pass yields per-parameter gradients that identify which scene elements most influence the metric. We call these gradient fields metric saliency maps. Unlike neural saliency, which propagates attribution through learned weights, metric saliency propagates through the image formation process itself, including multi-bounce light transport, capturing parameter dependencies that are semi-opaque to manual inspection. We compute metric saliency maps for qualitatively different objectives: psychovisual glare indices, mean scene luminance, and neural perceptual scores. The saliency rankings differ substantially across metrics for the same scene, with parameters that dominate one objective being negligible for another. The saliency map is specific to the metric, not an intrinsic property of the scene. Our results suggest that differentiable renderers produce derivative images that are as informative for scene understanding as the primal images they were designed to generate.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Supervised Learning of Structured Dynamics from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21576)
*Lukas Knobel,Andrew Zisserman,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化动力学模型(SDM)，通过解耦相机运动和物体运动，从预训练图像ViT的冻结特征中学习结构化视频动态表示，在ProbeMotion评测集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中帧间变化同时包含相机运动和物体运动，这两种动态在表示学习中难以解耦和监督，但分离它们对学习鲁棒的运动表示至关重要。

Method: SDM模型通过未来特征预测的方式，显式分离时间变化的主要来源（相机运动）与残差动态（物体运动），结合真实视频的自监督学习和合成Kubric数据的弱监督训练。

Result: 在ProbeMotion评测集上，SDM优于使用全局CLS或平均池化特征的骨干基线，且在与强监督方法VGGT比较中表现有竞争力，尽管使用了更弱的监督信号。

Conclusion: 预训练图像模型可以有效地重用于结构化视频动态表示，为学习和分析潜在视频动态提供有用的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Understanding motion in video is a fundamental challenge for visual learning, as frame-to-frame change entangles two sources of dynamics: camera motion and object motion. This decomposition has remained underexplored in representation learning, partly because these factors are tightly coupled in natural videos and difficult to supervise separately. Yet recovering it is important for learning robust motion representations that separate meaningful object dynamics from camera-induced variation. We study whether such structured motion representations can be recovered from frozen features of a pretrained image vision transformer. We propose the Structured Dynamics Model (SDM), which explicitly separates the dominant source of temporal change from residual dynamics through future-feature prediction, rather than representing video change with a single entangled latent or with unstructured, spatially dense transition tokens. Training combines self-supervised learning on real video with weak supervision of scene dynamics on synthetic Kubric data. We evaluate SDM on ProbeMotion, a new evaluation suite spanning synthetic and real videos with camera motion, object motion, and combined dynamics. SDM outperforms backbone baselines using global CLS or average-pooled features, and compares favorably to strongly supervised representations such as VGGT on several probes, despite using substantially weaker supervision. These results suggest that pretrained image models can be readily repurposed into structured video-dynamics representations, providing a useful inductive bias for learning and analyzing latent video dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Synthetic data generation framework for quality control automation in gravure printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21577)
*Korota Arsène Coulibaly,Mohamed Hamlich,Khalid Hmali,Andrea Trombin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向凹版印刷质量控制的合成数据生成框架，自动生成高保真印刷缺陷图像及标注，解决了真实工业缺陷图像极度稀缺的问题。基于该框架生成的7533张合成图像训练的RFDETR模型，在真实工业测试样本上达到了80.9%的mAP，实现了零成本、快速部署的自动化缺陷检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹版印刷中的质量控制仍依赖缓慢、昂贵且主观的人工检测。深度学习模型（如YOLO、Vision Transformers）虽有前景，但训练鲁棒模型受到真实工业缺陷图像极度稀缺的严重阻碍。

Method: 提出一种面向凹版印刷质量控制的合成数据生成框架，能够自动生成高保真的特定印刷缺陷图像（如折痕、条纹、套印不准等），并输出对应的边界框和标注信息。使用该框架生成的7533张合成数据集训练了最先进的目标检测模型RFDETR。

Result: 在真实工业测试样本上，基于合成数据训练的RFDETR模型取得了80.9%的平均精度均值（mAP）。

Conclusion: 该框架为印刷生产线自动化缺陷检测提供了一种零成本、快速部署的解决方案，无需大量人工数据采集。

Abstract: Quality control in printing, particularly in rotogravure printing, still depends on slow, costly, and subjective manual inspection. Automated surface defect detection is critical for maintaining high-quality standards in rotogravure printing. Deep learning models give prospects for automation. However, training robust deep learning models, such as YOLO or Vision Transformers, is heavily hindered by the extreme scarcity of real-world industrial defects images. To overcome this limitation, this paper introduces a novel synthetic data generation framework tailored for rotogravure printing quality control. The proposed pipeline automatically generates high-fidelity images of specific printing defects (creases, streaks, misregistration, etc.) and outputs corresponding bounding boxes and annotations. To validate the framework, a synthetic dataset of 7533 images was generated and used to train the state-of-the-art object-detection model RFDETR. Experimental results demonstrate that the model trained on our synthetic data achieves a Mean Average Precision (mAP) of 80.9\% on real industrial testing samples. This framework provides a zero-cost, rapid-deployment solution for automating defect inspection in printing lines without requiring massive manual data collection.

</details>


### [90] [GraphVid: Interactive Graph-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21580)
*Vedant Shah,Onkar Susladkar,Tushar Prakash,Kiet Nguyen,Tianjio Yu,Adheesh Juvekar,Muntasir Waheed,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphVid，一种基于图结构的图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化交互图实现多主体可控视频生成，并构建了GraphVid-Bench数据集。相比Motion-I2V，GraphVid在FID上降低39.9%，FVD降低37.6%，PSNR从9.87提升至15.98，SSIM从0.38提升至0.61，展示了结构化语义界面在可控视频生成中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法依赖文本提示或运动控制输入来约束像素运动，但在多物体交互场景中，轨迹控制需要用户为多个物体绘制精确轨迹，随场景复杂度增加而扩展性差，且在遮挡或重叠情况下存在歧义。因此需要一种更灵活且精确的多主体控制方法。

Method: 提出GraphVid，一种基于图条件化的图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化交互图实现交互式多主体控制；同时构建GraphVid-Bench，一个大规模以交互为中心的视频数据集，包含结构化关系标注，用于训练交互感知的视频生成模型。

Result: 相比Motion-I2V，GraphVid在FID上降低39.9%，FVD降低37.6%，PSNR从9.87提升至15.98，SSIM从0.38提升至0.61，且使用的训练数据和可训练参数显著更少。

Conclusion: 结构化语义界面作为一种强大的可控视频生成范式具有巨大潜力，GraphVid证明了通过结构化图条件实现灵活、精确的多主体视频控制的可行性。

Abstract: Controllable video generation remains challenging due to the difficulty of specifying precise multi-object interactions using text prompts or motion-control inputs that primarily constrain pixel movement. In practice, trajectory-based control often requires users to draw accurate tracks for multiple objects, which scales poorly with scene complexity and becomes ambiguous under occlusion or overlap. To enable flexible yet precise multi-subject control, we introduce $\textbf{GraphVid}$, a graph-conditioned image-to-video generation model that enables interactive control through structured interaction graphs. We further curate $\textbf{GraphVid-Bench}$, a large-scale interaction-centric video dataset with structured relational annotations to enable training of interaction-aware video generation models. Despite using substantially less training data and fewer trainable parameters than prior motion-control methods, GraphVid delivers strong controllability and video quality. Compared with Motion-I2V, GraphVid reduces FID by up to 39.9% and FVD by 37.6%, while improving PSNR (9.87=>15.98) and SSIM (0.38=>0.61). Our results highlight the potential of structured semantic interfaces as a powerful paradigm for controllable video generation.

</details>


### [91] [Inference-Time Scaling of Diffusion Models via Progressive Seed Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21591)
*Rogerio Guimaraes,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式种子剪枝（PSP）方法，通过在推理早期阶段评估大量噪声种子并激进剪枝，固定计算预算下更有效地探索扩散模型的条件图像生成。实验表明，PSP在奖励引导选择和提示对齐方面优于best-of-N、重要性采样和树搜索等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型主导条件图像生成，但其推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）远不如自回归语言模型成熟。最终质量对初始噪声种子高度敏感，现有方法通常使用额外计算进行种子搜索或重采样，但推理过程中保持恒定内存占用。作者希望放松这一约束，探索一种新的推理时扩展维度。

Method: 提出Progressive Seed Pruning (PSP)：在推理早期阶段，评估大量中间去噪估计，逐步缩小候选集，对中间得分进行评分并激进剪枝，仅保留有希望的轨迹进行完整去噪，同时保持总模型评估次数固定。通过前端加载探索（front-loading exploration），在早期评估许多种子并激进剪枝，更有效地利用固定计算预算。

Result: 在扩散和流匹配主干网络上，PSP在奖励引导选择方面持续改进，在GenEval得分（自动评估）和人工评估的提示对齐方面优于best-of-N、重要性采样和树搜索等基线方法，且计算量相匹配。

Conclusion: 放松推理过程中内存占用恒定的约束，采用前端加载探索和渐进剪枝策略，可以在固定计算预算下更有效地提升扩散模型的推理性能，为扩散模型的推理时扩展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching models dominate conditional image generation, yet inference-time scaling for these models is far less developed than for autoregressive language models. Because final quality is highly sensitive to the initial noise seed, many approaches spend extra compute on seed search or resampling under a black-box reward, but typically maintaining a constant memory footprint throughout inference. We show that relaxing this constraint enables an underexplored inference-time scaling axis: by front-loading exploration, evaluating many seeds early, and pruning aggressively, we can use a fixed compute budget more effectively. \emph{Progressive Seed Pruning} (\PSP) scores intermediate denoised estimates and progressively narrows the candidate set so that only promising trajectories are fully denoised, while keeping the total number of model evaluations fixed. Across diffusion and flow-matching backbones, \PSP \ consistently improves reward-guided selection and achieves higher GenEval scores (automated) and better human evaluation on prompt-alignment than best-of-$N$, importance-sampling, and tree-search baselines at matched compute. Project page: https://www.vision.caltech.edu/psp. Code: https://github.com/rogerioagjr/psp.

</details>


### [92] [Unified Video Dense Prediction from Disjoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21592)
*Yihong Sun,Seoung Wug Oh,Jiahui Huang,Bharath Hariharan,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniD提出了一种统一的视频模型，通过知识蒸馏从分离的领域特定数据集中学习八种密集场景属性（几何、外观和语义），无需标注重叠或伪标签，利用预训练扩散模型的视觉先验弥合域间隙，在多任务和泛化方面取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景理解系统受限于任务特定标注的碎片化问题——不同数据集覆盖不同任务且领域不兼容。统一系统要么依赖完全共标注数据（稀缺），要么使用伪标签（计算成本高），缺乏高效利用分离标注数据的方法。

Method: 提出UniD统一视频模型，采用两阶段训练：1）各任务专家在各自领域数据上独立训练；2）通过轻量级任务投影器，利用预训练扩散模型的强大视觉先验作为桥梁，将专家知识蒸馏到统一骨干网络，无需标注重叠或伪标签生成。

Result: 在深度、法向、语义分割、边界、人体部件、反照率、着色和材质八项任务上，UniD与单任务专家和多任务基线性能相当，且在分布外场景中展现出强泛化能力，同时提升了时序一致性和跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: 预训练扩散模型蕴含的视觉先验足以弥合分离训练数据带来的域间隙，使统一模型能够泛化到训练中从未见过的场景-任务组合，为高效利用碎片化标注数据提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Scene understanding requires simultaneous prediction about geometry, appearance, and semantics. However, existing task-specific annotations are fragmented across incompatible, domain-specific datasets. Current unified systems circumvent this by restricting training to fully co-annotated data, or by incurring the large computational cost of pseudo-labeling. To mitigate this, we introduce UniD, a unified video model that jointly predicts eight dense scene properties-depth, surface normals, semantic segmentation, boundaries, human parts, albedo, shading, and materials-all learned from disjoint, domain-specific datasets. We propose a simple yet effective distillation step in which per-task experts supervise a unified backbone through lightweight task projectors, eliminating the need for annotation overlap or pseudo-labeling. Our key insight is that the strong visual priors of a pretrained diffusion model are sufficient to bridge the domain gaps introduced by disjoint training sources, enabling robust generalization to scene-task combinations never seen during training. UniD achieves competitive performance against per-task specialists and multi-task baselines, with strong generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and enhanced temporal and cross-task consistency. Code and video results are available at https://unid-video.github.io/.

</details>


### [93] [Streaming Multi-Agent Autoregressive Diffusion Model with World State Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21594)
*Sicheng Mo,Yuheng Li,Ziyang Leng,Krishna Kumar Singh,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeaver (W²) 是一种流式多智能体视频扩散模型，通过引入可学习的跨智能体世界状态寄存器（world state registers）来维护共享世界信息，并使用 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构分离世界状态建模与视觉帧建模，在双智能体 Minecraft 视频生成中显著提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型仅将观测历史作为条件上下文传递，在多智能体、多视角场景下难以维护共享世界状态，导致逻辑不一致。

Method: 提出 WorldWeaver (W²)，包含：(1) 可学习的跨智能体世界状态寄存器，在生成每个片段后动态更新，存储全局和个体状态；(2) 使用个体智能体状态、鸟瞰图全局视图、场景文本等多重监督信号进行训练；(3) 采用 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构，用独立权重分别处理世界状态建模和视觉帧建模。

Result: 在双智能体 Minecraft 视频生成任务中，显式的世界状态建模显著提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。

Conclusion: 显式维护世界状态寄存器是提升多智能体多视角视频生成一致性的有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent interactive world models should not only generate consistent observations, but also maintain world states that persist across agents and evolve across views. Existing autoregressive video diffusion pipelines carry forward observation history as conditioning context, which makes shared state difficult to maintain in multi-agent and multi-view settings. We present WorldWeaver (W^2), a streaming multi-agent video diffusion model that augments rollout with cross-agent world state registers: learnable tokens that store shared world information, track individual agent status, and are dynamically updated after each generated chunk. We ground these registers with supervision signals spanning individual agent status, global state views including bird's-eye views, and scene text. We further improve the architecture with a Mixture-of-Transformers design that uses separate weights for world state modeling and visual frame modeling. Extensive experiments in two-agent Minecraft video generation show that explicit world-state modeling improves logical consistency and generation quality.

</details>


### [94] [3D-Aware VLMs with Implicit and Explicit Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21595)
*Wenhao Li,Xueying Jiang,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Ran Xu,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-IE3D框架，通过从RGB视频中学习隐式和显式3D几何特征（隐式几何令牌IGT和显式几何令牌EGT），并借助3D感知适配器与2D视觉线索融合，在无需额外3D输入的情况下显著提升视觉语言模型在多种3D任务（3D视频检测、3D视觉定位、3D密集描述、空间推理）上的细粒度空间理解与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D视觉输入的视觉语言模型在处理需要细粒度空间理解与推理的3D任务时表现不佳，缺乏有效的3D空间感知能力。

Method: 提出VLM-IE3D统一框架：(1) 隐式几何令牌(IGT)从输入视频中捕捉高层几何先验；(2) 显式几何令牌(EGT)从重建的3D属性中编码详细几何结构；(3) 3D感知适配器将两种几何表示与2D视觉线索有效融合，仅需RGB输入即可注入强3D归纳偏置。

Result: 在3D视频检测、3D视觉定位、3D密集描述和空间推理等多种3D任务上持续取得优越性能。

Conclusion: VLM-IE3D通过隐式和显式几何令牌的联合学习与融合，有效增强了VLMs的3D空间感知能力，且无需额外3D输入，为提升VLMs在3D任务上的表现提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, most existing vision-language models (VLMs) built from 2D visual inputs often struggle when handling various 3D tasks that require fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning. To bridge this gap, we present VLM-IE3D, a unified framework that enhances the 3D spatial awareness of VLMs by equipping them with both implicit and explicit 3D geometries learned from RGB videos. Our VLM-IE3D introduces Implicit Geometry Tokens (IGTs) that capture high-level geometric priors from input videos, as well as complementary Explicit Geometry Tokens (EGTs) that encode detailed geometric structures from reconstructed 3D attributes. On top of that, VLM-IE3D comes with a 3D-aware adapter that effectively fuses the two types of geometric representations with 2D visual cues. This RGB-only design injects strong 3D inductive biases for fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning without requiring any additional 3D inputs. Extensive experiments show that VLM-IE3D achieves superior performance consistently across various 3D tasks including 3D video detection, 3D visual grounding, 3D dense captioning, and spatial reasoning. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Vegetebird/VLM-IE3D.

</details>


### [95] [Deblurring in the Wild: A Real-World Image Deblurring Dataset from Smartphone High-Speed Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19445)
*Syed Mumtahin Mahmud,Mahdi Mohd Hossain Noki,Prothito Shovon Majumder,Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Sudipto Das Sukanto,Afia Lubaina,Md. Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于智能手机慢动作视频构建的最大真实世界图像去模糊数据集，包含超过42,000对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像，比现有数据集大10倍，场景数量多8倍，并对多个SOTA模型进行了基准测试，发现性能显著下降，可作为新的挑战性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊数据集规模小、场景有限，缺乏真实世界的复杂性和多样性，难以训练出鲁棒且泛化能力强的去模糊模型。作者旨在构建一个大规模、高多样性、真实世界的去模糊基准数据集。

Method: 利用智能手机慢动作视频（每秒240帧）捕捉一秒钟内的连续帧，通过平均多帧模拟长时间曝光模糊生成模糊图像，以时间中心帧作为清晰参考，构建了超过42,000对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像对，涵盖室内外多种场景和不同的物体/相机运动。

Result: 数据集规模约为现有数据集的10倍，场景数量多8倍。在多个SOTA去模糊模型上的基准测试显示性能显著下降，证明了数据集的复杂性和多样性。

Conclusion: 该数据集作为一个具有挑战性的新基准，能够促进更鲁棒和泛化能力更强的去模糊模型的发展。

Abstract: We introduce the largest real-world image deblurring dataset constructed from smartphone slow-motion videos. Using 240 frames captured over one second, we simulate realistic long-exposure blur by averaging frames to produce blurry images, while using the temporally centered frame as the sharp reference. Our dataset contains over 42,000 high-resolution blur-sharp image pairs, making it approximately 10 times larger than widely used datasets, with 8 times the amount of different scenes, including indoor and outdoor environments, with varying object and camera motions. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art (SOTA) deblurring models on our dataset and observe significant performance degradation, highlighting the complexity and diversity of our benchmark. Our dataset serves as a challenging new benchmark to facilitate robust and generalizable deblurring models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AINTMA，一个由六个专门AI代理组成的多智能体自主测试管理系统，通过强化学习优先排序、生成式质量情报和零信任安全通信，在12个软件项目18个月评估中实现88.4%测试优先排序准确率、43%测试周期缩短、缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%、340%投资回报率，支持50000+测试用例且响应时间低于400ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策的智能自主系统，传统测试管理方法无法满足云规模企业环境的需求。

Method: 提出AINTMA架构，部署六个专门AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先排序、执行编排、生成式质量情报、云安全监控），通过云原生微服务基础设施上的安全多代理通信框架协调。RL优先排序代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，从大规模历史测试执行数据（47个特征，滚动36个月窗口）学习上下文策略；生成式质量情报代理使用大语言模型生成自然语言质量叙述；安全通信通过零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT认证、加密代理间消息和多租户隔离实现。

Result: 在12个异构软件项目18个月评估中：测试优先排序准确率88.4%（APFD，对比随机51.2%、最佳商业基线82.1%）；测试周期减少43%；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；340%投资回报率，9个月回收期；支持50000+测试用例，响应时间低于400ms；生成式智能模块开发者有用性评分为4.3/5.0。

Conclusion: AINTMA证明，结合自主多代理协调、生成式智能和安全的智能连接性的智能体AI，可以在云规模企业环境中从根本上推进软件质量管理。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [97] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM水印对医学性能的影响，在11个LLM和7个VLM上评估5种水印方案，发现水印会显著降低医学推理质量、术语精度并导致幻觉，且现有通用基准无法检测这些临床关键退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于临床工作流，需要可靠的水印溯源技术，但现有水印评估仅在通用基准上进行，忽视了医学领域中小扰动即可导致重大语义变化的问题，缺乏对医学性能影响的研究。

Method: 在11个LLM和7个VLM上，对5种水印方案进行单模态和多模态临床推理任务基准测试；引入人工专家验证的流水线，系统审计医学推理质量、术语精度和诱发幻觉。

Result: 水印会导致多种退化：词汇损坏、幻觉术语、图像发现归因错误或遗漏增加；缺乏领域特定分析和聚合指标会系统性掩盖临床文本中的退化。

Conclusion: 领域特定评估是水印模型在医学中安全部署的前提条件，当前通用基准可能掩盖临床关键失败模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [98] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种AI驱动的浏览器扩展，采用混合机器学习架构（XGBoost + Transformer嵌入 + 语言特征 + "诱饵度"评分），在公开数据集上达到91% F1分数，能在用户点击前后警告点击诱饵文章，并提供百分比评分、可解释性分析和文章摘要欺骗提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点击诱饵检测方法存在局限，用户缺乏有效的实时防护工具，需要一种能在用户浏览网页时提供及时警告和可解释性反馈的解决方案，帮助用户避免被误导性文章欺骗。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构：结合Transformer嵌入（如LLM嵌入）与语言学特征，并引入自定义的"baitness"（诱饵度）评分指标。从经典向量化方法到大型语言模型嵌入等多种NLP技术中评估选择，最终基于XGBoost构建分类模型，并开发浏览器扩展实现实时检测与用户交互。

Result: 基于XGBoost的模型在公开组合数据集上达到了91%的F1分数，浏览器扩展能够在用户访问点击诱饵文章前后发出警告，并提供百分比概率评分、基于分析指标的预测解释以及1-2句话的文章摘要（点击诱饵欺骗提示）。

Conclusion: 该AI驱动的浏览器扩展有效结合了多种NLP技术和自定义指标，实现了高精度的点击诱饵检测，同时通过实时警告、可解释性评分和欺骗提示功能，为用户提供了完整的防护体验。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [99] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对比单模型多次采样（自一致性）与多模型集成（多样性集成）两种不确定性估计方法，发现自一致性不能检测跨问题结构，只有多样性集成才能揭示模型的知识边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型重复运行时产生的回答变异性是否不仅能通过自一致性（多数投票）提供逐问题的不确定性估计，还能揭示跨问题之间的关联结构（即不同问题是否像多样性集成那样一起翻转）。

Method: 在相同问题集上比较两种设置：单个模型在温度τ=1下运行100次 vs. 24个不同LLM组成的集成模型各在τ=0下运行1次。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵检验从信号中分离采样噪声。

Result: 单模型自一致性在所有五个模型系列和三个基准（MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K）上，最多只有一个维度高于噪声阈值；而多样性集成有四个特征值显著高于噪声阈值。匹配难度的伯努利零模型在500次蒙特卡洛模拟中最多产生一个虚假信号。

Conclusion: 自一致性能够提供准确的逐问题不确定性估计，但无法检测到跨问题结构；只有多样性集成才能揭示模型不知道什么。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [100] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench是一个专为Google Cloud TPU设计的AI生成内核优化基准测试套件，包含50个JAX工作负载（17个来自MaxText库的生产ML算子，33个来自KernelBench的翻译算子）。研究发现，在文档稀疏的Pallas DSL上，目标特定上下文比模型规模更重要：通过TPU文档条件化将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决了48/50个基准。AutoComp的波束搜索达到1.36x几何平均加速，在8个手工调优核上达到1.60x加速，接近2.08x的专家上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化领域已有成熟的基准测试推动进展，但TPU领域缺乏等效的基准测试套件，阻碍了AI生成的TPU内核优化研究。

Method: 构建包含50个JAX工作负载的基准套件：从MaxText库提取17个生产ML算子（涵盖Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-V3、Mixtral、Mamba-2、AlphaFold2等架构），并从KernelBench翻译33个算子并验证正确性。8个算子配有来自Tokamax库的手工优化Pallas内核作为专家上界基线。评估了四种反馈驱动方法在生成候选Pallas内核上的表现。

Result: TPU文档条件化将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决48/50个基准，速度提升1.28x。AutoComp波束搜索达1.36x几何平均加速，在8个手工核上达1.60x（接近2.08x Tokamax上界）。

Conclusion: 高质量TPU内核优化仍具挑战性，目标特定上下文比模型规模更重要。发布了JAXBench基准测试、评估框架和基线结果以支持开源贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [101] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap 是一种无需训练的框架，通过动态连续验证策略和草稿引导解码机制，在适度置信度区域实现扩散大语言模型的可靠加速，最高在 MBPP 上实现 53.19 倍加速，结合 KV-Cache 可达 105.02 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散大语言模型的并行解码策略因联合概率依赖错误（JPDE）而采用过于保守的置信度阈值，导致冗余的去噪迭代和次优的推理速度。

Method: 提出 DC-Leap 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束整合到并行解码中，逐步验证 token 依赖关系以消除 JPDE；(2) 草稿引导解码机制，利用草稿向前跳跃多个 token 以扩展上下文，在推理时保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，DC-Leap 在 MBPP 长序列生成上实现 53.19 倍加速，结合 KV-Cache 最高可达 105.02 倍，且生成质量相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap 通过解决 JPDE 问题，在适度置信度区域实现了无需训练的可靠加速，显著提升了扩散大语言模型的推理效率。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [102] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench是一个评估AI Agent在开放式AI工程中优化LLM推理速度的基准。Agent需在2小时内、使用单张H100 GPU，针对预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量及综合场景优化推理速度。尽管前沿Agent相比PyTorch基线最高提升8.08倍，但低于简单超参数搜索（最高11.53倍）。定性分析显示Agent虽枚举多种技术，但往往收敛于单一推理框架，缺乏探索多样性策略的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常让Agent执行规定好的工作流程或狭窄的操作空间，即使是名义上的开放式任务也常可通过检索已知方案和调整少量超参数解决，难以区分Agent表现是来自真正的优化能力还是记忆的解决方案。因此需要一个能够反映Agent在开放式AI工程环境中实际能力的基准。

Method: 构建InferenceBench基准，要求Agent部署OpenAI兼容推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个Agent获得一个目标LLM、一张H100 GPU、一个优化场景（预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量或三者平衡）和两小时墙钟时间预算。在15个前沿Agent配置上进行评估，并对Agent轨迹进行定性分析。

Result: 前沿Agent相比朴素PyTorch基线最高提升8.08倍，常能匹配或超过带默认设置的vLLM推理引擎（4.05倍），但仍低于相同时间预算下简单超参数搜索的效果（最高11.53倍）。Agent虽枚举了多种相关优化技术，但绝大多数收敛于单一推理框架，仅测试少数不同配置，剩余时间用于重新测量、修复或优化超参数而非探索不同策略。

Conclusion: Agent的瓶颈不在于领域知识的缺乏，而在于提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最佳解决方案的能力不足。InferenceBench能有效反映Agent在开放式AI工程环境中的能力，在此类场景下记忆的解决方案只能带来有限的改进。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [103] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DecodeShare协议，识别LLM在解码阶段隐藏状态中跨任务共享的低维子空间，并验证其因果作用。干扰该子空间比干扰预填充或随机子空间对决策性能影响更大；该子空间可用于激活引导，且解码时评估引导向量比预填充代理更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在KV缓存推理场景下，LLM解码阶段的隐藏状态中是否存在任务通用结构尚不明确，论文旨在探究并验证这一结构的存在及其因果作用。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议，识别解码时隐藏状态中跨任务共享的低维子空间，通过去除该子空间进行因果干预实验，并在激活引导任务中验证其实用性。

Result: 干扰解码共享子空间比干扰预填充或随机子空间对决策性能破坏更大；解码共享子空间与常见激活引导方向存在重叠；投影去除该子空间可分离两种功能组件；解码时评估引导向量比预填充代理更可靠。

Conclusion: 解码阶段存在一个紧凑但高杠杆的跨任务共享子空间，具有因果重要性，可用于更有效的激活引导和下游部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [104] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个用于构建基于抽取式的大语言模型的规划框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理三部分，并引入DTI规划器选择最优基座模型及其组合，以降低标注成本并提升任务特定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM在增强任务特定能力时需要大量的指令微调数据集，但数据集规模过大会带来高昂的标注成本，且缺乏针对特定任务优化LLM的方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理；并引入DTI（Data-Tuning-Inference）规划器，用于为特定数据集选择最优的基座LLM及其DTI组合，以提高构建效率。

Result: 实验结果从两个视角验证了PlanE的有效性：(1) 相同基座LLM在不同数据集上的表现；(2) 相同数据集在不同基座LLM上的表现。同时验证了DTI规划器在不同优化目标下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlanE框架能有效降低标注成本并提升LLM在特定任务上的能力，DTI规划器具有良好的泛化性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SDR-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在经典“两方个性化”问题（生成消息诱导第三方采取行动）中的表现。研究发现现有前沿模型存在一致的个性化瓶颈，在Fortune 100科技公司数据上无法统计区分成功与不成功的推广消息。通过12名专业销售代表的实地部署验证了框架的有效性，并公开了SDR-Arena和SDR-Bench以支持可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型消除了传统检索排序方法的有界库存约束，能够生成连续的消息变体，但现有个性化基准仅衡量服务同一用户的发送方适应能力，而经典的两方个性化问题（生成的消息能否诱导第三方采取预期行动）仅通过无法复现的A/B测试和小规模人工研究进行。缺乏可复现、可扩展的评估基准。

Method: 将Kamenica和Gentzkow（2011）的贝叶斯说服框架适配到生成式智能体，在销售领域实例化。构建SDR-Bench语料库（6,279条客户成功故事，涵盖22个行业约200家企业），采用时间约束模拟防止未来数据泄露。在多个前沿LLM和深度研究智能体上进行评估。

Result: 所有前沿模型均表现出一致的个性化瓶颈；在Fortune 100科技公司队列上，没有任何模型能统计上区分成功与不成功的推广消息。实地部署中，48%的模型生成内容被评为即时有用，高级专家一致性达到Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在经典两方个性化任务中表现有限，存在个性化瓶颈。所提出的框架和基准（SDR-Bench和SDR-Arena）为可复现的生成式个性化研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [106] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCGS是一个通过推断对话中每轮用户意图来提升LLM多轮对话安全性的框架，它将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用基于价值和遗憾的评论家对候选回复进行评分，并证明推理时重加权近似于基础策略的指数倾斜，保证期望回报的提升。在多个基准测试中，DCGS优于强鲁棒基线和前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全框架在多轮对话中采用上下文强盗式处理，忽略了对话轨迹，无法区分用户的真实有害意图与善意但被误解的提问，导致安全性不足或过度防御。

Method: 将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在token和utterance两个层面学习基于价值和遗憾的评论家，通过行动-价值评论家对候选回复进行评分，实现推理时的重加权采样。

Result: 在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench和Harmbench上，DCGS在对抗性对话任务中优于强鲁棒基线和前沿模型，且可迁移到前沿模型上无需微调即可提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DCGS通过推断对话历史中的用户意图来动态调整安全策略，其在理论上保证有限候选池下的期望回报提升，在实践中有效提升了多轮对话的安全性。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [107] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地形式化了不完整提示越狱（IPJ）现象，揭示了LLM在句子补全中对不完整有害提示的脆弱性，并通过神经元层面的分析提出了更鲁棒的防御方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重的LLM虽有安全防护，但句子补全任务仍易被不完整的有害提示利用，现有防御不足，亟需对此类攻击进行系统表征与分析。

Method: 形式化定义IPJ，系统分析不同吸引子类型对不完整句子补全的影响，通过参数调优实验验证防御泛化不足，并识别了两类功能性神经元（终止神经元与延续神经元）用于细粒度控制。

Result: LLM系统性地将拒绝延迟到句子终止；参数调优训练的防御无法跨内容领域和吸引子类型泛化；识别出终止与延续两类功能性神经元。

Conclusion: 神经元层面的干预为更精确和鲁棒的IPJ防御提供了潜在方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [108] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSimpl 是一种基于简化验证的求解器 LLM 框架，通过利用优化求解器生成简化诊断查询，使 LLM 能够在固定全局上下文中局部推理，从而将自然语言问题描述稳健地转化为正确的优化形式化表述，在多个优化基准上相比现有方法持续提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言接口能极大提升优化建模的可访问性和可用性，但现有方法难以确保 LLM 自动推导出的求解器公式化表述正确实现了预期任务，即便其能无错误执行。因此需要一种能保证形式化结果正确性的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出 VeriSimpl 框架，核心思想是基于简化验证（simplification-based verification）：利用优化求解器生成关于候选公式的简化诊断查询，使 LLM 在固定全局上下文下对约束和决策变量等不同维度进行局部推理，从而可判定地验证公式相对于任务描述的正确性。
