For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [216] [OpenForgeRL: Train Harness-native Agents in Any Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21557)
*Xiao Yu,Baolin Peng,Ruize Xu,Hao Zou,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Wenlin Yao,Nikhil Singh,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenForgeRL 是一个开源框架，通过轻量级代理和Kubernetes编排器，实现基于推理框架（如Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw）的智能体端到端训练，在多种复杂环境和基准测试中优于同规模开源基线，并分析了不同框架和强化学习对智能体行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体依赖复杂的推理框架（如Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw）进行多轮推理、工具调用和外部系统访问，但这些框架难以用开源基础设施进行端到端训练，因为标准的SFT/RL栈无法原生表达有状态、多进程的框架推理过程。

Method: 提出OpenForgeRL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级代理，服务于框架的模型调用并将调用记录为训练数据，供标准RL代码库（如veRL）使用；(2) Kubernetes编排器，将每次rollout运行在独立的远程容器中，从而实现任意框架在任意环境中的可扩展训练。通过解耦训练和推理，支持在真实部署框架和环境中直接训练智能体。

Result: 在工具/爪类智能体上，OpenForgeClaw在ClawEval上达到31.7 pass^3和55.9 pass@3，在QwenClawBench上达到33.7。在多模态GUI浏览器和计算机使用智能体上，OpenForgeGUI在OSWorld-Verified上达到37.7，Online-Mind2Web上达到63.0，WebVoyager上达到72.3。在所有基准测试中均优于同规模开源基线，GUI设置中匹配或超越数倍更大的模型。

Conclusion: 不同框架的学习难度差异显著；RL提升了智能体的可靠性，如自我验证、工具覆盖率和完成多步计划的能力，但错误恢复等关键能力仍然薄弱。

Abstract: Modern AI agents rely on elaborate inference harnesses such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw to drive multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and access to external systems. While powerful, these complex harnesses also make agents hard to train end-to-end with open infrastructure, whose SFT/RL stacks cannot natively express stateful, multi-process harness inference. To address this, we present OpenForgeRL, an open-source framework for training harness-based agents end-to-end in diverse environments. OpenForgeRL achieves this with a lightweight proxy that serves the harness's model calls while recording them as training data for a standard RL codebase (e.g., veRL), and a Kubernetes orchestrator that runs each rollout in its own remote container, together enabling training on any harness in any environment at scale. By decoupling training and inference, OpenForgeRL allows researchers to easily train, study, and improve agents directly in the real harnesses and environments they are deployed with. We validate our framework across diverse, complex harnesses and environments, spanning tool/claw-based agents and multimodal GUI browser- and computer-use agents. Using only hundreds to a few thousand tasks, OpenForgeClaw reaches 31.7 pass^3 and 55.9 pass@3 on ClawEval and 33.7 on QwenClawBench. OpenForgeGUI reaches 37.7 on OSWorld-Verified, 63.0 on Online-Mind2Web, and 72.3 on WebVoyager. Both outperform open baselines of similar size on nearly all benchmarks, and in the GUI setting match or surpass models several times larger. Beyond benchmarks, we analyze how harness choice (e.g., ZeroClaw, OpenClaw, Codex) and RL shape agent behavior. We find that some harnesses are substantially harder to learn than others, and that RL improves agentic reliability, such as self-verification, tool coverage, and completing multi-step plans, though critical abilities such as error recovery remain weak.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在社会影响下的判断修正过程，发现其遵循与人类社会心理学相似的三个维度：观点距离、来源归因和联盟结构。研究重新将谄媚行为定义为更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现，并提供了区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性服从的框架，以改善模型在道德重要交互中的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐研究主要专注于减少谄媚行为（sycophancy），但作者认为这过于简化。模型需要学习何时采纳他人观点、何时坚持自身道德判断，因此需要理解更广泛的抵制-服从（resistance-compliance）过程。

Method: 通过三项研究，系统操纵三个社会心理学维度：观点距离（incoming view与模型初始立场之间的差距）、来源归因（观点呈现为模型自身先前判断还是外部来源）、联盟结构（群体压力的大小），观察模型判断修正的模式。

Result: 模型对相近观点更易接受；当观点被呈现为模型自身先前判断时影响更大；对群体压力的响应呈现差异化。这些维度与人类社会心理学中的经典现象平行。

Conclusion: 谄媚行为应被重新理解为更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现形式。该框架为区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性服从提供了原则性基础，有助于在道德重要场景中实现更好的对齐。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


### [218] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究采用共识异常检测框架分析加纳2014-2023年月度疟疾监测数据，识别出时空高度结构化的异常传播模式。阿散蒂和北部地区是主要异常热点区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续性热点。关键发现是异常负担（累计病例数）与异常频率（持续性）之间存在空间差异：塔马利在异常期间负担最高，而异常率最高的区域却集中在阿散蒂各区。异常月份构成一个统计学上显著的独立群体，其病例数（Cohen's d=3.252）和季节性偏差（d>1.2）均远高于正常月份。该框架通过区分疟疾最流行区域与传播行为最异常区域，可加强监测、优先调查并支持针对性控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的疟疾监测仅关注疾病负担（病例总数），但忽略了传播行为的异常模式。作者认为仅凭疟疾负担无法全面反映传播动态，因此需要一种能够区分"高负担"与"高异常"的方法，以更好地指导监测和干预资源分配。

Method: 对加纳2014-2023年的月度疟疾监测数据应用共识异常检测框架，识别时空异常传播模式，并分别量化异常负担（累积病例数）和异常频率（异常行为持续性），通过Cohen's d统计量比较异常月份与正常月份的差异。

Result: 异常在时空上高度结构化；阿散蒂和北部地区是最主要的异常区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续性热点。异常负担与异常频率存在空间分离：塔马利在异常期间负担最高，而异常率最高值集中在阿散蒂各区。异常月份与正常月份在病例数（d=3.252）和季节性偏差（d>1.2）上存在极大且统计显著的差异。

Conclusion: 疟疾负担本身不足以全面反映传播动态。通过区分疟疾最流行地区与传播行为最异常地区，该共识异常检测框架能够加强疟疾监测、优先调查异常区域并支持更具针对性的控制策略。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [DataPrep-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs as Training Data Preparators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20465)
*Hao Liang,Qifeng Cai,Yibo Lin,Jianzhuo Du,Qifeng Xia,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Meiyi Qiang,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Bohan Zeng,Ruichuan An,Conghui He,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DataPrep-Bench，首个统一基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在数据构建和数据质量评估两种互补能力上的表现。同时发布了 Data-Construction-Skill 智能体（在金融领域提升近20个点）和 Distributional Alignment Score (DAS) 评估方法（在四个领域取得最佳跨模型相关性），为数据准备提供了统一的下游训练效用评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据质量从根本上决定了LLM的能力，但缺乏统一的基准来端到端衡量LLM、智能体以及数据中心工作流如何实际制备训练数据。

Method: 提出DataPrep-Bench统一基准，将LLM驱动的数据准备拆解为数据构建（将原始数据转换为监督训练数据）和数据质量评估（预测数据集的下游训练价值）两种能力，在六个领域和多个基座模型上进行共享下游训练的评估协议。同时提出Data-Construction-Skill智能体（基于技能引导）和Distributional Alignment Score (DAS)评估方法（基于MMD分布对齐）。

Result: Data-Construction-Skill在Llama-3.1-8B金融领域超越Dolly-only基线近20个绝对点；DAS在六个领域中的四个取得最强跨模型相关性，是唯一同时在数学、科学和医学领域相关性超过0.70的指标，优于现有基于质量、多样性和启发式的评估方法。

Conclusion: DataPrep-Bench提供了统一的下游训练评估框架，将数据构建和数据质量评估作为LLM数据准备中同等重要的两个目标进行衡量。

Abstract: The quality of training data fundamentally determines the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet no unified benchmark exists to measure how well LLMs, agents, and data-centric workflows actually prepare training data end to end. We view LLM-driven data preparation as comprising two complementary capabilities: data construction, which transforms raw sources into supervised training data, and data quality evaluation, which predicts the training value of candidate datasets before downstream training; throughout, "quality" refers to downstream training utility rather than surface-level textual properties. We introduce DataPrep-Bench, the first unified benchmark that jointly evaluates both capabilities under a shared downstream-grounded protocol over six domains and multiple base models. For data construction, methods consume identical raw sources and are scored by fine-tuning a base model on their outputs jointly with Dolly-15k; alongside this track we release Data-Construction-Skill, a skill-guided agent that lifts the Dolly-only baseline by nearly 20 points absolute on Llama-3.1-8B Finance and is competitive with the strongest agent- and DataFlow-based methods in knowledge-extraction-dense domains. For data quality evaluation, scoring functions are scored by Pearson correlation with downstream performance on a shared candidate pool; we release the Distributional Alignment Score (DAS), a distribution-based evaluator that uses MMD between a candidate dataset and a domain proxy. DAS attains the strongest cross-model correlation in four of six domains and is the only metric clearing r > 0.70 simultaneously in Math, Science, and Medical, outperforming existing quality-, diversity-, and heuristic-based evaluators. DataPrep-Bench provides a unified, downstream-grounded framework for measuring progress on both capabilities as co-equal targets of LLM-driven data preparation.

</details>


### [220] [PhantomFill: When the Form Demands an Answer, Language Models Invent One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20492)
*Rana Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了一个普遍但此前未被充分量化的AI安全问题：当语言模型被强制填入JSON字段、函数参数等结构化表单时，即使面对无法回答的问题，模型也会系统性地编造答案。通过控制变量实验（仅改变输出格式），研究发现GPT-5.5等模型在自由文本中诚实回答，但在必填JSON字段下100%编造。即使是显式指令"不要推断情感"也被模式覆盖。论文发布了PhantomFill基准测试，并提出了一个单行schema的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评估主要关注自然文本生成质量，但生产环境中模型实际是以结构化表单（JSON字段、函数参数等）的方式输出。研究者观察到格式本身会导致模型产生幻觉，但这一现象缺乏系统性量化。他们希望揭示格式约束对模型诚实性的影响，并建立可复现的基准测试。

Method: 论文设计了控制变量实验：对同一问题、同一输入，仅改变答案格式（自由文本 vs. 必填JSON字段）。构建了无法回答的输入场景（如无回复的帖子、未转录的客服工单）。测试了13个不同规模和开源程度的模型，并引入"证据不足"选项和显式不推断指令作为对照条件。最后发布了PhantomFill基准测试，包含两个指标：强制编造率（Coerced Fabrication Rate）和逃脱利用率（Escape Utilization Rate）。

Result: 1）GPT-5.5在自由文本中98%诚实回答，在必填JSON字段下40/40次全部编造；2）必填字段导致10/13个模型编造率100%；3）"证据不足"选项仅被闭源前沿模型利用，9个开源模型完全忽略；4）显式指令"不要推断情感"在4/6个模型中被schema覆盖；5）抵抗能力与模型规模呈非单调关系（小模型拒绝→中模型编造→大模型再次拒绝）。

Conclusion: 格式压力下的诚实性是当前未被评估的训练结果。编造恰好发生在无法规避的地方（必填枚举、最小长度数组）。论文提供了简单修复方案（单行schema）和量化指标（PhantomFill）。强调这是一个系统性漏洞，而非个别模型的问题。

Abstract: Language models in production do not write prose. They fill forms: JSON fields, function arguments, extraction templates. We show that the form itself causes hallucination.
  We ask thirteen models the same question about the same input and change only the answer format. The inputs are built so the question cannot be answered: a viral post showing 12,400 likes but no visible replies, a support ticket whose call was never transcribed. In free text, GPT-5.5 answers honestly. It says there is no reply data, 98% of the time. Given a required JSON field for sentiment, the same model invents an answer 40 times out of 40. It fabricates the mood of crowds it never saw and quotes customers it never heard.
  The pattern holds with force. Required fields drive fabrication to 100% in ten of thirteen models. An explicit "insufficient evidence" option rescues only the frontier: all nine open-weight models ignore it. A direct instruction, do not infer sentiment, is overridden by the schema in four of six models. Resistance does not come with scale: within a single model family, the smallest model refuses, the mid-sized model fabricates, the largest refuses again. Honesty under format pressure is a training outcome that no one is measuring.
  The fabrication hides exactly where hedging is impossible: in required enums and minimum-count arrays, fields where no disclaimer fits. We release PhantomFill, a benchmark with deterministic scoring and two reportable numbers: the Coerced Fabrication Rate and the Escape Utilization Rate. The fix we test is one line of schema. The failure we measure is everywhere.

</details>


### [221] [The Active Ingredient in Muon's Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20512)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统解耦了Muon优化器在模算术上快速实现grokking的机制，发现核心是动量正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代）而非谱范数约束；正交化优化器以更低谱范数找到泛化解，且Newton-Schulz迭代次数存在速度与稳定性的权衡；此外谱缩放可被移除而不影响性能。研究还提出稳定性感知指标来避免"更快"的误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将Muon优化器在模算术上比AdamW更快达到grokking阈值归因于"谱范数约束加正交化动量"，但未隔离具体哪种机制起关键作用。本文旨在分解并压力测试各组件的影响，以理解Muon行为背后的真正原因。

Method: 进行多种子和多学习率的网格搜索，通过消融实验（正交化only vs 谱范数only vs 完整Muon）、机制分析（比较谱范数、嵌入移动量）、以及改变Newton-Schulz迭代次数（1到5次）来分解各组件的贡献，并引入稳定性感知指标（首次穿越时间 vs 持续泛化时间）重新评估提速效果。

Result: (1) 正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代）是加速的关键，谱范数约束无额外加速且不可靠；(2) 正交化优化器在约3倍更低的谱范数下找到泛化解，且是通过找到更低范数的解空间而非单纯减少嵌入扰动；(3) 减少Newton-Schulz迭代次数加速了首次泛化但使解脆弱，易发生瞬态塌缩，高学习率下更严重；5次迭代是鲁棒性最优的选择；(4) 谱缩放可被移除且无性能损失；(5) 在稳定性感知指标下，"更快"的结论可能反转。

Conclusion: Muon在模算术grokking上的加速完全来自动量正交化，而非谱范数约束；正交化帮助优化器找到更低谱范数的泛化解；Newton-Schulz迭代次数需在速度与稳定性间权衡，5次为推荐设置；谱缩放是冗余组件；评估grokking加速时应采用稳定性感知指标。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer reaches the grokking threshold on modular arithmetic faster than AdamW. Prior work attributes this to "spectral-norm constraints plus orthogonalized momentum" but does not isolate which mechanism matters. To better understand Moun's behavior, we run multi-seed and multi-learning-rate sweeps to decompose and stress-test the effect. First, an ablation shows the speedup comes from orthogonalization (the Newton-Schulz iteration): orthogonalize-only matches full Muon, whereas spectral-only is no faster than AdamW and is unreliable, and this verdict holds across learning rates. Second, a mechanistic analysis finds that orthogonalizing optimizers reach generalization at roughly 3x lower spectral norm and, controlling for how much the embedding actually moves, settle into a lower-norm solution rather than simply perturbing the embedding less. Third, reducing the Newton-Schulz iteration count from five to one accelerates reaching the threshold but makes the grokked solution fragile, prone to transient collapse, with fragility that grows with learning rate; a single iteration is fast and stable only at small learning rate, while the canonical five iterations are the learning-rate-robust choice. We also show spectral scaling can be dropped at no measured cost. A methodological thread runs throughout: under a stability-aware metric, "faster" claims about grokking optimizers can invert, so we report both first-crossing and sustained-grok times. To support reproducibility, we release our full training and analysis code at https://github.com/louiswang524/muon-grokking-frontier

</details>


### [222] [Scaling Closed-Loop Feature Channel Configuration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20516)
*Tolgay Atinc Uzun,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在更大规模（每个微调周期250个候选网络，共2000个生成候选，462个验证评估）上验证了基于大语言模型的闭环通道配置搜索方法，发现平均准确率呈正向线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4, p=0.043），最佳准确率从0.3144提升至0.3676，参数效率显著改善（最佳模型11.8M参数 vs 早期166.5M参数），并揭示了非2的幂通道宽度（41.8%）及中等早期宽度+扩展中后期块的结构化分配模式等架构规律性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证初始闭环大语言模型通道配置搜索方法在更密集采样下的优化行为是否可迁移，以及更多生成网络评估能否揭示额外的架构规律性。

Method: 将搜索扩展到每个微调周期250个候选网络，共8个完整周期2000个生成候选，经任务与元数据过滤后得到462个CIFAR-100验证评估，分析平均准确率趋势、性能前沿、参数效率及架构模式。

Result: 每周期平均准确率正向线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4, p=0.043）；最佳准确率从0.3144提升至0.3676；参数效率大幅提升（11.8M vs 166.5M）；41.8%的候选采用非2的幂通道宽度；最强模型呈现中等早期宽度与扩展中后期块的结构化分配模式。

Conclusion: 初始研究的通道搜索信号在密集采样下可迁移，大规模搜索揭示了更清晰的优化趋势和架构规律性。

Abstract: Promising initial results in closed-loop large-language-model-based channel-configuration search demonstrated that neural-network widths can be optimized directly through executable code generation and accuracy feedback. However, those results were obtained from a relatively sparse set of valid evaluations, leaving open whether the observed optimization behavior transfers to a denser sampling regime and whether additional architectural regularities emerge when more generated networks are evaluated. To test this, the same search setting is scaled to 250 candidate networks per fine-tuning cycle. The analysis covers 2000 generated candidates from 8 complete cycles, yielding 462 verified CIFAR-100 evaluations after task and metadata filtering. Per-cycle mean accuracy exhibits a positive linear trend with slope 9.87e-4 (p=0.043), while the high-performing frontier improves more strongly: the best observed accuracy increases from 0.3144 to 0.3676, and both the top-5 and top-10 cycle-level means exhibit positive trends. The scaled run also reveals improved parameter efficiency. The best model reaches 0.3676 with 11.8M parameters, compared with an early high-performing model at 0.3144 with 166.5M parameters. Beyond accuracy, the larger sample exposes architectural regularities that were difficult to assess from sparse observations. Non-power-of-two channel widths occur in 41.8% of verified candidates, and the strongest models share structured channel-allocation patterns characterized by moderate early widths and expanded middle or later blocks. These findings indicate that the channel-search signal observed in the initial study transfers

</details>


### [223] [Multimodal CoLRAG-TF: Triple-Filtered Retrieval for Complex PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20517)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Multimodal CoLRAG-TF，一种四轴融合架构，结合密集文本嵌入、BM25关键词匹配、知识图谱三元组过滤和图像相似性，用于异构PDF文档的检索增强生成。在43份日语灾害教训PDF（2,403个块）上构建多模态索引，贝叶斯优化显示三元组轴权重最大（α=0.44），在457对基准上达到0.9909的检索召回率，多跳答案相似度较单跳提升71.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构PDF集合的检索增强生成面临多模态内容、领域专业术语以及需要跨分散证据进行多跳推理的挑战，现有方法难以有效处理这些问题。

Method: 提出四轴融合架构，集成密集文本嵌入、BM25关键词匹配、知识图谱三元组过滤和图像相似性；构建混合OCR流水线和大语言模型生成标题；提取11,414个OpenIE三元组并用FAISS索引；采用HippoRAG2启发的粗到细检索器（卷→章→块）缩小搜索空间；通过贝叶斯优化确定融合权重。

Result: 在457对基准上，检索召回率达0.9909，多跳答案相似度较单跳提升71.6%；三元组轴权重最优为0.44；图像到教训流水线验证了视觉输入的适用性。

Conclusion: 三元组过滤的多模态融合对于嘈杂、异构PDF的结构化推理至关重要，该框架可推广到灾害领域之外。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over heterogeneous PDF collections remains challenging due to multimodal content, domain-specific terminology, and the need for multi-hop reasoning across dispersed evidence. We present Multimodal CoLRAG-TF, a four-axis fusion architecture that integrates dense text embeddings, BM25 keyword matching, knowledge-graph triple filtering, and image-based similarity for robust retrieval over complex documents. Our system constructs a multimodal index of 2,403 blocks extracted from 43 Japanese disaster lesson PDFs, supported by a hybrid OCR pipeline and LLM-based caption generation. To enhance compositional reasoning, we extract 11,414 OpenIE triples and index them with FAISS, enabling sub-second triple lookup and hierarchical propagation of relevance signals. A HippoRAG2-inspired coarse-to-fine retriever (volume $\to$ chapter $\to$ block) narrows the search space before final fusion scoring. Bayesian optimization over fusion weights reveals that the triple axis must dominate ($α_\text{triple} = 0.44$) to counteract lexical bias and sustain multi-hop retrieval quality. Evaluated on a 457-pair benchmark, Multimodal CoLRAG-TF achieves a Retrieval Recall of 0.9909 and a 71.6$\%$ improvement in multi-hop answer similarity over single-hop queries. An image-to-lesson pipeline using a vision LLM further demonstrates the applicability of the approach to visual inputs. These results show that triple-filtered multimodal fusion is essential for structured reasoning over noisy, heterogeneous PDFs and provides a general framework applicable beyond the disaster domain.

</details>


### [224] [Adaptive Depth in Looped Transformers: Diagnosing Learned Halting Gates and Trajectory Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20519)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环Transformer通过共享循环块重复计算来增加测试时计算量。本文研究了循环Transformer中自适应深度的问题，发现将出口选择与轨迹形成纠缠会导致自适应计算性能不佳。通过固定先验深度监督和事后置信度读出头，可以解耦这两个过程，并指出性能瓶颈主要来自联合门控训练诱导的轨迹而非门控表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer中学习到的终止目标通常将单个出口分布同时用作推理时的停止规则和训练时各深度损失的权重，这导致出口选择与轨迹形成相互纠缠：门控不仅选择使用哪个循环状态，还决定每个中间状态的监督强度。因此，自适应计算性能的低下可能源于读出头、诱导轨迹或两者的交互作用，但以往工作往往忽略了这一区别。

Method: 通过轨迹-读出头视角系统研究循环Transformer的自适应深度问题，在受控合成任务（模算术和二进制奇偶校验）和大规模Ouro-1.4B和2.6B检查点上进行实验。比较了固定先验深度监督、事后置信度读出头以及学习的线性/MLP门控，并在冻结轨迹上拟合门控以定位失败原因。

Result: 固定先验深度监督能产生具有难度感知的轨迹，其中间状态暴露有用的停止信号；简单的事后置信度读出头通常匹配或超越学习的线性和MLP门控。在冻结轨迹上拟合门控表明失败主要源于联合门控训练诱导的轨迹，而非门控表达能力的限制。在Ouro评估中，预训练的ponder门控具有竞争力但并非均匀帕累托最优，测量的延迟证实平均出口深度的减少转化为实际的推理时间节省。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer的自适应深度应被重新定义为轨迹形成和出口读出的联合问题，而不仅仅是门控学习问题。这一诊断性评估凸显了先前学习终止工作往往隐含忽略的区别。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase test-time computation by repeatedly applying a shared recurrent block. Learned halting objectives in looped Transformers typically use a single exit distribution both as the inference-time stopping rule and as the training-time weighting of per-depth losses. This entangles exit selection with trajectory formation: the gate not only chooses which recurrent state to use, but also determines how strongly each intermediate state is supervised. Consequently, poor adaptive-compute performance can arise from the readout, the induced trajectory, or their interaction. We study adaptive depth in looped Transformers through this trajectory--readout lens, across controlled synthetic tasks (modular arithmetic and binary parity) and large-scale Ouro-1.4B and 2.6B checkpoints. We find that fixed-prior depth supervision, which shapes the trajectory without an input-dependent halting policy, produces difficulty-aware trajectories whose intermediate states expose useful stopping signals, and that simple post-hoc confidence readouts often match or outperform learned linear and MLP gates. Fitting gates on frozen trajectories localizes the failure: it appears to stem mainly from the trajectory induced by joint gate training rather than from limited gate expressivity. The same pattern is present in Ouro evaluations, where pretrained ponder gates are competitive but not uniformly Pareto-optimal, and measured latency confirms that the resulting reductions in average exit depth translate into practical inference-time savings. Our systematic diagnostic evaluation reframes adaptive depth in looped Transformers as a joint problem of trajectory formation and exit readout, rather than gate learning alone, highlighting a distinction that prior learned-halting work has often left implicit.

</details>


### [225] [Generative Bayesian Filtering for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20521)
*Lei Cao,Sihang Feng,Jixin Yan,Tao Sun,Naichen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成贝叶斯滤波（GBF）框架，用预训练的条件变分自编码器（CVAE）替代传统线性-高斯观测模型，将测量更新转化为基于分数的后验采样问题，在合成数据和实际工业及医疗应用中提升了状态估计精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滤波方法（如卡尔曼滤波）依赖线性-高斯等简单观测模型，无法刻画高维传感器信号中日益非线性和异构的模式，亟需更具表达能力的滤波框架。

Method: GBF框架用预训练的CVAE作为条件生成模型替代传统观测模型；在线推理时执行贝叶斯预测-更新递归，将测量更新表述为结合动力学先验与CVAE诱导似然的 posterior 采样问题，进而转化为基于分数的采样问题。

Result: 在合成数据集、制造系统监测和心律失常诊断等实际应用中，GBF相比基线方法在状态估计精度和鲁棒性上均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: GBF框架通过生成模型与分数采样的结合，既继承了生成模型的灵活性，又保留了集成的不确定性量化能力，为高维非线性动态系统的滤波问题提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: The state of a dynamic system evolves over time, switching among several latent modes that govern its observable behavior. Filtering methods infer the latent state from observations. Classical filtering approaches, including Kalman filters, typically rely on simple observation models, such as linear-Gaussian models, that are incapable of characterizing the increasingly nonlinear and heterogeneous patterns in high-dimensional sensor signals. To tackle the challenge, we propose Generative Bayesian Filtering (GBF), a filtering framework that replaces restrictive observation models with pretrained conditional generative models parametrized by conditional variational autoencoders (CVAE). For online inference, GBF performs a Bayesian prediction-update recursion in which the measurement update is formulated as a posterior sampling problem that combines the dynamical prior with the CVAE-induced likelihood. The resulting filtering problem is then transformed into a score-based sampling problem, which naturally inherits the flexibility from generative models and the uncertainty quantification capabilities from ensembling. Experiments on synthetic datasets and real-world applications involving manufacturing system monitoring and arrhythmia diagnosis demonstrate that GBF improves state estimation accuracy and robustness relative to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [226] [Do Active SAE Feature Planes Carry More Holonomy? A Preregistered Reversal in Gemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20522)
*Larry Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册实验检验了Gemma 2 2B模型中holonomy是否集中于激活的稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征平面，结果否定了该预测：激活特征平面比混合特征对照平面携带更少的holonomy（调整后对数对比度-0.29439，95%区间[-0.43989, -0.14889]）。纯幅度解释不成立，三向排序因共同支持缺失而无法定义。后续诊断支持面积律，但有界匹配中心位移，并识别出传输畸变为潜在机制或混淆因素。结论为窄范围的、可审计的操作性反转，而非因果性断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"语义集中性"假设的一个具体操作性版本：模型表示空间中的holonomy（沿小环路平行输运后的旋转）是否集中于激活的SAE特征平面，从而验证语义特征是否具有几何上的特殊性。

Method: 在Gemma 2 2B模型的最后一层（layer-12到layer-13）残差流读出头测量holonomy：使用仪器的受限雅可比输运规则携带局部坐标系绕小环路，将所得旋转按包围面积归一化。设计、实质性阈值、分析和判定规则均在观测测量数据前预注册并冻结。对比激活特征平面与混合特征对照平面。

Result: 激活特征平面携带的holonomy少于混合特征对照平面，调整后对数对比度-0.29439，95%区间[-0.43989, -0.14889]。纯幅度解释不被支持。三向排序（随机、混合特征、激活特征平面）因共同支持缺失而无法在匹配幅度下定义。后续诊断在小型验证子集上支持面积律，发现传输畸变是可能的机制或混淆因素。

Conclusion: 该结果是窄范围的、可审计的操作性反转，不构成"意义抑制holonomy"的因果性断言。原因仍开放，备选解释包括激活强度几何、特征参与度、字典几何、匹配中心位移、激活流形邻近性和传输剪切等。

Abstract: This paper tests whether holonomy concentrates on active sparse-autoencoder (SAE) feature planes in Gemma 2 2B, a concrete operationalization of the broader semantic-concentration prediction. Holonomy is measured at the final-token layer-12 to layer-13 residual-stream readout by carrying a local frame around small loops using the instrument's restricted-Jacobian transport rule, then normalizing the resulting rotation by enclosed area. The design, materiality threshold, analysis, and verdict rules were preregistered and frozen before the analysed measurements were inspected. The prediction was falsified in reverse: active-feature planes carried less holonomy than matched mixed-feature controls, with an adjusted log contrast of -0.29439 and 95% interval [-0.43989, -0.14889]. A magnitude-only explanation was not supported in this design, while the three-way ordering across random, mixed-feature, and active-feature planes was undefined at matched magnitude because common support failed. Post-freeze diagnostics at the same readout supported the area law on a small validation subset, bounded matched-center displacement under a simple paired regression, and identified transport distortion as a live mechanism or confound. The result is therefore a narrow, auditable operational reversal, not a causal claim that meaning suppresses holonomy. The cause remains open, with activation-strength geometry, degree of feature engagement, dictionary geometry, matched-center displacement, activation-manifold proximity, and transport shear as live alternatives.

</details>


### [227] [Uncertainty-Aware Trust Estimation for Multi-LLM Systems via Structured Expert Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20529)
*Jiawei Zheng,Jiazhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多LLM聚合问题形式化为不确定性感知的信任估计问题，借鉴决策理论中的结构化专家判断方法，采用Cooke风格的对数加权机制，根据专家在上下文校准问题上的概率预测质量来估计其可靠性。实验表明，在异质性和污染场景下，该方法相比传统聚合方法能实现更优的准确率-可靠性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成聚合方法假设所有模型同样可信，忽略了不同LLM间不确定性的质量差异，这在能力差异显著的异质LLM场景下会导致聚合结果易受不可靠或对抗性专家影响。

Method: 将多LLM聚合建模为不确定性感知的信任估计问题，引入决策理论中的结构化专家判断方法，通过上下文感知的校准问题评估专家的概率预测质量，采用Cooke风格的对数加权机制——惩罚过度自信的错误预测并奖励校准良好的专家。

Result: 在同质专家面板场景下各聚合方法表现相似；但在异质性和污染场景下，Cooke加权方法显著优于其他方法，实现了更优的准确率-可靠性平衡，且在引入不可靠专家时保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多LLM聚合不仅需要组合预测，更需要在不确性条件下校准信任。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles are increasingly used to improve reliability by combining predictions from multiple LLMs. However, existing aggregation methods typically assume that all models are equally trustworthy, overlooking differences in uncertainty quality. This assumption is poorly suited to heterogeneous LLMs, whose reliability and capability vary significantly, making naive aggregation vulnerable to unreliable or adversarial experts. In this work, we formulate multi-LLM aggregation as a problem of uncertainty-aware trust estimation. We adapt structured expert judgment from decision theory, using context-aware calibration questions to estimate expert reliability based on the quality of its probabilistic predictions. Specifically, we employ Cooke-style log weighting, which penalises overconfident incorrect predictions and favours well-calibrated experts. We evaluate our approach on MMLU and MMLU-Pro across homogeneous, heterogeneous, and contaminated expert panels. Results show that while aggregation methods perform similarly in homogeneous settings, Cooke weighting becomes critical under heterogeneity and contamination. It achieves a superior accuracy-reliability balance and remains robust when unreliable experts are introduced. These findings suggest that Multi-LLM aggregation requires not just combining predictions, but calibrating trust under uncertainty.

</details>


### [228] [CLOE: Christoffel Loss Autoencoder for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20530)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLOE 是一种结合自编码器和 Christoffel 函数的半监督异常检测方法，通过新颖的损失函数引导表示学习，在高维表格数据上实现了优越性能，同时保持了轻量级和低超参数调优的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel 函数方法因简洁性和扎实的理论基础而具有吸引力，但可扩展性差，难以处理高维数据。现有轻量级方法也常需多个超参数调优，难以应对高维场景。

Method: 将自编码器用于降维，在潜空间应用 Christoffel 函数检测器；设计基于 Christoffel 函数的新型损失函数引导自编码器学习更贴合正常数据分布支撑的表示；提出基于原理的检测阈值设定方法和剩余超参数的高效调优策略。

Result: 在多个高维表格异常检测基准上，CLOE 相比现有方法取得了更优越的性能。

Conclusion: CLOE 成功将 Christoffel 函数的优势扩展到高维场景，同时保留了其轻量级和低超参数调优的优良特性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised anomaly detection plays a key role in diverse fields such as process monitoring, healthcare, and finance. However, lightweight methods often struggle with high-dimensional data and typically require careful tuning of multiple hyperparameters. Among existing approaches, Christoffel Function--based methods are attractive due to their simplicity, requiring at most a single hyperparameter. They also benefit from a well-established theoretical foundation that yields several interesting results for data science. However, their main limitation is poor scalability to high-dimensional settings. In this paper, we introduce CLOE, a new method that combines an autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a Christoffel Function--based detector applied in the latent space. To better align representation learning with anomaly detection, we design a novel loss function that leverages the Christoffel Function to guide the autoencoder toward representations that better capture the support of the normal data distribution. We further propose a principled procedure to set the detection threshold and an efficient strategy to tune the single remaining hyperparameter. Experiments on multiple high-dimensional tabular anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that CLOE achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, while preserving the lightweight and low-tuning advantages of Christoffel Function--based approaches.

</details>


### [229] [Position: Stop Reactively Patching Your Model Every Time and Start Proactive Test-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20532)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Jialiang Wang,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张将AI系统的维护从反应式飞轮（仅基于用户错误反馈进行模型修补）升级为主动式测试驱动飞轮，通过构建"测试空间"将反馈数据映射到任务目标，从而更有效地处理开放世界的长尾分布问题，数学证明表明主动式飞轮在长期扩展中迭代次数更少、效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反应式AI飞轮机制仅被动观察用户行为中的错误并进行修补，忽略了错误的系统目标上下文，无法预判未来的边缘情况，导致不必要的迭代增多；同时开放世界长尾分布使得收集剩余错误在统计上越来越困难，亟需一种更主动的维护机制。

Method: 提出主动式测试驱动飞轮，核心是构建一个"测试空间"（test space），将用户反馈数据系统性地映射到任务目标上；并通过数学证明对比两种飞轮在长期扩展中的性能差异。

Result: 数学证明表明，主动式飞轮相比反应式飞轮能够在更少的迭代次数下实现更好的长期扩展性能。

Conclusion: 为了构建更具泛化能力的AI系统，应当从反应式维护范式转向主动式测试驱动维护范式，以减少不必要的飞轮迭代并更高效地处理开放世界的长尾错误分布。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems are designed to operate under diverse, open-ended, use-cases. To help generalize deployed systems, many deployed-system maintenance pipelines use a reactive AI flywheel that observes emerging feedback from user behavior (errors) and patches the model accordingly. However, when used as the primary maintenance mechanism, these flywheels often ignore the broader context of these errors within the system's objectives, failing to preempt potential future edge cases, which leads to more unnecessary flywheel iterations. Also, it is statistically increasingly difficult to collect remaining errors due to the long-tail nature of open-world use-cases. This position paper argues that a proactive test-driven flywheel is required to address reactive flywheel's limitations and to approach a generalizable system. We advocate for creating a "test space" to technically map feedback data to task objectives, evolving the flywheel from reactive to proactive. We augment our position by mathematically proving a proactive one achieves better long-term scaling with fewer iterations than the reactive flywheel.

</details>


### [230] [Grounding Investor Views: Neural Predicates in the Black-Litterman Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20533)
*Marcos Florencio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经谓词的形式化方法，用于在Black-Litterman模型中自动生成结构化、可解释的投资观点，将金融分析数据通过组合层次神经谓词映射到观点矩阵、收益向量和不确定性矩阵，实现端到端可微学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Litterman模型要求投资者主观指定资产收益观点及不确定性估计，这一过程主观性强且难以规模化扩展。

Method: 构建组合层次的神经谓词，处理结构化金融分析数据；神经谓词输出市场立场的概率分布，映射到Black-Litterman模型的P矩阵、q向量和Ω矩阵；观点置信度从谓词输出分布推导，替代主观不确定性评估。

Result: 模型具有可解释性（任何投资组合权重可回溯至底层数据）和完全可微性（支持端到端学习）。

Conclusion: 该方法为Black-Litterman模型提供了一种数据驱动的观点生成替代方案，兼具可解释性与可学习性。

Abstract: Portfolio construction under the Black-Litterman model requires investors to specify views on asset returns alongside explicit uncertainty estimates -- a process that remains largely subjective and difficult to scale. We propose a formal approach in which neural predicates serve as a structured, probabilistic mechanism for view generation. In our formulation, structured financial analysis data is processed through a compositional hierarchy of neural predicates whose outputs -- probability distributions over market stances -- are mapped to the pick matrix $\mathbf{P}$, the view return vector $\mathbf{q}$, and the view uncertainty matrix $\boldsymbolΩ$ of the Black-Litterman model. View confidence is derived from predicate output distributions, providing a data-driven alternative to subjective uncertainty elicitation. The resulting approach is interpretable, in the sense that any portfolio weight can be traced back through the predicate's logical chain to the underlying data, and fully differentiable, enabling end-to-end learning.

</details>


### [231] [A Graph Neural Network approach to zero-shot Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20535)
*Alicia Tierz,Icíar Alfaro,David González,Elías Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本数字孪生框架，通过热力学信息图神经网络（TIGN）与实时视觉感知耦合，在无需重新训练的情况下，实现对任意几何域上粘弹性梁大变形和粘性流体非线性晃荡的物理精确仿真，延迟约25ms/帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测性数字孪生几何刚性过强，当物理域或边界条件发生变化时需要大量重新训练或微调，缺乏跨几何泛化能力。

Method: 1）热力学信息图神经网络（TIGN）作为几何无关的物理信息推理引擎，基于度量-热力学形式化方法，通过图消息传递局部强制执行能量守恒和非负熵产生；2）辅助GNN从稀疏初始视觉边界推断不可观测场（如应力张量、速度和能量分布）；3）闭环数据同化机制：深度分割网络+稀疏光流实时跟踪宏观变形和自由流体边界，动态校正自回归仿真滚动，消除数值漂移。

Result: 在两个截然不同的物理场景（粘弹性梁大变形的固体力学、粘性流体非线性晃荡的流体力学）中，统一框架在未见过的几何上生成了物理精确的仿真，无需特定案例重新训练，延迟约25ms/帧，支持增强现实直接投影潜在机械变量。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了零样本数字孪生框架具备跨物理域、跨几何的极端泛化能力，为实时、几何无关的物理仿真提供了有效范式，弥合了仿真到真实之间的差距。

Abstract: Traditional Predictive Digital Twins often remain geometrically rigid, requiring extensive retraining or fine-tuning whenever the underlying physical domain or boundary conditions change. To overcome this limitation, we present a novel framework for \textit{Zero-Shot Digital Twins} that seamlessly couples real-time visual perception with a geometry-agnostic, physics-informed reasoning engine. At the core of our architecture is the Thermodynamics-Informed Graph Neural Network architecture, a Geometric Deep Learning solver grounded in a metriplectic thermodynamic formalism that enforces energy conservation and non-negative entropy production locally through graph message passing. The framework integrates an auxiliary Graph Neural Network to infer unobservable fields (such as stress tensors or velocity and energy distributions) directly from sparse initial visual boundaries, mitigating numerical start-up transients. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a continuous closed-loop data assimilation mechanism; the pipeline tracks macroscopic deformations and free-surface fluid boundaries in real-time using deep segmentation networks combined with sparse optical flow, dynamically correcting the autoregressive simulation rollout and eliminating numerical drift. To test the validity of our approach, we demonstrate the extreme generalization capabilities of our approach across two disparate physical regimes: the large deformations of a viscoelastic beam and the non-linear sloshing of a viscous fluid. In both scenarios, the unified framework instantiates physically accurate simulations on novel, unseen geometries without case-specific retraining, operating well within real-time latency budgets (approximately 25 ms per frame) and enabling the direct projection of latent mechanical variables via Augmented Reality.

</details>


### [232] [ReliableTableQA:How Much Supervision Does Reliability Annotation Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20537)
*Huei-Chung Hu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Koyo Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReliableTableQA框架，用于训练LLM标注表格QA结果的统计可靠性（而非可回答性）。贡献包括十类别可靠性分类法（R1-R10）、程序优先的数据流水线生成5万标注样本、以及SFT与GRPO的对比研究。发现小规模SFT即可达到极高性能（Rel-F1从0.61提升至0.98，UCAR归零），而GRPO仅在SFT训练不足时有微弱提升，在SFT充分时无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实企业分析中，语法正确的SQL查询可能因样本量过小、置信区间过宽或混杂因素过多而返回统计上不可靠的结果。现有系统在这些情况下仍自信地回答，导致不可靠的决策支持。需要训练LLM识别这种统计可靠性问题。

Method: (1) 提出十类别可靠性分类法（R1-R10），覆盖小样本聚合、多重比较膨胀、分布尾部不匹配等风险；(2) 构建程序优先的数据流水线，基于公开零售模式的上下文无关文法生成5万个可靠性标注训练样本，并按模式分层划分SFT/GRPO数据；(3) 设计受控实验对比SFT与GRPO的效果。

Result: 200个SFT样本即可将可靠性标注F1从0.61提升到0.98，解析率从0.52提升到1.00，UCAR降至零，并可泛化到未见过的零售领域（H&M上Rel-F1达0.997）。GRPO仅在SFT欠训练（100样本）时有0.06-0.16的增益，SFT充分后无 measurable benefit。

Conclusion: 可靠性标注本质上是一个数据效率问题；强化微调（GRPO）的效果取决于SFT的充分程度，在SFT充足时没有额外收益。这为何时使用以及何时不需要使用强化微调提供了明确的边界条件。

Abstract: We introduce ReliableTableQA, a framework for training an LLM to annotate the statistical reliability of tabular QA results, not whether the query is answerable, but whether the computed answer is statistically meaningful. In real enterprise analytics, a syntactically correct SQL query can return a value that is based on too small a sample, has an excessively wide confidence interval, or is too confounded to support action. Existing systems answer confidently in all such cases, a failure we quantify as the Unreliable Confident Answer Rate (UCAR). We contribute (1) a ten-category reliability taxonomy (R1-R10) covering hazards such as small-sample aggregates, multiple-comparison inflation, and distribution-tail mismatch; (2) a program-first data pipeline that generates 50,000 reliability-labeled training examples from a context-free grammar over public retail schemas, with schema-stratified SFT/GRPO splits; and (3) a controlled study of how much supervision calibrated reliability annotation actually requires. We find that a small, schema-stratified SFT set is remarkably sufficient: 200 examples raise reliability-flag F1 from 0.61 to 0.98 and parse rate from 0.52 to 1.00, drive UCAR to zero, and yield a model that generalizes to an unseen retail domain (Rel-F1 0.997 on held-out H&M). Against this strong SFT baseline, GRPO, commonly assumed to be essential, helps only when SFT is under-trained (+0.06-0.16 exact-flag-set match at 100 examples, in- and out-of-distribution) and provides no measurable benefit once SFT is adequate, a null result we confirm across a hard compound-flag slice, a strict exact-match metric, and out-of-distribution evaluation. Our findings reframe reliability annotation as a data-efficiency problem and delineate precisely when reinforcement fine-tuning does and does not pay off.

</details>


### [233] [Codec-Gauge: Learning Compression-Friendly Gauges for Transformer KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20538)
*Yitao Jiang,Yaoqing Yang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Codec-Gauge，一种后训练缓存坐标变换层，通过学习小型正交通道变换来优化KV缓存压缩的质量，在不改变模型权重和注意力语义的前提下，平均降低zfp KL散度44.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文Transformer推理日益依赖KV缓存压缩或量化，但现有压缩后端对每个键/值向量的通道基的选择敏感，不同坐标表示下压缩保真度差异显著，亟需一种无需改变模型权重即可提升压缩保真度的方法。

Method: 提出Codec-Gauge后训练方法，在现有压缩/量化后端前插入一个可学习的正交通道变换层；设计频域分布目标函数，结合token-通道DCT谱质心损失和平滑率代理，将KV能量集中到低频编解码友好布局中；使用实际字节测量和滚动压缩历史评分进行评估。

Result: 在六种模型、3/4/6 bits/value设置下，Codec-Gauge平均降低zfp KL散度44.0%，优于原始坐标、随机、Hadamard、DCT和PCA/KLT基线；同样提升了block-uniform和KIVI风格量化的质量保持；27B模型和长上下文任务提示验证了质量趋势，串行存储和时间测量验证了压缩缓存路径的有效性。

Conclusion: 缓存坐标几何是一种实用的后训练可调变量，能够在不改动模型权重、注意力语义或后端编码规则的前提下显著提升压缩保真度。

Abstract: Long-context Transformer inference increasingly relies on KV-cache compression or quantization. Prior rotation and transform-coding results suggest that the channel basis of each key/value vector affects how faithfully a fixed backend preserves model behavior. We introduce Codec-Gauge, a post-training cache-coordinate layer that learns small orthogonal channel transforms around existing compression and quantization backends. Its frequency-distribution objective combines a token-channel DCT spectral-centroid loss with a smooth rate proxy to concentrate KV energy in low-frequency codec-facing layouts. We evaluate actual compression and decompression using measured bytes and rolling compressed-history scoring. Across six models at $3$, $4$, and $6$ bits/value, learned gauges reduce zfp KL divergence by $44.0\%$ on average relative to raw coordinates and outperform random, Hadamard, DCT, and PCA/KLT controls. The same gauges improve quality preservation for block-uniform and KIVI-style quantization. Experiments on a 27B model and long-context task prompts reproduce the quality trend, while serial storage and timing measurements validate the implemented compressed-cache paths. These results establish cache-coordinate geometry as a practical post-training variable for improving compression fidelity without changing model weights, attention semantics, or backend coding rules.

</details>


### [234] [Leveraging Biokinetic Knowledge Priors for Data-Scarce Bioprocess Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20539)
*Kyunghoon Hur,Eunjung Jeon,Hyun Woo Kim,Gyubok Lee,Seongjun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了将生物动力学ODE知识注入神经网络的方法，比较了数据级先验（模拟曲线预训练）和架构级先验（将ODE嵌入解码器）两种策略。两者在11个数据集和7种微生物上均优于无先验基线，且可相互替代——用模拟数据预训练的通用解码器能达到全生物结构解码器在真实数据上的效果。这表明模拟预训练是一种简单、数据高效的深度学习方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在药物发现中已发挥作用，但在生物制造领域进展有限，主要原因是生物反应器实验成本高、周期长、数据稀缺。然而该领域拥有丰富的先验知识（如描述微生物生长的生物动力学ODE模型），如何将这些知识注入神经网络尚未被系统研究。

Method: 系统比较了两种将ODE知识注入神经网络的方法：(1) 数据级先验——在模拟ODE曲线上预训练通用解码器；(2) 架构级先验——将ODE直接嵌入解码器结构内部。在11个数据集、7种微生物物种上进行实验验证。

Result: 两种ODE知识注入方法均一致优于无先验基线。核心发现是两者可相互替代：用模拟数据预训练的通用解码器性能匹配全生物结构解码器在真实数据上的表现。

Conclusion: 模拟预训练提供了一种简单、数据高效的深度学习方案，有效应对生物过程数据稀缺问题，为生物制造领域应用深度学习提供了实用途径。

Abstract: While deep learning has accelerated drug discovery, its impact on biomanufacturing has been considerably more limited. The reason is data scarcity. Bioreactor experiments are high-cost, take days to weeks, and are rarely shared in public form, leaving each research work with only a handful of experiments. The domain itself, however, is rich in prior knowledge. Biokinetic ordinary differential equation (ODE) models have described microbial growth for decades, yet how to inject this knowledge into a neural network has not been studied systematically. We present the first systematic study of how to inject this ODE knowledge into a neural network, comparing a data-level prior that pre-trains a generic decoder on simulated ODE curves against an architecture-level prior that embeds the ODE inside the decoder. Both consistently outperform no-prior baselines across 11 datasets and 7 microbial species. Our central finding is that the two are substitutable. A generic decoder pre-trained on simulation matches a fully bio-structured decoder trained on real data. Simulation pre-training therefore offers a simple, data-efficient recipe for deep learning under bioprocess data scarcity.

</details>


### [235] [From Atoms to Entropy: Optimal Noise Allocation for Diffusion Training in the Convex Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20540)
*Luca Ambrogioni,Giulio Franzese,Alberto Foresti,Gabriel Raya,Bac Nguyen,Georgios Batzolis,Yuhta Takida,Naoki Murata,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为扩散模型的噪声调度问题建立了统计框架，证明最优调度是原子性的（集中在有限个噪声水平上），并提出基于生成熵率平方根的代理调度方法，在多个实验场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型训练中的噪声调度主要依赖启发式方法或经验调优，缺乏理论基础。作者希望建立一个通用的统计框架来研究噪声水平分配的最优策略。

Method: 1) 在全耦合机制下，基于凸性或Polyak-Lojasiewicz假设，证明最优训练调度具有原子最小元。2) 在独立学习机制下，引入特征-噪声解耦条件，通过随机矩阵分析得出解耦采样密度与生成熵率平方根成正比。3) 在Dirac混合、低维流形、MNIST等可控设置中验证耦合目标可直接优化的场景。

Result: 1) 优化后的调度始终具有有限支撑集（原子性）。2) 平滑的熵代理（平方根生成熵率）在神经网络模型中紧密跟踪原子最优解，在全耦合参数化情况下失效。3) 在更大规模实验中，平方根熵调度在离散域上显著提升训练效率，在连续图像上与EDM风格启发式方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 本文为扩散模型噪声调度提供了理论基础，证明了最优调度的原子性特征，并提出了一种可扩展的平方根熵代理调度方法，兼顾理论最优性与实际可行性。

Abstract: How should a diffusion model decide which noise levels to train on, and how much? Despite the importance of this choice, current noise schedules are based largely on heuristics or empirical tuning. Here, we develop a general statistical framework for studying asymptotically optimal noise-level allocation in diffusion training. Our first main result concerns the fully coupled regime, where information can spread between different time points. Under convexity or Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type assumptions, we show that the optimized training schedule admits an atomic minimizer, concentrated on finitely many noise levels. Our second main result specializes this framework to an idealized independent-learner regime, intended to model temporal specialization in neural networks. Under an additional feature-noise decoupling condition, a random-matrix analysis leads to an information-theoretic proxy: the decoupled sampling density is proportional to the square root of the generative entropy rate, the rate at which conditional entropy grows along the forward process. We test these predictions in controlled settings where the coupled objective can be optimized directly, including Dirac mixtures, low-dimensional manifolds, and MNIST. In these settings, the optimized schedules are consistently finite-support, while the smooth entropic proxy closely tracks the atomic optimum in neural-network models and breaks down mainly in the fully coupled parametric case, as the theory suggests. We then evaluate the entropic schedule in larger-scale experiments, where full schedule optimization is currently intractable. The results indicate that square-root entropy scheduling can substantially improve training efficiency on discrete domains and remains competitive with standard EDM-style heuristics on continuous images.

</details>


### [236] [HypNO: A Graph-Based Neural Operator with Physics-Informed Message Passing for Hyperbolic Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20541)
*Dimitrije Ždrale,Cassie An Jeng,Katie Wang,Sonia Vanier,Alexandre Bayen,Hossein Nick Zinat Matin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HypNO，一种基于图的神经算子，用于标量双曲守恒律。它直接在有限体积单元的时空图上操作，使用基于邻接分解、物理感知的消息传递来尊重激波附近的迎风格式和熵可容性。在LWR和ARZ交通流模型上验证，能准确预测各种初始条件下的解快照，并捕捉激波和不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算子学习方法在处理同时具有全局传输和激波形成的双曲守恒律问题时面临挑战，需要一种能够尊重物理特性（如迎风格式和熵条件）的神经算子架构。

Method: 构建基于时空图的神经网络，在有限体积单元图上采用邻接分解的物理感知消息传递机制，确保激波附近的迎风格式和熵可容性。

Result: 在LWR和ARZ交通流模型上，HypNO能够准确预测多种初始条件下的解快照，成功捕捉激波和不连续性。

Conclusion: HypNO是一种有效的图神经算子方法，能够处理双曲守恒律中的激波和不连续性问题，为基于学习的偏微分方程求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce HypNO, a graph-based neural operator for scalar hyperbolic conservation laws. HypNO operates directly on a space-time graph of finite-volume cells and uses adjacency-factored, physics-informed message passing to respect upwinding and entropy admissibility near shocks. We benchmark the architecture on the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) and Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic-flow models, a stress test for operator-learning methods because of their simultaneous global transport and shock formation. HypNO predicts solution snapshots accurately across a range of initial conditions while capturing the shocks and discontinuities of the solution.

</details>


### [237] [Improving Access to Essential Medicines via Decision-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20542)
*Angel Tsai-Hsuan Chung,Jatu Abdulai,Patrick Bayoh,Lawrence Sandi,Francis Smart,Hamsa Bastani,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对中低收入国家医疗系统中基本药物分配效率低下的问题，本文提出了一种结合多任务学习和催化先验的决策感知机器学习框架。在与塞拉利昂政府的合作中，该系统在全国范围内分阶段部署作为决策支持工具，使得处理地区分配产品的消费量增加了约19%，随后推广至全国，覆盖约200万妇女和五岁以下儿童。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家（LMICs）的医疗系统面临稀缺资源（特别是基本药物）高效且公平分配的关键挑战，而有限的高质量数据限制了传统数据驱动技术的适用性。

Method: 提出一种决策感知机器学习框架，利用多任务学习确保样本效率，并引入催化先验（catalytic priors）确保分配公平性。该系统与塞拉利昂国家政府合作，在全国范围内分阶段部署作为决策支持工具。

Result: 计量经济学评估显示，处理地区分配产品的消费量增加了约19%。系统随后在全国范围推广，覆盖约200万妇女和五岁以下儿童。

Conclusion: 本文展示了机器学习方法能够在资源受限的全球健康环境中以极低成本提高效率。

Abstract: A critical challenge in healthcare systems in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) is the efficient and equitable allocation of scarce resources, particularly essential medicines. This problem is complicated by limited high-quality data, which restricts the applicability of traditional data-driven techniques. We propose a novel decision-aware machine learning framework for essential medicines allocation, which additionally leverages multi-task learning to ensure sample efficiency and catalytic priors to ensure equitable allocation. In collaboration with the Sierra Leone national government, we performed a staggered, nationwide deployment of our system as a decision support tool. Our econometric evaluation finds an estimated 19% increase in consumption of allocated products in treated districts, demonstrating its efficacy at improving access to essential medicines. Our tool was subsequently scaled nationwide, covering an estimated 2 million women and children under five. Our work demonstrates how machine learning methods can improve efficiency at very low cost in resource-constrained global health settings.

</details>


### [238] [When RLVR Shrinks the Reasoning Boundary: Diagnosing Pass@k Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20543)
*Todd Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLVR训练会提升单次采样准确率，但可能降低多次采样的覆盖率（pass@k反转），原因是边界提示中的罕见正确轨迹在有限采样中被遗漏。本文提出PBA方法，通过锚定基础分布来保留这些轨迹，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示RLVR训练中存在的pass@k反转现象——模型在重复采样下解决的独特问题数量反而少于基础模型，并分析其根本机制：边界提示中罕见正确轨迹因采样不足而消失的"证据缺失"问题。

Method: 提出Per-Problem Base Anchoring (PBA)方法：对存在充足冻结基础模型正确证据的提示进行锐化，对有风险的提示则锚定回基础分布。在Omni-MATH-Test和MATH500上对比GRPO进行实验。

Result: PBA在三个训练种子上同时提升了Pass@1和高预算覆盖率。3000提示的诊断研究一致表明：普通GRPO丢失了基础模型可解的边界提示，而PBA保留了罕见的验证器正向轨迹。

Conclusion: 推理后训练不仅要决定优化强度，还需判断哪些提示是安全的优化对象。pass@k反转风险同样适用于依赖外部工具或验证器的视觉-语言智能体场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can improve one-sample accuracy while making a model worse under repeated sampling. We study this pass@k inversion: after training, the policy may solve fewer distinct problems than its base model at large $k$. The failure concentrates on boundary prompts, where the base model contains rare correct trajectories that are recoverable by sampling but too sparse to reliably appear in finite RLVR rollout groups. We argue that a two-mode account explains this as an absence-of-evidence failure: rare correct trajectories may disappear before RLVR samples and reinforces them often enough. The main contribution is this diagnostic and mechanistic framing. Per-Problem Base Anchoring (PBA) is a deliberately simple proof-of-concept: sharpen prompts with sufficient frozen-base correct evidence, and anchor risky prompts to the base distribution. Across three training seeds on Omni-MATH-Test, with MATH500 as a secondary high-coverage validation benchmark, PBA improves both \PassK{1} and high-budget coverage over matched GRPO. A 3000-prompt regime-controlled diagnostic study is consistent across seeds with the expected signature: ordinary GRPO loses base-solvable boundary prompts, while PBA preserves rare verifier-positive trajectories. We use mathematical verifiers as a controlled testbed for verifier-guided optimization; the same pass@k inversion risk applies to ECCV-relevant vision-language agents when repeated visual, spatial, or chart-reasoning attempts are checked by external tools or verifiers. Reasoning post-training should decide not only how strongly to optimize, but which prompts are safe to optimize.

</details>


### [239] [Conflict Resolution under Degraded Surveillance in Air Corridors Using Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20547)
*Esrat Farhana Dulia,Syed Arbab Mohd Shihab,Caleb Adams,Ruben Del Rosario*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于深度Q网络的多智能体强化学习框架，用于在噪声、延迟、不完整或不可用的监控信息下，实现异构无人机和电动垂直起降飞行器在三维走廊内的分散式冲突消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级空中机动（AAM）运行要求飞行器在监控信息存在噪声、延迟、不完整或暂时不可用时仍能保持安全间隔，现有方法在退化监控条件下的冲突消解能力不足。

Method: 采用深度Q网络（DQN）的多智能体强化学习框架，为两类飞行器分别训练独立策略，使用局部观测和14动作空间（包括保持航向、转弯、垂直机动、着陆和速度控制），并模拟了多种退化条件（观测噪声、通信延迟、信息丢失、风扰动、执行器不确定性、模型不确定性）。

Result: 在90种交通密度和最小间隔阈值组合下评估，安全条件下约79%时间保持运动；冲突期间转弯占33%、保持运动占29%、速度控制占25%、垂直机动占13%；得到6个帕累托最优配置，揭示安全性与走廊容量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架支持在退化监控条件下对更安全的AAM冲突消解策略进行基于仿真的评估。

Abstract: Safe Advanced Air Mobility operations require aircraft to maintain separation when surveillance information is noisy, delayed, incomplete, or temporarily unavailable. This study develops a Deep Q-Network-based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning framework for decentralized conflict resolution among heterogeneous small unmanned aerial vehicles and electric vertical takeoff and landing aircraft operating within a structured three-dimensional corridor. Separate policies are trained for the two aircraft categories using local observations and a 14-action space that includes maintaining course, turning, vertical maneuvering, landing, and speed control. The simulation incorporates aircraft-specific dynamics, energy use, corridor constraints, observation noise, communication delay, information dropout, wind disturbance, actuator uncertainty, and model uncertainty. The trained policies are evaluated across 90 combinations of traffic density and minimum separation thresholds. Loss-of-separation frequency and duration generally increase with traffic density and separation requirements, although most events are resolved within 1s. Under safe conditions, agents maintain their motion approximately 79% of the time. During conflicts, turning accounts for 33% of actions, followed by maintaining motion at 29%, speed control at 25%, and vertical maneuvers at 13%. Six Pareto-optimal configurations reveal trade-offs between safety and corridor capacity. The framework supports the simulation-based evaluation of safer AAM conflict-resolution strategies under degraded surveillance conditions.

</details>


### [240] [SOAP, Muon, and Beyond: Pushing LLM Pretraining Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20548)
*Mikail Khona,Aditya Vavre,Boxiang Wang,Deyu Fu,Hao Wu,Mike Chrzanowski,Bryan Catanzaro,Dheevatsa Mudigere,Jeff Pool,Michael Lightstone,Mohammad Shoeybi,Mostofa Patwary,Nima Tajbakhsh,Tijmen Blankevoort*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模LLM预训练中高阶优化器（如Muon和SOAP）的数值不稳定性和计算开销问题，提出算法改进（如逐步QR正交化、改进预条件策略）和系统优化（层式分布式优化器），实验表明在百亿参数/万亿token规模下SOAP和Muon优于AdamW，并在大batch size下保持稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶优化器（Muon、SOAP）相比AdamW收敛更快，但计算成本高且数值稳定性差，限制了其在规模化训练中的采用。

Method: 1) 算法层面：发现SOAP在大batch size下的不稳定性，提出逐步QR正交化和改进预条件策略消除loss spikes；2) 实验层面：通过update-RMS匹配实现公平学习率迁移，统一对比SOAP、Muon和AdamW；3) 系统层面：设计层式分布式优化器，兼容Megatron-LM，平衡内存并隐藏通信开销。

Result: 在多亿参数模型、万亿token规模的实验中，SOAP和Muon一致优于AdamW；在batch size高达1亿token时保持训练稳定性，而AdamW性能下降。

Conclusion: 通过算法改进和系统优化，高阶优化器可在大规模LLM预训练中实际部署，并带来显著收益。

Abstract: Higher-order optimizers such as Muon and SOAP offer faster convergence than AdamW, but their computational cost and numerical stability challenges have limited adoption at scale. In this work, we adapt and enhance preconditioned gradient methods to overcome the practical challenges of large-scale LLM pretraining. We first identify instabilities in SOAP at large batch sizes and propose algorithmic modifications including per-step QR orthogonalization and improved preconditioning strategies that eliminate loss spikes and enable stable training in these regimes. We then present a unified empirical study of SOAP, Muon, and AdamW using update-RMS matching to ensure fair learning rate transfer across optimizers. As part of this analysis, we empirically evaluate the orthogonalization quality of Muon. Our experiments on multi-billion-parameter models trained on trillions of tokens reveal that SOAP and Muon consistently outperform AdamW at the scales we tested. Notably, at batch sizes of up to 100M tokens for next-token prediction, these optimizers maintain training stability and quality while AdamW degrades. To enable efficient training at large scale, we introduce a layer-wise distributed optimizer compatible with Megatron-LM. Our implementation balances memory and hides communication while avoiding approximations to the optimizer computations, thus retaining their convergence benefits. Additionally, we identify and build specific system-level improvements to further accelerate our layer-wise implementation. To support the research community, we release a codebase that contains emerging algorithms for optimization: https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/Emerging-Optimizers

</details>


### [241] [SevDiff: Severity-Conditioned Diffusion for Long-Tail Conflict Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20549)
*Eni Solomon Laughter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SevDiff是一种基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的严重性条件轨迹生成方法，能够以目标最小TTC值作为输入，生成冲突严重程度匹配的车辆交互轨迹。在UTE SQM-W-1数据集上训练后，对于0.5-1.5秒的TTC目标实现了100%命中率（±0.5秒容忍度），2.0-2.5秒时达到97-99%，生成的轨迹具有良好的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADAS评估用的轨迹数据集严重偏向常规驾驶，真实车-车冲突事件极为罕见，而越罕见的事件一旦ADAS系统未能处理，代价越高。现有生成方法只能基于场景级属性（空间目标、智能体结构、自然语言对抗目标）进行条件控制，无法接受目标TTC值作为输入并确保生成结果的冲突严重性可控。

Method: 提出SevDiff，一种严重性条件去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM），以请求的最小TTC值作为标量条件信号，生成配对车辆交互轨迹。采用命中率指标评估生成轨迹的冲突严重性是否匹配请求。训练数据来自UTE SQM-W-1高速公路交织段数据集，包含468个交互窗口（1,041辆车，822,691个观测值）。

Result: TTC目标0.5-1.5秒时命中率100%（±0.5秒容忍度），2.0-2.5秒时97-99%，TTC=5.0秒时降至39%。12个运动学特征中最大超出范围率仅4.7%，超过96.5%的样本无负速度或负间隙值。

Conclusion: SevDiff能够以可控精度生成指定严重性的冲突轨迹，命中率随TTC增加而下降的模式可被物理解释为条件信号强度相对于训练先验的减弱，这使其成为对生成器精度的刻画而非简单的通过/失败评判，为ADAS系统的面向安全性评估提供了可控的对抗性测试数据生成能力。

Abstract: Trajectory datasets used in ADAS evaluation are heavily biased toward routine driving; genuine vehicle-to-vehicle conflict events are rare, and the rarer the event, the higher the cost when an ADAS system fails to handle it. Existing generative approaches address this imbalance by conditioning on scene-level properties - spatial goals, agent structure, or natural-language adversarial objectives - but none can accept a target Time-to-Collision (TTC) value as input and be held to producing it within a measurable error. This paper introduces SevDiff, a severity-conditioned denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) that accepts a requested minimum TTC value as a scalar conditioning signal and generates paired vehicle interaction trajectories whose realized conflict severity matches the request, evaluated through a hit-rate metric. Trained on 468 interaction windows extracted from the UTE SQM-W-1 expressway weaving-section dataset (1,041 vehicles, 822,691 observations after smoothing), SevDiff achieves 100% hit-rate within +/-0.5 s for TTC targets of 0.5-1.5 s and 97-99% at 2.0-2.5 s, with graceful degradation to 39% at TTC = 5.0 s. Generated kinematic features are physically plausible, with a maximum out-of-range rate of 4.7% across 12 features and no negative speed or gap values in more than 96.5% of samples. The hit-rate degradation pattern is physically interpretable as the strength of the conditioning signal relative to the training prior, making it a precision characterization of the generator rather than a pass/fail result.

</details>


### [242] [Beyond SBDD: Geometric Deep Learning in Polypharmacology and Multi-target Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20550)
*Tianming Han,Zhijie Pan,Wenchi Ge,Qi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了几何深度学习（GDL）在解决多靶点药物设计中几何约束冲突问题的最新进展，涵盖从不变图神经网络到SE(3)-等变扩散模型的架构，以及共享结合口袋表征、多靶点活性预测和双靶点配体从头生成等核心应用，强调结构条件生成算法与多组学整合在理性多药理学分子工程中的范式转变作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"一药一靶"的SBDD范式难以应对代偿信号通路和耐药性引发的多因素疾病（如癌症、神经退行性疾病），而多药理学虽有协同治疗优势，但理性设计能同时满足多靶点几何约束的配体存在计算瓶颈，亟需新的计算方法来突破这一限制。

Method: 系统调研从不变图神经网络到SE(3)-等变扩散模型的GDL架构，分析其在三个核心维度的应用：（1）利用几何嵌入表征共享结合口袋；（2）通过异构图融合进行多靶点生物活性预测；（3）从头生成双靶点配体。重点关注结合扩散模型与强化学习的结构条件生成算法。

Result: GDL能够有效利用非欧几里得分子数据捕捉内在3D结构相互依赖关系；结构条件生成算法可自主解决竞争性结合位点间的几何冲突；多模态组学集成和专门几何基准基础设施对模型验证起关键作用。

Conclusion: 药物发现正从偶然探索向理性的、结构驱动的多药理学分子工程范式转变，GDL为下一代治疗药物开发提供了清晰的结构化指导框架。

Abstract: The traditional "one drug, one target" paradigm of structure-based drug design (SBDD) frequently proves inadequate for treating multifactorial diseases such as cancer and neurodegenerative disorders, owing to compensatory signaling pathways and the emergence of drug resistance. While polypharmacology offers a synergistic therapeutic strategy, the rational design of ligands capable of simultaneously satisfying the geometric constraints imposed by multiple targets remains a major computational bottleneck. This review positions geometric deep learning (GDL) as a powerful integrative approach to overcome these limitations. We systematically survey GDL architectures ranging from invariant graph neural networks to SE(3)-equivariant diffusion models that harness non-Euclidean molecular data to capture intrinsic three-dimensional (3D) structural interdependencies. We critically analyze GDL applications across three core dimensions, including the characterization of shared binding pockets via geometric embeddings, multi-target bioactivity prediction through heterogeneous graph fusion, and de novo generation of dual-target ligands. Particular emphasis is placed on emerging structure-conditioned generative algorithms that integrate diffusion models with reinforcement learning to autonomously resolve complex geometric conflicts between competing binding sites. Furthermore, we evaluate the pivotal role of multimodal omics integration and specialized geometric benchmarking infrastructures in validating these models. By synthesizing these methodological advances, this review elucidates the paradigm shift in drug discovery from serendipitous exploration to rational, structure-driven polypharmacological molecular engineering, thereby providing a clear, structured guide for navigating the complexities of next-generation therapeutics.

</details>


### [243] [SenCos-GEM: SENet-Calibrated and Law-of-Cosines-Constrained Geometry-Enhanced Molecular Representation for Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20551)
*Tianming Han,Li Zhang,Qi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SenCos-GEM是一个显式解耦的几何增强分子表征学习框架，通过余弦定理驱动的物理引导几何一致性损失和SENet校准机制，在分子性质预测任务上取得SOTA，尤其在FreeSolv、Lipophilicity和QM9等3D构象敏感回归任务上分别实现12.9%、5.3%和8.2%的相对误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D GNN的自监督分子表征学习方法缺乏显式物理约束，易受粗经验力场引入的几何噪声影响，且在下游适配过程中忽略动态特征调制，导致灾难性遗忘和负迁移。

Method: 提出SenCos-GEM框架：1) 基于余弦定理的物理引导几何一致性损失，用于提取高保真且数学不变的3D空间先验；2) 在主干网络中集成轻量级Squeeze-and-Excitation模块作为任务特定适配器；3) 双调制预测头结合Feature-wise Linear Modulation和SENet机制实现动态特征重校准。

Result: 在MoleculeNet基准测试中，SenCos-GEM在FreeSolv上RMSE降低12.9%，Lipophilicity上RMSE降低5.3%，QM9上MAE降低8.2%；在立体异构体区分和构象扰动判别上表现优越。

Conclusion: SenCos-GEM通过显式物理约束和动态特征调制，实现了分子性质预测领域的显著突破，尤其在3D构象敏感回归任务上建立了新的SOTA。

Abstract: Effective molecular representation learning is crucial for accurate molecular property prediction. Recently, numerous self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches leveraging 3D GNNs have been developed to capture comprehensive 3D structural information for drug discovery. However, existing methods lack explicit physical constraints and are highly susceptible to geometric noise induced by coarse empirical force fields during large-scale pre-training.Furthermore, they overlook dynamic feature modulation during downstream adaptation, often resulting in catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer. To address these limitations, we introduce SenCos-GEM, a novel explicitly decoupled geometry-enhanced molecular representation learning framework that incorporates SENet-calibrated and law-of-cosines-constrained enhancements. SenCos-GEM employs a physics-guided geometric consistency loss based on the law of cosines to derive high-fidelity and mathematically invariant 3D spatial priors. In addition, lightweight Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) modules are integrated into the backbone as task-specific adapters, while a dual-modulation prediction head combines Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) and SENet mechanisms to enable dynamic feature recalibration. SenCos-GEM demonstrates highly competitive performance across diverse classification and regression tasks on MoleculeNet benchmark, establishing new state-of-the-art results specifically on 3D conformation-sensitive regression tasks, such as FreeSolv, Lipophilicity, and QM9, achieving relative error reductions of 12.9% (RMSE), 5.3% (RMSE), and 8.2% (MAE), respectively. Moreover, our model exhibits superior capability in distinguishing stereoisomers and discriminating conformational perturbations, underscoring its robust spatial modeling performance. Collectively, SenCos-GEM represents a significant breakthrough in accurate molecular property prediction.

</details>


### [244] [Thermodynamic Weight Decay: Exploring Grokking Acceleration via Attention Specific Heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20552)
*Chitraansh Pandey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CvAdamW优化器，利用注意力对数方差（类比热容Cv）预测并促进神经网络的"顿悟"（grokking）现象，在模算术任务上将顿悟时间提前至2802轮（基线从未顿悟），并通过冷启动变体平均减少257轮延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在过拟合训练数据后延迟泛化的"顿悟"现象浪费大量训练轮次且不可预测，现有方法无法有效加速或预测这一转变。

Method: 基于Transformer注意力与热力学系统的同构性，将注意力对数方差视为比热容Cv；提出CvAdamW优化器，在线监测Cv峰值并在检测到相变时动态缩放权重衰减以注入热能；针对初始化噪声、小批量微涟漪和弹弓盲区三个失败模式设计了记忆门和指数移动平均减震器；进一步提出尺度不变z-score重构以消除任务特定超参数。

Result: 在a+b mod 97任务上，CvAdamW在4000轮预算内于2802轮实现顿悟（基线从未顿悟）；10个配对种子的冷启动变体平均减少257轮（6.0%），8/10种子改善，Wilcoxon p=0.049，Cohen's d=0.68；所有10个种子中Cv峰值均先于顿悟出现。

Conclusion: 神经网络的泛化转变前存在可检测的前兆信号，基于物理启发的比例干预可在固定计算预算内促进泛化。

Abstract: Grokking -- the delayed generalization of neural networks long after they have memorized their training data -- wastes thousands of training epochs and is notoriously unpredictable. Building on the recent result that Transformer attention is formally isomorphic to a thermodynamic system, we treat the variance of attention logits as a specific heat Cv and show that its peak reliably precedes the generalization transition. We introduce CvAdamW, a drop-in AdamW variant that monitors Cv online and injects thermal energy by dynamically scaling weight decay when a phase transition is detected. Through a strictly iterative development process we identify three failure modes -- initialization noise, mini-batch micro-ripples, and slingshot blinding -- and resolve them with a memorization gate and an exponential-moving-average shock absorber. On modular arithmetic (a+b mod 97), CvAdamW enables grokking at epoch 2802 in a 4000-epoch budget where the baseline never groks. We further propose a scale-invariant z-score reformulation that removes task-specific hyperparameters, and evaluate it across 10 paired seeds. A paired analysis shows the cold-start variant reduces mean grokking latency by 257 epochs (6.0%; median 166 epochs; Wilcoxon p=0.049, Cohen's d=0.68, bootstrap 95% CI [53,489]), improving 8 of 10 seeds; on this single task Cv peaks before grokking in all 10 seeds. Our results indicate that neural networks may expose detectable precursors of impending generalization transitions, and that a physically motivated, proportional intervention can facilitate generalization within a fixed compute budget. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [245] [Double-Scoring: Reliable Extraction of Strong Lottery Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20555)
*Bryce A. Christopherson,Jack Baretz,Darian Colgrove,Salah Dandan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双评分法（double-scoring），通过扩展评分空间参数化来改进边缘弹出（edge-popup）方法，解决了强彩票假设中子网提取的层稀疏度选择瓶颈问题，实验证明该方法显著提升了强票提取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强彩票假设理论虽证明了过参数化随机网络中存在训练前就已准确的子网络，但可靠的子网提取仍然困难。边缘弹出方法虽可用于提取强票，但其层稀疏度选择是核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出双评分法，通过扩充分数张量维度（零填充）来替代层稀疏度搜索，将固定密度掩码在扩增分数空间中保持对所有原始坐标掩码的可达性，该方法可解释为零扩增网络上的边缘弹出。

Result: 双评分法在强票提取上显著优于固定密度边缘弹出和初始剪枝基线，改进了倒带稀疏训练拓扑的性能，且对稀疏度超参数的敏感性显著降低。消融实验表明增益不仅来自额外可训练参数，更与扩增分数空间的竞争诱导有效原始稀疏度有关。

Conclusion: 双评分法有效解决了强彩票提取中层稀疏度选择的瓶颈问题，是一种更鲁棒且高效的强票提取方法。

Abstract: The lottery ticket hypothesis proposes that large random neural networks contain sparse subnetworks that can match the performance of dense models after comparable training. A stronger version asserts that sufficiently overparameterized random networks contain subnetworks that are already accurate before any weight training. Existing theory establishes that such strong lottery tickets exist, but reliable extraction remains difficult. We revisit edge-popup, a frozen-weight score-training method for extracting strong tickets, and identify layerwise sparsity selection as a central bottleneck. We introduce double-scoring, an augmented score-space parameterization that replaces a layerwise sparsity search with optimization over enlarged score tensors. We prove that fixed-density masking in an augmented score space preserves access to all original-coordinate masks, and we show that the resulting method can be interpreted as edge-popup on a zero-augmented network. In controlled experiments, double-scoring substantially improves strong-ticket extraction over fixed-density edge-popup and pruning-at-initialization baselines, improves on the performance of rewound sparse-training topologies, and exhibits markedly lower sensitivity to sparsity hyperparameters. Ablations show that the gain is not merely due to additional trainable score parameters, but is tied to the augmented score-space competition that induces the effective original sparsity.

</details>


### [246] [Monkey King Bang: A Unified Scientific Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20557)
*Hesen Chen,Xinyu Su,Xiaomeng Yang,Yuetan Lin,Zixiong Yang,Junyi An,Fenglei Cao,Yifeng Jiao,Yunqi Zhang,Yuan Cheng,Zhiyu Tan,Hao Li,Libo Wu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MKB（多学科知识基础模型），一个统一的多模态科学模型，能够同时进行理解和生成。MKB基于共享Transformer骨干网络，搭配模态特定的编码器、适配器和解码器，覆盖DNA、RNA、蛋白质、小分子、地球科学和医学图像六个科学分支，支持生物序列、分子字符串、气象场和分割掩码等原生输出。两阶段训练（先模态后语言）使其在科学理解和生成任务上达到竞争性表现，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现正从单一学科转向多领域推理，但现有AI系统要么专精于单一领域，要么主要通过文本分词和提示接口统一科学数据，难以处理多样化的科学输入、生成模态原生输出，也无法支持跨科学领域的联合理解、推理和生成。

Method: MKB采用共享Transformer骨干网络，搭配模态特定的编码器、适配器和解码器。训练采用两阶段课程：第一阶段将模态特定组件与冻结的骨干网络对齐；第二阶段使用混合科学和通用语料库将模态组件与语言骨干网络整合。模型覆盖六个科学分支（DNA、RNA、蛋白质、小分子、地球科学、医学图像），支持多种模态原生输出。

Result: 在生物和分子基准上实现了竞争性的科学理解能力；在天气预报、生物生成和医学图像分割上生成了高保真原生输出；同时很大程度上保留了Qwen3-VL骨干网络的通用能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了所提范式的可行性，表明共享骨干网络搭配模态特定组件的模型可以为未来跨领域科学多模态探索提供有前景的基础。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is increasingly shifting from isolated disciplines to multi-domain reasoning, and AI for science faces a similar transition. Existing systems are either specialised for individual domains or unify scientific data mainly through text tokenisation and prompt-based interfaces, limiting their ability to handle diverse scientific inputs, produce modality-native outputs, and support joint understanding, reasoning, and generation across scientific domains. We introduce MKB, a unified scientific multimodal model for both understanding and generation, built around a shared Transformer backbone and modality-tailored encoders, adapters, and decoders. MKB covers six scientific branches, including DNA, RNA, proteins, small molecules, earth science, and medical images, and supports native outputs such as biological sequences, molecular strings, meteorological fields, and segmentation masks. Training follows a two-stage modality-then-language curriculum: Stage 1 aligns modality-specific components with the frozen backbone, and Stage 2 consolidates them with the language backbone using mixed scientific and general corpora. Experiments show that MKB achieves competitive scientific understanding across biological and molecular benchmarks, produces high-fidelity native outputs for weather forecasting, biological generation, and medical-image segmentation, and largely retains the general capabilities of its Qwen3-VL backbone. These results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed paradigm, suggesting that shared-backbone models with modality-tailored components can provide a promising foundation for future cross-domain scientific multimodal exploration. The model and code are publicly available at https://github.com/Shanghai-Academy-of-AI-For-Science/MKB and https://huggingface.co/sais-org/MKB.

</details>


### [247] [StabilityBench: Benchmarking Instability in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20558)
*Emma Kondrup,Zachary Yang,Anne Imouza,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StabilityBench，一种将单轮基准查询转化为多轮交互历史的基准算子，通过注入人口统计代理或谄媚诱饵等真实用户模拟，揭示大语言模型在数学推理、健康问答和安全等任务中的性能不稳定问题，并提出了成本不变的轻量级变体StabilityBench-Mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估协议依赖静态、单轮的基准测试，无法捕捉对话场景中真实世界的变异性，导致对AI助手在高风险环境中的实际行为理解不足。

Method: 提出StabilityBench，一种模型无关的基准算子，将单轮基准查询扩展为多轮交互历史，通过人口统计代理或谄媚诱饵等用户模拟注入来增强现有基准，同时保持原始任务意图。并进一步提出StabilityBench-Mini，在保持规模的前提下沿多样化轴采样，实现更经济的评估。

Result: 在四个基准（数学推理、健康问答和安全）上评估九个LLM，结果显示模型性能在注入条件下持续不稳定，四个基准中有三个出现显著性能下降。

Conclusion: 静态评估存在重要局限性，需要更贴近现实的评估设置来揭示模型在实际部署中的行为不稳定性。

Abstract: AI Assistants are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, such as healthcare or government services. Yet their real-world behavior remains poorly understood due to strong context dependence. Current evaluation protocols follow a defense-in-depth paradigm with compounding layers of safeguards, ranging from traditional benchmarks to live or adversarial testing. Such benchmarks remain largely static and single-turn, limiting their ability to capture real-world variability in conversational settings. We propose StabilityBench, a principled, general and model-agnostic benchmark operator that transforms single-turn benchmark queries into multi-turn interaction histories. StabilityBench augments existing benchmarks by injecting realistic user simulations, through demographic proxies or sycophantic baits, while preserving original task intent. We apply StabilityBench to four benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, health question-answering and safety, and evaluate nine large language models under these conditions. Our results show that model performance is consistently unstable under these injections, with considerable performance degradations on three out of four benchmarks studied. These highlight important limitations of static evaluations and motivate more realistic evaluation settings. To this end, we propose StabilityBench-Mini: a size-preserving variant of StabilityBench that samples across diversification axes, enabling more realistic evaluation without increasing costs.

</details>


### [248] [Joint Utilization of Geospatial and census proxies for Autoencoder-Assisted Downscaling (JUGAAD) of socioeconomic indicators in India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20559)
*Aditya Dutt,Paul Gader,Aditya Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JuGAAD深度学习框架，通过整合印度人口普查和地理空间数据，在村庄集群尺度上高精度预测社会经济指标，解决数据源尺度不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家的贫困和粮食安全监测面临数据源尺度不匹配的挑战：人口普查数据覆盖面广但缺乏社会经济指标，而社会经济调查虽信息丰富但频率低、分辨率粗，亟需一种能从粗粒度调查数据生成细粒度预测的方法。

Method: JuGAAD框架包含三步：(1) 将普查和地理空间数据平均聚合到中间村庄集群尺度以降低噪声并规范化行政边界变化；(2) 利用自编码器将高维NSSO调查数据压缩为低维潜在表示；(3) 构建回归模型将聚合后的普查/地理空间数据映射到该潜在表示，然后将该函数应用于细粒度普查数据生成高分辨率预测。

Result: 该方法在村庄集群尺度上实现了对NSSO社会经济指标的高精度预测，并通过实际区域级NSSO指标验证了预测结果的有效性。

Conclusion: JuGAAD框架成功解决了数据源尺度不匹配问题，能够以强准确性从粗粒度调查数据生成细粒度的社会经济指标预测，为发展中国家的贫困和粮食安全监测提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Monitoring poverty and food security indicators is imperative for addressing socioeconomic challenges in developing nations. A limitation is mismatches in scale between data sources: census data provide geographic coverage, while socioeconomic indicators are derived from infrequently conducted surveys at coarse resolutions, posing a methodological challenge. This study introduces a deep learning framework, JuGAAD, using Indian census and survey data from 2001 and 2011 as a case study. We employ a three-step process: census and geospatial data are averaged into intermediate village-cluster-scale tessellations to reduce noise and regularize administrative boundary changes; an autoencoder compresses high-dimensional National Sample Survey Office (NSSO) data into a low-dimensional latent representation; and a regression model maps upscaled census and geospatial data to this representation. This function is applied to fine-grained census data to generate high-resolution predictions, validated against ground-truth district-level NSSO indicators. Results confirm the methodology predicts socioeconomic indicators at fine scales with strong accuracy.

</details>


### [249] [Chronofy: A Temporal-Logical Decay Architecture for Information Validity in Time-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20560)
*Muntaser Syed,Marius Silaghi,Sheikh Abujar,Sharun Akter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronofy是一种三层神经符号框架（TLDA），将时间有效性嵌入RAG系统的表示、检索和推理层，通过Matryoshka嵌入的时间子空间、基于贝叶斯衰减系数的图检索以及STL鲁棒性函数，解决RAG系统的时间幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG架构将所有检索事实视为同等有效，忽略其时间来源，导致时间幻觉——过时但看似合理的事实污染LLM输出（如昨天的化验结果与六个月前的结果被同等对待）。

Method: 三层架构：Layer 1在Matryoshka嵌入中保留专用时间子空间，使事实时效在表示层面不可移除；Layer 2将可学习指数衰减函数集成到基于图的检索中，衰减系数β_j通过贝叶斯决策理论近似为潜在过程均值回归率的两倍；Layer 3应用信号时序逻辑（STL）鲁棒性函数评估检索知识的时间有效性，并执行可能性最弱链接原则，用推理链中最衰减的证据约束输出置信度。

Result: 在时序知识图谱预测基准、TimE时序QA基准和领域敏感度分析上评估，显式时间衰减建模提高了检索精度，减少了时间幻觉，并在时间上下文不足时实现原则性的数据重新采集触发。

Conclusion: 将时间有效性直接嵌入RAG系统的各层能有效缓解时间幻觉，提升系统的时序感知能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve and integrate external knowledge to ground large language model (LLM) outputs.
