TL;DR: 提出了一种面向符号联邦学习的轻量级信息论安全聚合框架，通过单轮安全乘法计算多数投票多项式，结合逆形式指数约减和单轮安全乘法技术，在保证信息论安全的同时将在线通信量降低99.5%、延迟降低85.7%，并利用MDS码解码机制实现了对节点掉队和对抗行为的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号梯度方法（如signSGD）虽能降低通信开销，但其梯度符号暴露容易遭受推理攻击；现有安全聚合方案与符号方法不兼容或带来巨大的计算和通信开销。因此需要一种轻量级且信息论安全的聚合框架来保护符号联邦学习中的梯度隐私。

Method: 1) 设计单轮安全乘法协议，在诚实多数假设下安全计算多数投票多项式，仅向服务器暴露最终聚合符号；2) 提出逆形式指数约减技术，将有效多数投票多项式度数减半，降低通信与计算成本；3) 提出单轮安全乘法，实现线性离线复杂度和存储，且仅需单轮在线通信；4) 利用MDS码解码机制实现节点掉队和对抗行为的鲁棒性。

Result: 在线通信量降低99.5%，延迟降低85.7%；在节点掉队场景下准确率提升最高20.65%，在对抗行为场景下准确率提升最高10.74%。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模、低延迟、信息论安全的符号联邦学习聚合建立了实用基础。

Abstract: On-device federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving and personalized model training on resource-constrained devices such as smartphones and IoT nodes. To reduce communication cost, sign-based methods (e.g., signSGD) transmit one-bit gradients. However, exposing gradient signs makes them vulnerable to inference attacks, while existing secure aggregation schemes are often incompatible with such methods or incur significant computational and communication overhead. We propose a lightweight and information-theoretically secure aggregation framework tailored for sign-based FL. The framework securely computes the majority vote (MV) polynomial through single-round secure multiplication, ensuring end-to-end information-theoretic security under the honest-majority assumption while revealing only the final aggregated sign to the server. To enhance efficiency and scalability, we introduce two key techniques. First, inverse-form exponent reduction halves the effective MV polynomial degree, reducing both communication and computation costs. Second, we propose single-round secure multiplication, achieving linear offline complexity and storage with only a single online communication. Together, these techniques reduce online communication by up to 99.5% and latency by up to 85.7% compared to conventional approaches. Also, by leveraging inherent MDS-code-based decoding, the framework achieves robustness against both dropouts and adversarial behaviors, yielding accuracy gains of up to 20.65% and 10.74%, respectively. Overall, the proposed framework establishes a practical foundation for large-scale, low-latency, and information-theoretically secure aggregation in sign-based FL.

</details>


### [286] [HierarchicalDAEW: Domain-Aware Edge-Weighted Graph Convolution with Evidential Uncertainty for Multi-Section Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from H&E Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20896)
*Kritanu Chattopadhyay,Soumya Chatterjee,Ondrej Krejcar,Debotosh Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HierarchicalDAEW是一个双图架构，通过领域感知的边加权卷积操作处理组织异质性，结合蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用先验与组织特异性共表达，并利用证据不确定性估计提供校准的置信区间，在六个Visium数据集上超越了十三种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高昂且技术要求高，限制了其临床常规部署；现有从H&E组织学预测空间基因表达的方法忽略了组织架构，且很少量化预测的可信度。

Method: 提出HierarchicalDAEW双图架构：(1) 点图层面，使用领域感知边加权卷积算子，根据Leiden聚类结果区分域间、域内和边界边的不同投影；(2) 基因图层面，融合STRING-DB的蛋白质互作先验与组织特异性共表达，通过学习的注意力门控从地标基因集传播预测到更广基因面板；(3) 采用证据不确定性估计提供校准的置信区间。

Result: 在六个人类Visium切片（乳腺、结直肠、前列腺、小脑组织）上，针对十三种已发表基线方法，HierarchicalDAEW实现了与真实表达最强的相关性，在多种子可重复性检验和阴性对照中保持优势。

Conclusion: 领域感知的边类型分层深度对于性能提升是必要的，校准的不确定性估计能识别低置信度预测以供病理学家审查后再做出临床决策。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics assays remain costly and technically demanding, restricting transcriptome-wide profiling to specialist settings and preventing routine clinical deployment. Predicting spatially resolved gene expression from H&E histology could close this gap, yet current methods largely ignore the underlying tissue architecture and rarely quantify how their predictions can be trusted. We introduce HierarchicalDAEW, a dual-graph architecture that addresses both gaps. On the spot graph, a Domain-Aware Edge-Weighted convolutional operator learns separate projections for inter-domain, intra-domain, and boundary edges derived from Leiden clustering, allowing the model to treat tissue heterogeneity as an explicit structural signal rather than an implicit one. A second gene-level graph then fuses protein-protein interaction priors from STRING-DB with tissue-specific co-expression through learned attention gating, propagating predictions from a landmark gene set to a broader gene panel. Reliability is handled through evidential uncertainty estimation, which produces far better calibrated confidence intervals than Monte Carlo dropout under identical conditions. Across six human Visium sections spanning breast, colorectal, prostate, and cerebellar tissue, and against thirteen published baselines, HierarchicalDAEW achieves the strongest correlation with ground-truth expression, with gains that hold up under multi-seed reproducibility checks and negative controls that rule out positional shortcuts. Ablations further confirm that both the domain-aware edge typing and the hierarchical depth are necessary to this improvement, and calibrated uncertainty estimates identify low-confidence predictions for pathologist review before clinical action.

</details>


### [287] [Multi-turn RL with Structural and Performance Aware Rewards for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20908)
*Quazi Ishtiaque Mahmud,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CudaPerf，一个结合可验证执行奖励和结构感知奖励的反射式RL框架，用于优化CUDA代码生成。该框架包含离线成对排序和在线RL训练两个阶段，并引入包含2.9k C→CUDA和1k PyTorch→CUDA程序的数据集。实验表明，CudaPerf在加速比和正确性上显著优于Qwen-3-32B和CUDA Agent等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法主要依赖正确性和加速比等基于结果的信号，忽略了程序性能关键的结构属性（如内存合并、占用率、算术强度、同步模式等），导致无法生成优化充分的代码。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 离线成对排序模块，通过对比学习区分强弱程序候选；(2) 在线RL训练阶段，联合优化正确性、性能和结构效率的统一奖励信号，并利用执行反馈进行迭代细化。

Result: 在C→CUDA任务上相比Qwen-3-32B实现最高5倍加速比提升和17%正确性提升；在PyTorch→CUDA任务上相比CUDA Agent实现最高3.32倍加速比提升和7%正确性提升。

Conclusion: 结构感知的奖励信号能显著提升RLVR框架生成优化代码的能力，验证了将并行化特征纳入强化学习训练的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful technique to enhance the reasoning capacity of LLMs for optimized code generation. However, existing RLVR approaches primarily rely on outcome-based signals such as correctness and speedup, overlooking performance-critical structural properties of programs that are essential for generating optimized code. In this work, we propose CudaPerf, a reflective RL framework that incorporates both verifiable execution rewards and structural code-aware rewards derived from parallelization features (e.g., memory coalescing, occupancy, Arithmatic Intensity, and synchronization patterns). CudaPerf operates in two stages: (1) an offline pairwise ranking module that learns to distinguish strong and weak program candidates via contrastive comparisons, and (2) an online RL training phase that jointly optimizes for correctness, performance, and structural efficiency through a unified reward signal. To further enhance learning, CudaPerf utilizes iterative refinement using execution feedback enabling progressive improvement of generated candidates. We also introduce a dataset comprising 2.9k C to CUDA and 1k PyTorch to CUDA programs, each paired with diverse input configurations and multiple CUDA implementations encompassing diverse optimization strategies. CudaPerf is evaluated across multiple benchmarks comprising both C to CUDA and PyTorch to CUDA transformations. Empirical findings suggest that CudaPerf significantly outperforms strong baselines, including Qwen-3-32B (for C to CUDA) and CUDA Agent (for PyTorch to CUDA) by achieving up to 5X & 3.32X improvements in speedup, and 17% & 7% improvements in correctness, respectively.

</details>


### [288] [Three-Pronged Spectral Control for Federated Parameter Efficient Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20914)
*Shiva Raj Pokhrel,Dipsan Bhattarai,Anwar Walid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRISHUL是一个面向联邦参数高效微调(PEFT)的谱控制框架，通过共享冻结的多头低秩基、核范数近端收缩和非均匀层分配策略，在非IID客户端异质性下提升收敛性和最终性能，且不增加额外通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦PEFT在非IID客户端异质性下表现脆弱，LoRA中不同客户端学习到的更新子空间存在谱错位，导致聚合方差高和全局迁移效果差。

Method: TRISHUL包含三个核心组件：(1) 共享冻结的多头低秩基，实现核心更新的代数精确聚合；(2) 核范数近端收缩，在上传前抑制客户端特定的高秩谱分量；(3) 基于预训练层容量的凹水填充预算规则，非均匀分配各层的适配头。收缩仅在小核心矩阵上进行，计算开销可忽略。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、SVHN、20 Newsgroups、MRQA、GLUE(使用LLaMA3.2-1B)等多个视觉和语言基准上，TRISHUL相比联邦LoRA基线在收敛性、稳定性和最终性能上均有提升，异质性越强提升越大。

Conclusion: TRISHUL通过谱控制有效缓解了联邦PEFT在非IID场景下的异质性问题，在几乎不增加额外开销的情况下显著提升性能，但本身不提供正式隐私保证。

Abstract: Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) enables communication-efficient adaptation of large pretrained models on decentralized edge data, but it remains fragile under non-IID client heterogeneity. In low-rank adaptation (LoRA), different clients may learn locally useful but spectrally misaligned update subspaces, causing high-variance aggregation and poor global transfer. We propose TRISHUL, a spectral-control framework for robust federated PEFT. TRISHUL follows the FL no-raw-data-sharing setting but does not itself provide formal privacy guarantees. TRISHUL uses shared frozen multi-head low-rank bases to obtain algebraically exact aggregation of compact core updates, applies nuclear norm proximal shrinkage to suppress client-specific high-rank spectral components before upload, and allocates adaptation heads non-uniformly across layers using a concave water filling budget rule derived from pretrained layer capacity. Because shrinkage is performed only on small core matrices, TRISHUL adds negligible computation and no extra per-round communication over the underlying multi-head PEFT protocol. Across vision and language benchmarks, including CIFAR-100, SVHN, 20 Newsgroups, MRQA, and GLUE with LLaMA3.2-1B, TRISHUL improves convergence, stability, and final performance over federated LoRA baselines, with greater gains under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [289] [Best-of-Evidence: Best-of-N Selection under Partial Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20950)
*Cenwei Zhang,Teng Fang,Yuxia Wang,Derek Li,Bryan Dai,Lei You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Best-of-Evidence (BoE)框架，在仅有部分验证可用的情况下，通过带符号候选-因子图和有预算的证据分配，改进多模态任务的推理时选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BoN方法假设可以可靠评估完整候选答案，但许多视觉-语言任务只提供部分验证（如发现、跨度、值、区域或关系），且同一主张可能在不同候选中以对立立场重复出现，需要一种能处理部分验证和冲突证据的选择框架。

Method: BoE保持BoN候选池固定，用带符号候选-因子图表示可重用主张，将有限预算分配给可以改变最终选择的证据行为，并形式化部分验证下的选择问题，提供基于分数的实用控制器。

Result: 理论分析表明残余证据容量限制了证据驱动的改进，且共享因子查询可实现O(log K)与Θ(K)的查询分离。在四个医学VQA场景的实验表明，当证据可靠、对比且与决策相关时，BoE能改进固定池选择并挽救部分BoN失败。

Conclusion: BoE为部分验证环境下的推理时选择提供了形式化框架，但通道质量和候选生成限制会阻止普遍增益。

Abstract: BoN improves model outputs by sampling several candidates and selecting one with a proxy score, but it assumes that complete candidates can be evaluated reliably. Many vision-language tasks instead provide only partial verification: a finding, span, value, region, or relation may be checkable even when no dependable whole-response verifier exists. Moreover, the same claim may recur across candidates with opposing stances, allowing one observation to support part of the pool and contradict another. We introduce Best-of-Evidence (BoE), an inference-time selection framework that keeps the BoN candidate pool fixed, represents reusable claims with a signed candidate--factor graph, and allocates a limited budget to evidence actions that can change the final choice. BoE formalizes selection under partial verification and provides a practical score-based controller, with the zero-budget case recovering the underlying BoN decision. Theoretically, we show that residual evidence capacity limits any evidence-driven improvement and that shared factor queries can achieve an O(log K) versus Θ(K) query separation in a factor-code model. Common-ledger experiments on four medical VQA settings show that BoE can improve fixed-pool selection and rescue some BoN failures when evidence is reliable, contrastive, and decision-relevant, while also revealing the channel-quality and candidate-generation limits that prevent universal gains.

</details>


### [290] [The Weight of Silence: A Causal Case for Weights Over the Scratchpad in Latent Chess Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20952)
*Ishan S. Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过象棋任务的强化学习实验，发现潜在推理（latent reasoning）的主要作用是在训练期间塑造模型参数，而非作为推理时主动查询的“内部草稿纸”。RL增强了模型对潜在思维向量扰动的鲁棒性，而非依赖其具体内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于潜在推理的因果分析局限于数学和逻辑任务，且仅比较模型在单一检查点对自身思维的依赖，从未在RL训练前后进行对比。本文旨在填补这一空白，检验RL是否改变模型对潜在思维向量的依赖模式。

Method: 训练一个下棋模型，经过分阶段潜在推理课程学习后接RL训练。在RL前后对同一模型执行六种条件的因果干预实验：替换或添加匹配噪声到潜在思维向量、消融向量、以及使用精确零向量破坏。

Result: RL后合法性从48%提升至61%，将杀虚报完全消除。精确零向量破坏下，合法性从RL前的1%提升至RL后的9%；其他较轻度的扰动条件下，RL前后性能差距趋势类似但未达统计显著性。模型对精确零向量之外的扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RL并未增加模型对潜在思维内容的依赖，反而增强了模型对扰动的鲁棒性。潜在推理的主要作用是在训练中塑造参数，而非作为推理时的主动草稿纸。同时证明在象棋领域RL可带来收益，打破了此前多组报告在数学和逻辑之外RL无效的结论。

Abstract: Latent, or silent, reasoning lets language models carry out intermediate computation in continuous vector space instead of words, and is widely assumed to function as an internal scratchpad the model actively consults during inference. Whether that assumption survives reinforcement learning has not been tested directly: existing causal analyses of latent reasoning are confined to math and logic tasks, and compare a model's reliance on its thoughts within a single checkpoint, never before and after an RL stage. We train a chess-playing model through a staged latent-reasoning curriculum followed by reinforcement learning, and find legality climbs monotonically to 61% (from a 48% pre-RL baseline) while checkmate confabulation is eliminated entirely. To locate this gain, we run a six-condition causal intervention suite on the same model before and after RL: substituting or adding matched noise to the latent thought vectors leaves performance unchanged, ablating them causes only mild degradation, and only exact-zero vectors cause collapse. This robustness gap is itself the finding: under exact-zero corruption, legality collapses to 1% pre-RL versus 9% post-RL, a gap that survives correction for testing across the full battery; milder conditions trend similarly without independently reaching significance. RL appears to add robustness to disruption, not reliance on thought content. These results push back against the field's default assumption that latent thoughts function as an actively consulted inference-time scratchpad, and instead indicate latent reasoning's principal effect here is shaping the model's parameters during training. We also demonstrate a working RL gain in chess, a domain outside the math and logic settings where multiple groups report the same latent-reasoning-plus-RL recipe failing to improve accuracy over SFT.

</details>


### [291] [ADABORD: a novel AdaBoost approach for ordinal classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21003)
*Rafael Ayllón-Gavilán,Francisco José Martínez-Estudillo,David Guijo-Rubio,César Hervás-Martínez,Pedro A. Gutiérrez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADABORD是一个专门为序数分类问题设计的AdaBoost框架，通过在基估计器中使用序数Gini分裂准则，以及在误差函数中使用绝对排序概率分数来充分利用序数信息，在TOC-UCO基准测试上显著优于七种先进方法，尤其在五个及以上类别的数据集上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序数分类方法未能充分利用类别间的序数信息，常将问题视为名义分类进行处理，从而损失了性能潜力。

Method: 在AdaBoost算法中融入序数分类特性：(1)基估计器采用带有序数Gini分裂准则的决策树；(2)误差函数使用绝对排序概率分数来更新样本权重和分类器权重，该指标同时考虑了类别的顺序和类别间的距离。

Result: 在TOC-UCO基准测试上与七种先进方法比较，ADABORD显著优于竞争方法，尤其在五个及以上类别的数据集上表现更为突出，且结果经过统计分析验证。

Conclusion: ADABORD有效利用了序数信息，显著提升了序数分类性能，代码和实验协议已公开以确保可重复性并促进未来研究。

Abstract: Ordinal Classification (OC) deals with classification tasks where the classes follow a natural order. Despite the progress in OC, many existing approaches fail to fully leverage the ordinal information, treating the problem as nominal classification and thereby losing performance potential. In this work, ADABORD, an AdaBoost framework specifically designed for ordinal classification problems, is introduced. The ordinal nature of the classes is incorporated into two key components of the well-known AdaBoost algorithm: 1) the base estimator, where decision trees with the ordinal Gini splitting criterion are proposed; 2) the error function used to update sample weights at each stage and the weights of the classifier in the final ensemble model, given by the absolute ranked probability score, a measure that accounts for both the ordering and the distance between classes. ADABORD is extensively compared against seven state-of-the-art methods on the TOC-UCO repository, the largest benchmark collection for OC to date. The experimental results, supported by statistical analysis, show that ADABORD significantly outperforms competing methods, particularly on datasets with five or more classes, where the ordinal structure becomes more pronounced. Source code, along with all experimental protocols, is publicly available to ensure reproducibility and facilitate future research in OC.

</details>


### [292] [Weight-norm Criticality: A Mechanism for Loss Spikes Induced by the Normalization and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21005)
*Xiaolong Li,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度神经网络训练中一种被忽视的不稳定来源——权重范数临界性（weight-norm criticality）。该现象源于归一化（引入尺度不变性）与权重衰减（持续缩小参数范数）的相互作用：当权重衰减系数增大时，尺度不变权重的范数被推向零，导致损失景观的锐度急剧上升，从而引发训练不稳定和损失尖峰。这解释了为何权重惩罚能提升泛化性能但无法任意增强——过度的权重衰减会使权重范数越过临界边界，破坏训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于学习率临界性（如Edge of Stability）来解释训练不稳定性，但忽略了实际深度神经网络训练中由归一化与权重衰减相互作用引发的另一类关键不稳定机制。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示权重衰减系数与尺度不变权重范数、损失景观锐度之间的关系，提出权重范数临界性机制，并在具有尺度不变组分的网络上进行实证验证。

Result: 发现权重衰减系数增加会驱使尺度不变权重范数趋近于零，导致损失景观锐度快速增长，破坏优化动力学并引发损失尖峰；实证结果支持了理论预测。

Conclusion: 权重范数临界性是深度网络训练中独立于学习率临界性的重要不稳定来源，为理解损失尖峰提供了新的机械论视角，并解释了权重惩罚在泛化与稳定性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Most explanations of training instability focus on \emph{learning-rate criticality}, typically characterized by the Edge of Stability, beyond which optimization becomes unstable. We argue that, in practical deep neural network training, there is an additional and often overlooked \emph{weight-norm criticality}. This criticality is induced by the interaction between normalization (which introduces scale-invariant components) and weight decay (which persistently shrinks parameter norms). As the weight decay coefficient increases, the norms of scale-invariant weights are progressively driven toward zero. Meanwhile, the sharpness of the loss landscape increases rapidly, destabilizing the optimization dynamics and resulting in abrupt loss spikes. This perspective provides a rationale for why weight penalties can improve generalization yet cannot be made arbitrarily strong: excessive decay drives scale-invariant weight norms past a critical boundary and destabilizes training. Our work provides a new mechanistic understanding of loss spikes through the lens of \emph{weight-norm criticality}. Moreover, \emph{weight-norm criticality} yields testable predictions that we validate empirically in networks with scale-invariant components, providing empirical support for the proposed mechanism.

</details>


### [293] [Regularized Optimization on Grassmann Manifold: Theory, Algorithm and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21039)
*Zhuan Liang,Zheng Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了正则化投影矩阵逼近（RPMA）框架，通过在经典谱投影中引入正则化项，在Grassmann流形上优化得到更鲁棒、稀疏且可解释的投影矩阵估计，并开发了高效的Riemannian梯度算法和Cayley-SMW方法，实验表明在噪声环境下显著优于传统谱方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱方法在社区检测、聚类和图学习中的性能严重依赖于底层谱子空间的准确估计，但在噪声、异常值或模型扰动下性能会大幅下降。为克服这一局限，需要一种更鲁棒的投影矩阵估计方法。

Method: 提出RPMA框架，在秩K投影矩阵的流形（等价于Grassmann流形）上优化带正则化项的目标函数；推导了一阶和二阶最优性条件，分析了正则化主导特征空间的局部稳定性及临界点景观的稳定性；开发了基于回溯线搜索的Riemannian梯度投影算法，以及避免重复特征分解的Cayley-Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury (Cayley-SMW)梯度方法。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，RPMA显著提高了投影矩阵的恢复精度，在噪声环境下的社区检测和聚类任务中持续优于传统谱投影方法。

Conclusion: RPMA框架通过正则化策略和流形优化的结合，有效提升了谱方法在噪声环境下的鲁棒性，为社区检测和聚类提供了一种更稳定、更准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Spectral methods are among the most widely used techniques for community detection, clustering, and graph learning. Their performance, however, critically depends on the accurate estimation of the underlying spectral subspace and can deteriorate substantially in the presence of noise, outliers, or model perturbations. To address this limitation, we propose a Regularized Projection Matrix Approximation (RPMA) framework for robust estimation of rank-$K$ projection matrices. RPMA extends classical spectral projection by incorporating a regularization term, producing projection estimates that are more robust, sparse, and interpretable. We formulate the proposed model as an optimization problem on the manifold of rank-$K$ projection matrices and exploit its geometric equivalence to the Grassmann manifold. Based on this manifold characterization, we derive the first- and second-order optimality conditions, establish the local stability of the regularized leading eigenspace, and characterize the stability of the critical-point landscape under sufficiently small regularization. To efficiently solve the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a Riemannian gradient projection algorithm with backtracking line search, together with a more efficient Cayley--Sherman--Morrison--Woodbury (Cayley--SMW) gradient method that avoids repeated eigendecompositions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that RPMA substantially improves the recovery accuracy of projection matrices and consistently outperforms conventional spectral projection methods for community detection and clustering under noisy environments.

</details>


### [294] [Counterfactual Explainability Framework With CycleGAN And Counterfactual-Classifier Alignnment Score for Retinal Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21068)
*Kritanu Chattopadhyay,Sayanjit Singha Roy,Soumya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CounterFundus框架，利用CycleGAN生成病理性眼底图像对应的健康反事实图像，通过差异图定位病变区域，并引入CCAS指标量化反事实解释与分类器显著图的空间一致性，从而提供可解释的视网膜疾病检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在眼底疾病检测中表现优异，但其缺乏可解释性，尤其是模型决策未能与临床有意义的视网膜区域关联，阻碍了临床采用。现有事后显著性方法存在局限性，亟需一种能够提供视觉可解释且临床可信的解释框架。

Method: 采用CycleGAN驱动的反事实可解释框架CounterFundus，结合EfficientNet-B5视网膜疾病检测器，实现疾病到正常眼底图像的视觉可解释转换；通过反事实生成的健康图像与原始病理图像的差异图定位病变区域；提出Counterfactual-Classifier Alignment Score (CCAS)，融合Spearman相关系数、二元IoU和指向精度三种指标，量化反事实差异图与分类器显著图的空间一致性。

Result: EigenCAM对齐评估表明，反事实解释在所有CCAS维度上与分类器相关的视网膜证据保持空间一致性；消融实验证实，经CCAS过滤的反事实增强可提升下游眼底图像分类性能。

Conclusion: CounterFundus作为临床可解释的XAI框架，通过反事实生成与空间一致性评估，有效桥接了深度学习模型决策与临床可解释性之间的鸿沟，为视网膜疾病检测提供了临床可信的解释方法。

Abstract: Automated detection of vision impairing retina-based ocular conditions from fundus images is important for early screening, timely referral and reducing dependency on specialist-only assessment, for which neural network-based deep learning (DL) models have been widely utilized. However, explainability of the DL frameworks remains a major bottleneck for clinical adoption, particularly when model decisions are not linked to retinal regions that are clinically meaningful. To address this issue, this study presents CounterFundus, a novel CycleGAN-driven counterfactual explainability framework, integrating EfficientNet-B5-based retinal disease detection with visually interpretable disease-to-normal fundus image translation. For each pathological image, the counterfactual yielded by the CycleGAN generator represents an estimated healthy counterpart and the resultant difference map is utilized to localize disease-associated retinal changes. Unlike conventional post-hoc saliency methods, CounterFundus provides counterfactual explanations through visually plausible disease-to-normal retinal translation. Thereafter, to quantify the spatial agreement between counterfactual difference maps and classifier saliency, the Counterfactual-Classifier Alignment Score (CCAS) is introduced, embedding Spearman correlation, binary IoU and pointing accuracy into a single assessment protocol. To this end, EigenCAM-aligned evaluation demonstrates that the generated counterfactual explanations remain spatially consistent with classifier-relevant retinal evidence across all CCAS dimensions. Along with that, ablation studies further confirm that CCAS-filtered counterfactual augmentation improves the downstream classification performance in fundus images, establishing CounterFundus as a clinically-grounded, explainable artificially intelligence (XAI) framework for retinal disease detection.

</details>


### [295] [From Evaluation to Optimisation: Hierarchy-Aware Training Signals for CWE Prediction in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21069)
*Muntasir Adnan,Manile Srun,Carlos C. N. Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过监督微调、双头分类损失和GRPO三种方式验证了ALPHA层次化惩罚作为训练信号的有效性，发现GRPO能显著降低CWE级漏洞预测的惩罚值，使7B模型达到与4.5倍规模零样本教师相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALPHA基准提出了面向CWE级漏洞预测的分类感知惩罚，理论上可作为训练信号，但缺乏实验验证。本文旨在填补这一空缺，验证层次化惩罚作为训练信号的可行性。

Method: 对比三种训练信号传递机制：1) 监督微调（SFT）；2) 双头分类损失；3) 基于归一化惩罚作为密集奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。在Security Hardening and Adversarial Testing (SVEN)数据集上评估Qwen2.5-Coder-7B模型。

Result: 监督方法在分布偏移下一致退化至低于零样本基线，而GRPO成功提升性能。最优策略在贪婪解码下将累积ALPHA惩罚降低27.9%，在采样解码下降低25.5%（p=0.005，Welch's t检验），达到与4.5倍规模零样本教师模型的统计等价水平。

Conclusion: 层次化惩罚作为训练信号的价值高度依赖于其传递方式的直接性：通过强化学习的密集奖励直接优化惩罚函数有效，而通过监督损失的间接传递则可能导致退化。

Abstract: The original ALPHA benchmark introduced a taxonomy-aware penalty for evaluating CWE-level vulnerability prediction in Python and proposed that the penalty could theoretically also serve as a training signal. This paper provides that validation. We compare three delivery mechanisms: supervised fine-tuning, a dual-head classification loss, and reinforcement learning with a dense reward derived from the normalised penalty. We find that supervised approaches consistently regress below the zero-shot baseline under distribution shift, while GRPO succeeds. Our best policy reduces the cumulative ALPHA penalty of Qwen2.5-Coder-7B on Security Hardening and Adversarial Testing (SVEN) dataset by 27.9% under greedy decoding, and by 25.5% under sampled decoding(p = 0.005, Welch's t-test), reaching statistical parity with its 4.5x larger zero-shot teacher. We conclude that the value of a hierarchical penalty as a training signal depends largely on the directness of its delivery.

</details>


### [296] [Spectral Transformation for Layer-wise Global Rank Discovery in Federated LoRA for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21074)
*Hariharan Ramesh,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecTraL，一种用于联邦学习中Vision Transformer微调的频谱变换方法。它通过堆叠客户端LoRA模块并在低秩潜空间中进行正交Householder变换，避免了全局更新的稠密重构和辅助精炼。利用随机矩阵理论中的Spiked Covariance Model分析非IID噪声，自动发现最优逐层全局秩，无需手动调参。引入填充感知初始化框架，让客户端在不重新合并残差维度到预训练模型的情况下匹配后续轮的秩。实验表明在DomainNet和NICO++上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA聚合策略存在根本性缺陷：独立平均LoRA因子会导致交叉项聚合误差；保留异构客户端秩的拼接方法增加下载开销并引入重新初始化滞后和不稳定收敛；其他方法通过重构稠密权重更新或训练辅助模型来精炼聚合误差，增加了服务器端开销。因此需要一种统一的方法解决这些问题。

Method: SpecTraL方法包含三个关键组件：(1) 将客户端局部LoRA模块堆叠，在低秩潜空间中直接进行正交Householder变换，无需稠密重构；(2) 基于随机矩阵理论中的Spiked Covariance Model，从理论上分离全局共识信号与非IID噪声，自动发现最优逐层全局秩；(3) 填充感知初始化框架，使客户端在不将残差维度重新合并到预训练基础模型的情况下，匹配后续通信轮的全局秩。

Result: 在DomainNet和NICO++数据集上对ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16进行联邦微调实验，SpecTraL实现了更好的准确率-通信权衡，降低了服务器计算开销，并消除了秩选择的超参数搜索。

Conclusion: SpecTraL通过频谱变换和随机矩阵理论，在统一框架中解决了联邦LoRA聚合的多个关键挑战，实现了高效、稳定且无需手动调参的联邦微调。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision Transformers (ViTs) with low-rank adapters (LoRA) promises better communication efficiency under federated setup, yet existing aggregation strategies face fundamental limitations. Independently averaging these LoRA factors is mathematically inconsistent, introducing cross-term aggregation error. In contrast, approaches that preserve heterogeneous client ranks by concatenating local adapters on the server substantially increase download cost and often require merging global LoRA updates into pretrained weights on the clients, causing reinitialization lag and unstable convergence. Other approaches further increase server-side overhead by reconstructing dense weight updates or training auxiliary models to refine aggregation error. In this work, we propose SpecTraL, spectral transformation for layer-wise global rank discovery, that resolves these challenges within a unified design. SpecTraL stacks local LoRA modules from clients and performs orthonormal Householder Transformation of the stacked adapters directly in the low-rank latent space, eliminating dense reconstruction of the global update and any auxiliary refinement on the server. By leveraging the Spiked Covariance Model from Random Matrix Theory, SpecTraL analytically separates the global consensus signal from non-IID noise, discovering optimal layer-wise global ranks without manual hyperparameter tuning. To match local ranks in subsequent rounds, we introduce a padding-aware initialization framework that lets clients incorporate residual LoRA dimensions without re-merging them into the pre-trained base model. Experiments on federated fine-tuning of ViT-B/16 and ViT-L/16 over DomainNet and NICO++ demonstrate improved accuracy-communication trade-offs, reduced server computation, and elimination of hyperparameter search for rank selection. Our code is available at https://github.com/DASS-Lab-Group/SpecTraL

</details>


### [297] [Nipping the Butterfly Effect in the Bud: Self-Output Fine-Tuning for Autoregressive Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21080)
*Yun-Ye Cai,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自回归深度学习天气预报中误差随预报时长增长的现象，发现误差源于输出误差与输入分布偏移之间的反馈循环。提出了一种即插即用的自输出微调（SOFT）策略，通过在第一步校准偏置的输入分布来缓解该问题，实验表明SOFT在长时预报任务上达到了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程天气预报中自回归模型的预测误差随预报时长快速增长，需要从理论和实证层面理解这一误差增长机制，并寻求有效的缓解方法。

Method: 提出Self-Output Fine-Tuning (SOFT)策略，利用模型自身的一步预测结果来校准第一步推理时出现的偏置输入分布，是一种即插即用的方法。

Result: SOFT在长时程天气预报任务上实现了最先进的性能，显著降低了预测误差和分布差异。

Conclusion: SOFT的成功凸显了重新审视深度学习天气预报基础流程的重要性，是大气科学中一项关键的流程进步。

Abstract: Long-horizon weather forecasting is a fundamental challenge in atmospheric science, for which autoregressive Deep Learning Weather Prediction (DLWP) has emerged as the primary paradigm. Although the autoregressive pipeline is highly scalable and flexible, its prediction errors grow rapidly over long forecasting horizons. In this work, we study this error growth phenomenon from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. Our analysis reveals that the growth is driven by a feedback loop between output errors and input distribution shifts. Specifically, the autoregressive process amplifies small initial output errors, which progressively corrupt subsequent input distributions, echoing the butterfly effect in atmospheric science and ultimately deteriorating forecasting accuracy over longer horizons. Furthermore, we show that this distributional shift originates at the earliest stage of inference, with out-of-distribution signatures detectable as early as the first autoregressive step. To mitigate this issue, we propose \textbf{Self-Output Fine-Tuning (SOFT)}, a plug-and-play strategy that leverages the model's own one-step predictions to calibrate the biased input distribution encountered at the first step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, despite its simplicity, SOFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon forecasting tasks and substantially reduces both prediction errors and distributional discrepancy. The success of SOFT highlights the importance of reexamining the fundamental pipeline of deep learning weather prediction, representing a critical pipeline advance for atmospheric science.

</details>


### [298] [CASC: Causal Adversarial Subspace Clustering for Multivariate Spatiotemporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21088)
*Francis Ndikum Nji,Vandana Janeja,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果对抗子空间聚类（CASC）框架，将深度子空间聚类从基于相关性的范式转变为因果-时间机制发现框架，用于高维时空数据中的演化潜在状态识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度子空间聚类方法依赖几何自表达性、假设静态子空间结构，无法捕获复杂时空系统中固有的因果依赖、局部空间交互和长程时间动态。

Method: CASC框架包含：U-Net启发深度对抗聚类架构+堆叠FAConvLSTM层保持时空结构；图注意力Transformer自表达网络联合建模局部空间关系、全局依赖和长程时间交互；提出因果子空间保存损失（对齐自表达系数与潜在因果关系）和动态时间子空间演化损失（捕获非平稳环境下的子空间结构演化与状态转变）。

Result: 将子空间聚类从相关性驱动范式转化为因果-时间机制发现框架，能够发现反映因果过程的演化潜在状态。

Conclusion: CASC通过引入因果学习和时间演化机制，有效克服了传统方法忽略因果依赖和动态子空间结构的局限，为海冰监测、疾病传播分析、神经退行性跟踪等应用提供了更强大的时空数据分析工具。

Abstract: Deep subspace clustering plays a critical role in applications involving multivariate spatiotemporal data, such as sea ice monitoring, disease spread analysis, and tracking neuro-degeneration over time. Despite recent advances, existing methods primarily rely on geometric self-expressiveness, assume static subspace structures, and often fail to capture causal dependencies, local spatial interactions, and long-range temporal dynamics inherent in complex spatiotemporal systems. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Causal Adversarial Subspace Clustering (CASC) framework for discovering evolving latent regimes in high-dimensional spatiotemporal data. CASC integrates a U-Net-inspired deep adversarial clustering architecture with stacked FAConvLSTM layers to preserve spatial and temporal structure while learning robust latent representations. A graph attention transformer-based self-expressive network is introduced to jointly model local spatial relationships, global dependencies, and long-range temporal interactions. Furthermore, we propose two new learning objectives: (1) a Causal Subspace Preservation Loss that aligns self-expression coefficients with latent causal relationships, encouraging clusters to reflect underlying causal processes rather than simple feature similarity, and (2) a Dynamic Temporal Subspace Evolution Loss that captures evolving subspace structures and temporal regime transitions in nonstationary environments. Together, these components transform deep subspace clustering from a correlation-driven paradigm into a causal-temporal regime discovery framework.

</details>


### [299] [Training Large Language Models for Self-Explanation Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21090)
*Yeoktatt Cheah,María Pérez-Ortiz,Noah Y. Siegel,Oana-Maria Camburu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种强化学习方法，直接优化大语言模型自解释的忠实度（faithfulness），即模型生成的推理是否准确反映其内部决策过程。通过将忠实度指标（Phi-CCT）转化为RL训练目标，对Llama3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B进行微调，在分布内和分布外任务上忠实度分数从接近0提升至最高0.691。跨干预泛化存在模型依赖效应，且未观察到reward gaming行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注评估忠实度或使用推理时提示框架来提高自解释的可追踪性，但缺乏一种直接优化模型参数以生成忠实自解释的机制。本文旨在弥合这一空白。

Method: 将现有忠实度指标（Phi-CCT相关性度量）修改为RL训练目标，采用两种干预类型（随机词插入和用户偏见插入），基于每个样本的奖励信号对Llama3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B进行RL微调。

Result: 分布内忠实度从接近0提升至0.664，分布外任务（如StrategyQA）达到0.691。跨干预泛化方面，仅在随机词插入上训练的Llama3.1-8B对用户偏见短语有非零迁移，但反向和Qwen3-8B未复现此结果。未观察到reward gaming行为。

Conclusion: 模型可以通过训练隐式识别影响其决策的因素并予以披露，为减少LLM不忠实推理提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) method to directly optimize the faithfulness of self-explanations - the extent to which a model's generated reasoning accurately reflects its internal decision-making process. While existing work focuses on evaluating faithfulness or using inference-time prompting frameworks to improve an LLM's self-explanation's tractability, these approaches do not provide a mechanism to directly optimize a model's parameters to generate faithful self-explanations. We bridge this gap by modifying existing faithfulness metrics into an RL training objective. We investigate (1) if models can be trained to accurately detect factors that affect their decisions, and (2) whether RL can directly optimize for the disclosure of these factors thereby improving LLM self-explanations' faithfulness. We experiment with two intervention types: random-word insertions and user-bias insertions, using a per-sample reward derived from the Phi-CCT correlation metric. RL fine-tuned Llama3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B show substantial improvements on the Phi-CCT faithfulness metric, with in-distribution scores rising from near-zero to as high as 0.664, and out-of-distribution scores reaching up to 0.691 on held-out tasks such as StrategyQA. Cross-intervention generalization is weaker but more interesting: a priori we would not expect a model trained only on random word insertions to generalize to user-bias phrases, yet Llama3.1-8B shows non-zero transfer in this direction. The reverse direction and Qwen3-8B do not replicate this, indicating model-dependent and setup-dependent effects we cannot yet explain. Lastly we analyze model behavior to rule out reward gaming behaviors that often plague RL training. Ultimately, we show that models can be trained to implicitly identify influential factors and disclose them, offering a scalable path toward reducing unfaithful reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [300] [A Polynomial Architecture-Attribution Co-Design Framework for Exact Aumann-Shapley Attribution in GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21094)
*Bizu Feng,Zhimu Yang,Shuming Wang,Shaode Yu,Yuan Cheng,Xiaojun Qian,Zixin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APEX框架，通过多项式GNN架构（PolyGIN）实现Aumann-Shapley归因路径积分的精确计算，利用Gauss-Legendre求积在有限评估点下达到浮点精度，在特征级和节点级解释上显著提升归因保真度并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN归因方法（如Integrated Gradients）依赖路径积分数值近似，存在求积误差与计算代价之间的权衡，缺乏精确且高效的归因计算方法。

Method: 提出APEX框架，核心是PolyGIN——一种保持有界多元多项式形式的GIN风格图网络。通过设计消息传递、归一化和变换操作使模型输出为多项式函数，证明L层PolyGIN沿归因路径的导数阶数不超过2^L-1，从而可用Gauss-Legendre求积以2^{L-1}个确定性点精确计算Aumann-Shapley路径积分。

Result: PolyGIN在多个合成和真实图基准上保持有竞争力的预测性能；APEX框架在归因保真度上优于对比基线，并大幅减少路径积分所需的评估次数。

Conclusion: 模型-归因协同设计（APEX）能够在不牺牲预测性能的前提下实现GNN解释的精确、高效计算，为可解释GNN提供新范式。

Abstract: We study feature-level and node-level explanations for graph neural networks (GNNs) through the lens of Aumann-Shapley attribution. Path-integral methods such as Integrated Gradients provide an axiomatic formulation of attribution, but their practical use in deep GNNs typically relies on finite-sample numerical approximations to the path integral, requiring a trade-off between quadrature error and computational cost. This paper proposes APEX, a model-attribution co-design framework that makes the attribution integral exactly computable under a polynomial GNN architecture. The key component is PolyGIN, a GIN-style graph network whose message-passing, normalization, and transformation operations preserve a bounded multivariate polynomial form for scalar model scores, such as pre-softmax logits. We show that, for a PolyGIN with $L$ polynomial transformation blocks, the derivative along the attribution path has degree at most $2^L-1$. Therefore, Gauss--Legendre quadrature can evaluate the Aumann--Shapley path integral exactly, up to floating-point precision, with $2^{L-1}$ deterministic evaluation points. The resulting attributions can be computed at the feature level and then aggregated into node-level scores while preserving completeness. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graph benchmarks show that PolyGIN maintains competitive predictive performance, while the complete APEX framework achieves higher attribution fidelity than the compared baselines and substantially reduces the number of evaluations required for path integration.

</details>


### [301] [Smooth Neural Point Processes via B-Splines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21098)
*Michele Bellomo,Riccardo Ramaschi,Alberto Dolara,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于B样条基函数直接参数化条件强度函数（CIF）的神经时间点过程模型，实现了负对数似然的精确计算、架构灵活性、训练并行化以及平滑正则化，在计算效率和预测精度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经TPP方法虽能精确计算NLL，但通常建模补偿器而非直接建模CIF，对网络架构施加约束，且训练时事件贡献需顺序计算，无法并行化，计算开销大。

Method: 将CIF直接参数化为B样条基函数的非负线性组合，其系数由神经网络预测。该形式支持NLL精确计算、任意网络架构、训练并行化，并通过积分二阶平方导数实现CIF平滑正则化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法相比参考神经TPP基线在计算效率和预测精度上均有提升。

Conclusion: B样条基函数直接参数化CIF的策略有效克服了现有神经TPP方法的架构限制和计算瓶颈，是一种兼顾表达力、效率与正则化的可行方案。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) provide a general and flexible framework for modeling sequences of events in continuous time. Neural networks have been successfully employed to model TPPs in a highly expressive and data-driven way. Neural TPPs are typically trained via Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) by minimizing the negative log-likelihood (NLL), which depends on both the conditional intensity function (CIF) and its integral over time, the compensator. Recent neural TPP approaches enable exact evaluation of the NLL without numerical integration. However, these methods typically model the compensator rather than the CIF directly, impose constraints on the neural network architecture, and are computationally expensive during training, as event contributions to the NLL are evaluated sequentially rather than in parallel. In this work, we propose a novel neural TPP model that directly parametrizes the CIF as a non-negative combination of B-spline basis functions, whose coefficients are predicted by a neural network. This formulation enables exact evaluation of the NLL, preserves full flexibility in the neural architecture, allows efficient parallelization during training, and naturally supports CIF smoothness regularization through the integrated squared second derivative. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show improved computational efficiency and predictive accuracy compared to the reference neural TPP baseline.

</details>


### [302] [TOUR: A Trajectory-Level Unlearning Benchmark for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21111)
*Chaofan Pan,Lingfei Ren,Xiangyu Jiang,Yanhua Li,Xuemei Cao,Xiangkun Wang,Hao Yu,Wei Wei,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOUR是一个针对离线强化学习中轨迹级遗忘的基准测试框架，通过多维度攻击审计发现：单一似然分数会高估删除质量，结论依赖于非成员匹配、重训练校准、攻击族和保留效用等多个因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL中轨迹级删除评估困难：低成员分数可能反映轨迹移除、残留记忆或策略崩溃，现有方法缺乏标准化的多维度评估框架。

Method: 提出TOUR基准，包含轨迹级分区、匹配非成员对照、重训练参考、保留效用锚点及多攻击隐私审计；在D4RL和AntMaze上评估多种删除基线（重训练、微调、GA+Refit、TrajDeleter）并使用参考模型、阈值、偏差、等价、动作误差、表征和查询限制等多类攻击。

Result: 1) 常见删除基线呈现环境依赖的隐私-效用权衡；2) 重训练和微调通常比统一GA+Refit提供更强的保留效用参考；3) 单一似然分数会高估删除质量，结论在单分数审计下不稳定。

Conclusion: 离线RL遗忘评估需要多维度（非成员构造、重训练校准、攻击族、保留效用、诊断架构）的综合审计，单一指标不足以可靠评估轨迹级删除效果。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are trained on fixed behavioral trajectories, which makes trajectory-level deletion important when selected data must be removed after training. Evaluating such deletion is difficult because a lower membership score can reflect trajectory removal, residual memorization visible to another attack, or policy collapse that destroys useful behavior. We introduce Trajectory-level memOrization and Unlearning in offline RL (TOUR), a benchmark that combines trajectory-level partitioning, matched non-member controls, retraining references, retained-performance anchors, and multi-attack privacy auditing. Across D4RL locomotion experiments and an exploratory AntMaze extension, TOUR shows that common deletion baselines have environment-dependent privacy-utility behavior. Retraining and fine-tuning often provide stronger retained-utility references than uniform GA+Refit, while TrajDeleter remains a useful comparator but is not uniformly stronger under the same audit. Reference-model, threshold, deviation, equivalence, action-error, representation-based, and query-limited attacks further show that a single likelihood-based membership score can overstate deletion quality. In the evaluated settings, conclusions about offline RL unlearning are therefore not stable under single-score auditing. They depend on matched non-member construction, retraining-relative calibration, attack family, retained utility, and explicit scope for diagnostic architecture or component-level evidence.

</details>


### [303] [GlucoTune: A Unified Framework for Blood Glucose Preprocessing, Forecasting, and Benchmarking in Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21117)
*Davide Marelli,Giorgia Rigamonti,Mirko Paolo Barbato,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoTune是一个用于1型糖尿病血糖时间序列数据预处理和模型评估的标准化框架，通过可配置YAML管道实现可重复实验，并集成了公开数据集、多种预测模型和基准排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血糖时间序列数据预处理缺乏标准化流程和评估协议，导致研究难以复现且跨研究对比不公平；同时数据共享限制（隐私和许可约束）阻碍了预处理后的医学数据集的再分发。

Method: 提出GlucoTune框架：通过可移植YAML配置文件定义可配置预处理管道实现可重复预处理；提供统一接口用于实现、训练和评估血糖预测模型；集成公开数据集的标准包装器；收集现有最优血糖预测和通用时间序列预测方法；构建基准排行榜以支持跨数据集、预处理配置和预测方法的系统对比。

Result: 通过综合实验验证了框架的有效性，并通过用户研究评估了其可用性。

Conclusion: GlucoTune通过标准化从预处理到模型评估的完整实验流程，在不分发敏感预处理数据的前提下实现了可重复实验，为糖尿病管理的数据驱动方法提供了统一的实验平台。

Abstract: Preprocessing blood glucose time-series data is a critical yet often overlooked step in developing data-driven methods for diabetes management, particularly for type 1 diabetes. The lack of standardized preprocessing workflows and evaluation protocols hinders reproducibility and complicates fair comparison across studies. These challenges are further exacerbated by data-sharing restrictions, as privacy and licensing constraints often prevent the redistribution of preprocessed medical datasets. To address these limitations, we present GlucoTune, a comprehensive and extensible framework for reproducible experimentation with blood glucose time-series data. The framework standardizes the entire experimental workflow, from preprocessing to model evaluation, enabling reproducible experiments directly from the original datasets. Reproducible preprocessing is achieved through configurable pipelines defined in portable YAML configuration files, ensuring consistent data handling without distributing sensitive preprocessed data. Beyond preprocessing, GlucoTune provides a unified interface for implementing, training, and evaluating blood glucose prediction models. The framework integrates public datasets through standardized wrappers and provides a curated collection of state-of-the-art blood glucose prediction and general time-series forecasting methods, while remaining readily extensible to additional datasets, preprocessing strategies, and forecasting models. To promote transparent and consistent evaluation, GlucoTune includes a benchmarking leaderboard that reports results across datasets, preprocessing configurations, and forecasting methods, enabling systematic comparison of experimental settings. We demonstrate the effectiveness of GlucoTune through comprehensive experiments and assess its usability in a user study.

</details>


### [304] [Relative Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21120)
*Marc Höftmann,Jan Robine,Stefan Harmeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出相对价值学习（RV）框架，直接学习状态价值差异而非绝对状态价值，通过反对称函数 Δ(s_i, s_j) = V(s_i) - V(s_j) 实现。推导了成对贝尔曼算子及多步目标，并基于此提出无偏策略梯度估计器 R-GAE。在 Atari 基准测试中，RV-PPO 达到与标准 PPO 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习中评论家估计绝对状态价值 V(s)，但控制任务仅依赖价值差异。作者认为直接学习相对价值差异比估计绝对价值更本质且更高效。

Method: 1) 定义反对称函数 Δ(s_i, s_j) 直接建模价值差异；2) 提出成对贝尔曼算子并证明其为 γ-收缩算子，唯一不动点为真实价值差异；3) 推导 1-step、n-step 及 λ-return 目标；4) 从成对差异重构广义优势估计（GAE），得到无偏策略梯度估计器 R-GAE；5) 将 RV 集成到 PPO 中。

Result: 在 Atari 49 个 ALE 游戏上，RV-PPO 与标准 PPO 表现相当，证明相对价值估计可作为绝对评论家的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 相对价值学习框架具备理论完备性（收缩性、不动点唯一性），且实践性能不逊于传统绝对价值方法，为价值函数学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, critics typically estimate absolute state values $V(s)$, estimating how good a particular situation is in isolation. However, it turns out that only differences in value are relevant for control. Motivated by this, we propose Relative Value Learning (RV), a framework that learns value differences directly via an antisymmetric function $Δ(s_i, s_j) = V(s_i) - V(s_j)$. We introduce a pairwise Bellman operator and prove it is a $γ$-contraction with a unique fixed point equal to the true value differences, derive well-posed $1$-step, $n$-step and $λ$-return targets and reconstruct generalized advantage estimation from pairwise differences to obtain an unbiased policy-gradient estimator (R-GAE). Beyond theoretical results, we integrate RV with PPO and achieve competitive performance on the Atari benchmark (49 ALE games) compared to standard PPO, indicating that relative value estimation is an effective alternative to absolute critics.

</details>


### [305] [Demographically-Informed Heat-Mortality Risk Curves via Risk Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21131)
*Alex O. Davies,Eunice Lo,Rui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs)，一种层次化图神经网络编码器，利用细粒度人口普查特征优化DLNM系数向量，在保持可解释风险曲线输出的同时显著提升预测校准能力，在英格兰和威尔士10个区域的2022年热浪评估中表现优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分布式滞后非线性模型（DLNMs）在估算热相关死亡率风险时虽然有效，但忽略了人口统计学和地理背景信息，而这些因素已被证实与热脆弱性密切相关。

Method: 提出Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs)，采用层次化图神经网络编码器，利用细粒度的人口普查特征来优化DLNM的系数向量，在保持可解释风险曲线输出的同时提升预测校准能力。

Result: 在英格兰和威尔士10个区域的两个前所未有的高温年份评估中，RGNN变体在2022年热浪期间保持了较低的点误差和接近标称的不确定性覆盖，而基线模型在此条件下性能崩溃。

Conclusion: RGNNs能够有效整合人口统计和地理上下文信息到热相关死亡率风险建模中，在极端热浪场景下显著优于忽略这些信息的传统DLNM方法。

Abstract: Estimating heat-related mortality risk is a core task in environmental epidemiology, typically addressed with Distributed Lag Non-linear Models (DLNMs); interpretable exposure-response surfaces fitted to temperature-mortality time series. DLNMs are effective but ignore demographic and geographic context, despite well-established relevance to heat vulnerability. We propose Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs), a hierarchical GNN encoder that uses granular census features to optimise DLNM coefficient vectors, preserving interpretable risk curve outputs while substantially improving predictive calibration. Evaluated across 10 regions of England and Wales on two unprecedented heat years, RGNN variants maintain both lower point-errors and near-nominal uncertainty coverage during the 2022 heatwave where baselines collapse.

</details>


### [306] [Agree on the Model, Verify the Inference: GKR Protocols for HND-Based Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21162)
*Xiaolong Liang,Juanjuan Li,Rui Qin,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对外包Transformer推理中的模型替换和不完整执行问题，本文提出GKR-HND协议，通过注册模型、GKR证明和同态-非同态分解，在少量本地验证和委托公开计算下实现高效验证，实验验证了该方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包Transformer推理面临模型替换和不完整执行的安全威胁，而直接重放计算会抵消委托的计算优势，需要在效率和安全性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出GKR-HND协议，采用注册模型协议，利用同态-非同态分解（HND）验证Transformer的多项式核心；保留验证器检查GKR转录本和注册权重公开值，将昂贵的公开计算委托给指定的计算工人，假设保留验证器诚实且证明者与工人不串通。

Result: 在预训练HND模型上的实验验证了证明路径和委托公开计算的有效性，且无需进行密集矩阵重放。

Conclusion: GKR-HND协议能在不牺牲计算优势的前提下，有效防范模型替换和不完整执行攻击，适用于外包Transformer推理场景。

Abstract: Outsourced Transformer inference exposes clients to model substitution and incomplete execution, while direct replay removes the computational benefit of delegation. We present GKR-HND, a registered-model protocol for verifying the polynomial backbone of Homomorphic--Nonhomomorphic Decomposition Transformers. The retained verifier checks the GKR transcript and registered-weight openings, but delegates expensive public evaluations to an assigned computation worker. Assuming an honest retained verifier and prover--worker non-collusion, the verifier accepts only when the worker's signed, request-bound response agrees with the proof claims. Experiments with pretrained HND models validate the proof path and the delegated public computation without dense-matrix replay.

</details>


### [307] [Automated Synthesis and Adversarial Validation of Executable Causal Research Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21173)
*Irena Girshovitz,Dan Zeltzer,Ran Gilad-Bachrach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARA是一个基于AI的流行病学研究助手框架，通过显式编码因果设计原则、生成合成数据（使用结构因果模型）和对抗性验证来暴露自动化研究中的静默失败。实验发现，虽然该方法在数值准确性上未显著优于标准LLM生成，但改变了失败模式——从静默返回错误因果估计转为主动提示协议问题、诊断失败或降级为非因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统虽然能加速实证分析，但容易出现"静默失败"——代码执行成功但依赖无效的因果假设。现有的自动化系统缺乏机制来暴露这类失败，因此需要一种能够显式编码因果假设并检测其有效性的框架。

Method: ARA将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议和可执行代码，通过结构因果模型(SCMs)生成已知真实效应的合成数据集（支持医疗数据受限场景），然后在可控违反识别假设的条件下评估分析结果。评估使用Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark，考察识别策略恢复、因果量估计、变量一致性和代码-协议一致性。

Result: 与标准LLM生成相比，协议构建和对抗性验证未持续提升数值估计的准确性。但改变了失败模式：ARA不再静默返回因果估计，而是经常主动报告协议问题、诊断失败、不完整推理或降级为非因果解释。

Conclusion: 有效性优先的自动化科学系统不应仅通过答案准确性来评估，还应衡量其在因果主张不成立时是否能够主动提示。这为构建更可信的AI驱动科学发现系统提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: While automated research systems promise to accelerate empirical analysis, they are prone to silent failures: instances in which analysis code executes successfully yet relies on invalid causal assumptions. We present the Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Epidemiology Research Assistant (ARA), a framework that makes these failures visible by explicitly encoding causal design principles, study-specific assumptions, and methodological constraints. ARA integrates protocol construction, synthetic data generation, and adversarial validation into a unified pipeline. The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [308] [Filter Learning for Subgraphs: Algebras and Performance Risk Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21263)
*Purui Zhang,Feng Ji,Yanan Zhao,Bihan Wen,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出子图滤波器学习（SFL）框架，利用基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造的子图滤波器代数，在部分观测下逼近全局图滤波器，理论证明了风险界，实验优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图信号处理中谱方法通常假设完整图拓扑可用，但实际中常面临部分观测情况，缺乏系统性的子图滤波器学习框架。

Method: 将SFL形式化为统计学习问题，定义基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造的子图滤波器代数，构建结构化的可控制滤波器类，并在最小二乘损失下推导性能风险界。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提代数模型在SFL任务中一致优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子以及直接数值滤波器学习基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的子图滤波器学习框架能有效在部分观测下逼近全局图滤波器，具有理论保证和实际优势。

Abstract: Graph signal processing tasks that leverage spectral information typically assume access to the complete graph topology, which is often unavailable in practice. We propose a systematic framework for subgraph filter learning (SFL), where subgraph-supported operators approximate ambient graph filters under partial observations. We formulate SFL as a statistical learning problem in which optimal subgraph operators are inherently data-dependent. To address the difficulty of directly estimating such operators, we develop a subgraph filter algebra based on distance-aware Laplacian constructions, defining a structured and controllable class of filters for effective approximation. We further establish performance risk bounds under the least squares loss, quantifying how well the learned operator approximates the restricted ambient mapping. Experiments real-world datasets show that, for SFL tasks, the proposed algebraic models consistently outperform polynomial filters, distribution-agnostic operators, and direct numerical filter learning baselines that attempt to recover the underlying structure from data.

</details>


### [309] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在GRPO（组归一化强化学习）下，对LLM智能体使用密集的逐步预测奖励（预测下一观测）不仅无效，反而会破坏策略——导致“暗室病态”：预测准确率逼近1.0，任务成功率归零，回合长度卡在 horizon。单因素消融定位病根在于GRPO的std归一化：移除后同一奖励从灾难变为基线持平。核心洞察是z-score放大了密集信号在“全失败组”内的方差，而方差随掌握度衰减的信号对这种放大是结构安全的。通过受控的信号传递矩阵实验，发现奖励通道最多中性，而辅助损失通道提升约20点，且打乱标签的安慰剂与真实标签表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在稀疏奖励、长程任务的LLM智能体中，密集的逐步预测奖励（监督下一观测预测）能否作为有效 remedy，以及GRPO框架下该方案的失效机制。

Method: 在ALFWorld环境上使用Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B进行GRPO训练，施加基于势能的预测奖励；通过单因素消融（移除std归一化）、方差特征分析、受控信号传递矩阵（对比奖励通道 vs 辅助损失通道，打乱标签安慰剂）等方法定位病根。

Result: 密集预测奖励在GRPO下导致策略崩溃（成功率0%），移除std归一化后恢复基线水平；辅助损失通道提升约20点，且打乱标签的安慰剂与真实标签效果相当，说明改进不依赖正确标签。

Conclusion: GRPO的z-score归一化在密集信号场景下会放大全失败组内的方差，导致有界奖励变为无界压力，从而破坏策略。方差随掌握度衰减的信号对z-score放大是结构安全的。奖励通道本身最多中性，辅助损失通道才是有效机制。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [310] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在团队多玩家游戏（以《坦克世界》为例）中，利用多任务学习（MTL）训练共享模型，以提升泛化能力并降低训练和推理成本。提出一种结合光栅视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和逐单元状态信息的多模态架构，通过图像编码器和注意力机制处理多任务。实验对比了单任务与多任务训练、混合损失与梯度冲突的加权策略，以及小样本下的预训练/微调效果，并研究了游戏地图间的迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏遥测数据能从同一结构化观测中提供多个相关的监督信号，多任务学习有望利用这些信号提升模型效果。然而，在团队多人游戏场景中，共享模型是否能在降低开销的同时提升泛化能力尚未得到充分验证。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用多模态架构，融合光栅化视觉输入（图像编码器）、全局比赛上下文和逐单元状态信息（注意力机制交互建模），将其适配为通用的多任务学习框架。在《坦克世界》大规模专有数据集上，对比单任务与多任务训练，评估多种损失加权策略以处理混合损失和冲突梯度，并在目标数据有限时测试预训练/微调效果，同时研究跨游戏地图的迁移能力。

Result: 多任务共享模型相比单任务模型在泛化能力上有所提升，同时降低了训练和推理成本。不同的损失加权策略对处理冲突梯度有影响。预训练/微调在目标数据有限时有效。模型具备跨游戏地图的结构化环境迁移能力。

Conclusion: 在团队多人游戏场景下，基于多模态架构的多任务学习是一种有效且高效的建模方案，能够利用多个相关任务提升模型泛化性并节约资源，同时具备一定的环境迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [311] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Int-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在学习场景中的干预决策。通过模拟"教师"监控"学生"解题过程，发现在代码调试、数学和脑筋急转弯三个领域，LLM比人类更频繁、更早地干预，且倾向于提供完整解答而非针对性提示，表明当前LLM助手优化短期成功而非长期学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在学习和思维引导中日益普及，但其干预时机和方式对学习效果有重要影响——过早或过频繁的干预可能阻碍真正的学习和认知参与。然而，当前缺乏系统评估LLM在问题解决过程中如何做出干预决策的方法。

Method: 构建Int-Bench仿真基准，模拟"学生"解题而"教师"监控推理过程并决定是否、何时及如何干预。在代码调试、数学和脑筋急转弯三个领域，评估LLM教师的干预频率、时机及其对即时任务成功和泛化能力的影响，并与人类行为进行比较。

Result: LLM比人类更频繁且更早地干预；与人类相比，LLM倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示；LLM的干预策略更注重短期任务成功而非支持深层推理过程。

Conclusion: 当前LLM助手在辅导场景中优化短期成功而非长期学习效果，其干预策略与人类存在显著差异，需要设计更好的干预机制来促进深层学习和长期成功。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [312] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M$^3$-Gen是一个新型框架，通过生成对抗网络（GAN）结合组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，解决了基因表达数据获取成本高和隐私受限的问题，并在TCGA数据集上验证了其生成数据的真实性和生物学意义，同时通过注意力机制提供了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据的获取受限于高成本和隐私问题，阻碍了其在多模态研究和AI驱动应用中的使用，因此需要一种能够从易获取的数据（组织病理学图像和临床元数据）生成基因表达谱的方法。

Method: 提出M$^3$-Gen框架，利用对比学习从临床变量和组织病理学图像中学习统一的潜在表示，并通过生成对抗网络（GAN）以这些嵌入为条件生成生物上连贯的基因表达谱；采用注意力机制整合多模态信息，实现内在可解释性。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上的实验表明，M$^3$-Gen能够生成真实且功能上有意义的基因表达数据，并且通过注意力机制可以识别组织病理学图像中对特定基因表达生成影响最大的区域。

Conclusion: M$^3$-Gen成功实现了从组织病理学图像和临床元数据到基因表达谱的生成，缓解了基因表达数据的获取瓶颈，同时模型设计本身具有可解释性，有助于理解组织形态与基因表达之间的关联。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [313] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过压缩感知的隐私分析方法，直接测量了LoRA适配器在冻结基座模型上"写入"的信息量（以比特计），发现适配器存储的信息远少于全量微调，且其容量主要取决于参数的位置（如MLP层比注意力层多存储近一倍）而非参数数量。该方法揭示了监督微调会直接复制隐私信息，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习则不会记录秘密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"LoRA适配器是技能而非数据记录"的普遍假设，量化低秩适配器在下游微调中实际"记住"了多少训练数据信息，并探讨其对隐私泄露的影响。

Method: 将基于压缩的显存分析扩展至冻结基座模型设置，直接以比特为单位测量低秩适配器写入模型的信息量。通过控制参数位置（注意力层vs MLP层）、破坏基座模型结构等消融实验分析容量来源，并应用于Qwen2.5的实际微调场景。

Result: (1) 适配器每个可训练参数仅存储几比特信息，远小于全量微调的预算；(2) 容量取决于参数位置而非数量：MLP层比注意力层多存储近一倍；(3) 破坏基座模型结构后容量几乎消失；(4) 隐私泄露随适配器写入的比特数增长；(5) 监督微调会逐字复制秘密，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习不会记录隐私信息。

Conclusion: 将"微调写了什么"从经验观察转化为可直接量化的指标，为设计隐私保护型微调方法提供了理论基础，证实了参数位置的结构性影响远大于参数数量本身。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [314] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验发现，基于显著性的权重选择（SalUn）在表示层面的遗忘效果与随机掩码或无约束更新无显著差异，表明表示级遗忘主要由梯度集中和表示几何结构主导，而非具体的权重选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于梯度显著性的参数子集选择方法在机器遗忘中被广泛采用，但其对表示层面遗忘的实际贡献尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一理解空白。

Method: 采用匹配计算量的实验设计，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上使用ResNet-18，对比基于显著性的掩码、同等稀疏度的随机掩码和无约束更新三种策略，同时保持遗忘目标、优化方案和计算预算一致，并从线性探测、原型恢复和层间CKA等多个表示层面进行评估。

Result: 1）三种掩码策略在表示层面遗忘上统计等价；2）遗忘梯度高度集中在最后几层（CIFAR-10上约92%的平方梯度能量）；3）显著性掩码的类别特异性极低（特异性指数0.09-0.11），不同遗忘类别选择的参数子集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 表示层面的遗忘主要由梯度集中性和表示几何结构决定，而非显著性选择的特定权重。有效的表示级遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的目标函数，而非依赖日益复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [315] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对对齐语言模型在窄域不良数据上进行微调，会出现"突现失调"现象——模型在无关问题上也表现出广泛的不对齐。研究发现，窄域微调会招募微调前已存在的"人格子空间"结构。从冻结的指令微调模型中，通过对比教师强制提取出跨域共享的低秩核心（随机子空间的657倍），该核心的82%位于风格核心之外。仅一步微调就能在广泛失调边际上显著上升，且该子空间的投射（而非随机子空间）可完全防止广泛失调（从27.7%降至0.0%），而注入该子空间则可诱导高达45.4%的失调。权重梯度上的投射无效，后验权重编辑只能抑制而非移除该行为，且被消融的结构会在被清空的子空间内重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么对对齐语言模型进行窄域不良数据的微调会导致模型在无关问题上出现广泛失调（emergent misalignment），即"窄域教训为何会泛化"。

Method: 以Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct为对象，通过对比教师强迫提取跨领域人格子空间；在微调过程中将子空间从残差流中投射出去以阻断失调；将子空间注入未微调模型以诱导失调；对比随机子空间、风格核心、权重编辑等对照实验。

Result: 1) 4个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（随机子空间显著性的657倍），82%在风格核心之外；2) 第一步微调即在广泛失调边际上攀升，并能预测至375步；3) 投射子空间彻底防止广泛失调（27.7%→0.0%），随机子空间无效；4) 注入子空间可诱导高达45.4%的失调；5) 权重梯度投射无效，后验编辑只能抑制不能移除；6) 将不良数据分散到4个领域比权重叠加和多样性匹配能产生更多失调。

Conclusion: 窄域不良微调导致的广泛失调，其机制是激活了模型微调前已存在的、跨域共享的人格子空间结构。通过操作该子空间（投射可预防，注入可诱导）可以控制这一现象，但简单的后验权重编辑不足以彻底消除。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [316] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE（Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding）框架，通过将网络压缩从离散域转移到连续函数的希尔伯特空间，利用秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子建模神经元，统一剪枝和神经元合并为低秩子空间投影，并引入宏块驱逐以覆盖多层结构，实现数据无关且超参数无关的模型压缩与微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂表征，但解构其内部知识仍具挑战。学习与压缩之间存在关联，网络压缩为分析知识提供了有前景的视角。然而，标准压缩启发式方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差影响，需要一种无偏的数学框架来逐步解构训练后的网络权重表征。

Method: 将单个神经元建模为秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，将剪枝和神经元合并统一为低秩子空间投影；在此基础上引入宏块驱逐，将整个残差通路等多层结构纳入统一度量；整个框架无需数据和超参数。

Result: 通过模型压缩和微调的验证性实验，展示了该理论框架的实践潜力。

Conclusion: HOPE提供了一种统一的、数据无关的、超参数无关的数学框架，从连续函数空间角度解构神经网络内部表征，并为网络压缩和后续微调提供了理论驱动的实用方法。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [317] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均值到得分（M2S）方法，通过预测干净token后验均值并利用精确的核相关线性映射转换为得分，解决了得分熵离散扩散（SEDD）中得分比率违反贝叶斯可实现性和桥多面体约束的问题。M2S在均匀和吸收掩码噪声过程中均适用，在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上显著优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和神经CTMC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD中无约束的正得分比率虽然保证非负跳变率，但不确保贝叶斯可实现性——即噪声状态下的得分比率不一定由任何干净token后验通过前向核诱导产生。这导致训练后的SEDD检查点中约四分之一得分向量违反坐标框约束，超过一半虽在框内但与有效后验不兼容，并产生负的预归一化权重，损害生成质量。

Method: 提出M2S方法：先预测干净token的后验均值，再通过一个精确的核相关线性映射将其转换为得分。该构造适用于任何满足温和支持条件的已知逐坐标连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀噪声，M2S将概率单形映射到桥多面体上；对于吸收掩码噪声，所得目标函数精确恢复MD4。

Result: 将原始得分投影到桥多面体上可消除所有负权重，将外部生成PPL从203.6降至175.1。在CIFAR-10上（28.4M参数），M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著改善。在OpenWebText上（170M参数，约262B token），M2S在每一采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和神经CTMC，128步时生成PPL为143.3 vs 183.6。

Conclusion: M2S通过显式强制贝叶斯可实现性约束，有效解决了SEDD中得分不一致的问题，在不同任务和规模上均取得一致改进，为离散扩散模型提供了一种更 principled 的建模方法。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [318] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"JITAI-Twins"的数字孪生方法，通过条件时间序列扩散模型生成目标子群体的数字孪生体，用于在真实部署前模拟和评估在线学习算法，并在HeartSteps系列研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中个性化决策算法设计不当可能导致参与者负担过重或流失，因此在真实部署前需要可靠的模拟用户来评估候选算法，但目前缺乏能够生成逼真、时序一致的数字孪生体的方法。

Method: 构建条件时间序列扩散模型，保证时序一致性（未来行为不影响过去生成）；采用三步更新策略：在大规模观察数据集上预训练、在相关人群的先前干预部署上微调、利用领域专家知识进行推理时校准；在HeartSteps系列（v2到v4）中以每次部署作为目标进行验证。

Result: 所提出的方法比简单模拟器更能复现目标子群体的时间序列结构和参与者间异质性结构，能够模拟目标部署的运行情况。

Conclusion: JITAI-Twins可作为真实部署前测试和优化在线算法设计决策的有效工具，为移动健康干预中安全部署个性化算法提供了前提条件。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [319] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向协作式多任务语义通信（CMT-SemCom）的语义感知任务聚类方法，通过层次密度聚类将语义对齐的任务分组后联合训练，有效缓解负迁移和破坏性协作，相比未聚类多任务和单独训练基线均取得准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CMT-SemCom中，多任务协作既可能是建设性的也可能是破坏性的，取决于任务间的语义关系。现有方法未区分这种关系，导致负迁移和性能下降。

Method: 提出一个序贯多阶段优化框架：第一阶段利用层次密度空间聚类对语义对齐的任务进行聚类；第二阶段仅在聚类组内进行端到端联合训练，避免跨语义差异任务的干扰。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架有效抑制了破坏性协作和负迁移，相比未聚类的多任务学习以及单独任务训练基线均获得了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 语义感知的任务聚类是实现CMT-SemCom中建设性协作的关键，分组联合训练比全局多任务学习更有效。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations.
